# === Wave 2a (wf_5b74a347-4bf) ===

## MG1:gen-terrain

**Summary:** TM-07 is done: params, tables, integer noise, symmetry and phase A for open, urban and coast (incl. moat, marsh, rim) work. Phase A measures 41-99 ms at 192x192 (target 500 ms) across 8 family/slot combinations. `tools/gd test map` is green (68 tests, 1874 checks). The xplat chain over 3 seeds x 3 families is identical on macOS arm64, linux amd64 and linux arm64 (9 hashes). `tools/gd check --strict` is clean for my files. The only remaining errors are in other agents' files: `src/sim/combat/sim_combat_mods.gd:8` (parse error, constant "C") and an unused-parameter warning in `src/sim/sim_path_service.gd:344`. U1, U15, U18 and exact D1X/D2/D4 symmetry pass.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Lattice caching plus a memo over the fold domain: each folded point is evaluated once (`MapGenNoise.Field` returns values bit-identical to the reference `vnoise`/`fbm`; a test compares 3000 samples incl. negative coordinates). This gives the 41-99 ms phase A instead of ~400 ms.
- MapGenParams.density defaults to -1 (family default 50/50/40 from map_gen.json) instead of 50, so the coast default of 40 can be expressed. Range is -1 or 0..100; `effective_density(tables)` and `effective_water_pct(tables)` resolve the family defaults. `to_ints()` = [VERSION, size, family, seed as int32 low bits, slots, water_pct, density, resources, neutrals, biome, start_near_water 0/1].
- `MapGenerator.validate_params` is not mine (TM-08). The U18 logic lives in `MapGenParams.validate_basic(family, size, slots) -> String`, with the spec's messages ("size must be a multiple of 8", family and size errors). MapGenerator.validate_params should delegate to it. `from_config` defaults size to `recommended_size(slots)` when absent.
- MapGenTables: `neutrals` rows stay in FILE order (garrison, substation, field_hospital, tower, depot, harbor), which is NOT the MapData.neutral_ids order. Use `neutral_row_of(id)` for the row and `MapGenTables.neutral_kind_of(id)` for the kind index. `neutral_count(row, fam, size)` evaluates the expression; `neutral_is_per_map(row, fam)` is true for "map1". `load_default()` is a cached singleton, `from_dict(d)` returns null plus push_error on error, `error_of(d)` returns the message without push_error, and `table_hash()` hashes the converted ints and strings. The loader rejects gen_version != MapGenParams.VERSION and rim_w != MapData.RIM_W. Typed accessors: `lay(key)`, `fam_water_pct/fam_density/fam_terraces/fam_moat/fam_marsh/fam_min_route_pct(fam)`, fields `street_w`, `avenue_w`, `pitch_base`, `bay_*`, `canal_*`, and raw `deposit`, `fields`, `biome_base`, `biome_sand_pm`, `progress` (Dictionary of [lo, hi]).
- Painting primitives live in MapGenSymmetry (spec 2.1 lists them under MapGenLayout, but U15 needs them and TM-08 can call them): `paint_disc(k, cx2, cy2, r, out)`, `paint_segment(k, ax2, ay2, bx2, by2, hw2, out)` and `paint_rect2(k, cx2, cy2, hx2, hy2, out)` append cell indices for image k, clipped to the interior `[RIM_W, size-RIM_W)`. They do NOT do paint modes (CLEAR/FORCE/ROAD), the `keep` mask or `route`; TM-08 applies them.
- MapGenSymmetry is an INSTANCE (`MapGenSymmetry.new(slots, size)`). `img_x(k, x2, y2)` and `img_y(k, x2, y2)` replace the spec's `img_pt` (no Vector2i). Also provided: `img_swaps(k)`, `to2(c)`, `cell_of2(p)`, `fold(x2, y2)` (packed; `fold_u`/`fold_v` unpack it), `is_exact()`, `start_x2()`/`start_y2()` (canonical p0) and `start_order()` (image indices sorted by `Fp.atan2`, ties by lowest index). `group` is G_D1X/G_D2/G_D4/G_DN.
- Phase A API: `MapGenTerrain.generate(tt, params, tables, seed_a, progress) -> MapGenTerrain`. It exposes `terrain_base`, `flags_base` (SF_RAMP on ramps, SF_NOBUILD on the rim), `height` (H byte), `moisture` (F2), `plateau`, the thresholds `t_water`, `t_forest`, `t_rock`, `t_plateau`, `t_sand`, and `sym`. `make_map()` returns a fresh un-finalized MapData (via `MapData.create`) with the layers copied and seed_value, family, biome (urban 3), slots, players = slots, gen_version and gen_params set. `water_level_u`, `heights`, `shore_dist`, `roads` and `deco` are left to TM-08's view step. `progress.call(stage, pct)` uses stage ids ST_HEIGHT=0, ST_CLASSIFY=1, ST_WATER=2, ST_CLIFFS=3 and reports pct in 0..64 from the tables' `progress` ranges, monotonic.
- Water percentile deviation (spec 5.12.4): `t_water` is computed on the UNCLAMPED coast height (hu = H0 - 3*edge, bias 128 in a 384-bin histogram) and only over the playable interior. The literal clamp put about 30 % of a coast map into bin 0, so DEEP (`H <= t_water-10`) was unreachable and the whole sea was shallow. Counting the 6-cell rim (23 % of a 96x96 map) would also have swallowed the 22 % water share. The stored `height` layer is still clamped 0..255. `t_water` is on the unclamped scale, -1000 when water_pct is 0.
- Spec ambiguities resolved: coast `d_norm` uses Chebyshev distance for exact groups (2/4/8 slots) and `isqrt(x2^2+y2^2)` for rotation groups. The urban alley picks column or row from `(hb>>5)&1` and sits at `street_w + (pitch-street_w)/2`. Percentile semantics: `top_threshold` returns the smallest t with count(v > t) <= target; plateau uses F3 >= t_plateau as written. Urban skips F3/F4/F5/F6 (not needed). Marsh is applied to GRASS/DIRT only. Moat and marsh use `layout.moat_*` and `marsh_*` from the tables.

**Tests:** `tools/gd test map -q`: 68 passed, 0 failed, 1874 checks (17 tests in test_map_gen.gd). Phase-A timing at 192x192, measured in the test (note line): fam0/2 slots 77 ms, fam0/4 53, fam0/8 43, fam0/6 60, fam1/2 56, fam2/2 99, fam2/3 89, fam2/8 59. `python3 tools/py/xplat_determinism.py res://tests/scenarios/xplat_map_gen.gd`: "IDENTICAL hash chains on 3 platforms (9 hashes)". `tools/gd check --strict`: my files report nothing; the remaining errors are in `sim_combat_mods.gd` and `sim_path_service.gd` (other agents).

**Known gaps:**
- Layout (phase B), views, validation, MapGenerator/MapGenJob and the template are TM-08.
- The phase-A hash goldens differ from any spec value (none were given); the tests pin behaviour, not hashes.
- DN (3, 6 slots) symmetry is checked only as a class-mismatch percentage (<= 25 %, sampled inside the inscribed circle); the measured value is not reported.
- No per-family statistics test over 200 seeds (10.4 is nightly, TM-08 territory).

**Cross-module requests:**


## MV1:movement-core

**Summary:** TM-10 and TM-11 are implemented. That covers SimMoveConfig, SimCompMove (kernel stub filled), SimMoveProfiles, SimPathService, SimSteering, SimSeparation, SimMovementSystem (stage 6) and the SimMovement facade. Ground, naval and submerged units move end to end: path service, steering, separation, terrain rule, medium switching, stuck escalation, glide, sidestep, request_layer, pause/resume, approach/follow. `tools/gd test move` gives 39 tests passing (341 checks), on macOS and in the amd64 Linux container. The xplat hash chain (tests/scenarios/xplat_move.gd) is identical on macOS arm64, linux amd64 and linux arm64. Two things from the wider suite are not mine: `sim_test_kit.gd` and `test_sim_types.gd` had to change, and `econ/test_placement.gd::test_units` now fails because I added `eject_units_from_rect` (see CROSS_MODULE_REQUESTS). Benchmark: 256x256 map, 1200 units, 300 moving, 8 players, path bursts included. Stage 6 averages 4.4 ms/tick (best of 3 on a loaded dev Mac), worst about 8 ms; the whole step averages about 4.9 ms. That is inside 1.5x the spec's 3.9 ms average. S1 (50-cell run) arrives at tick 505, exactly the spec's independent simulation.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- NAMES: the component is SimCompMove (file comp/sim_comp_move.gd), not SimMoveComp. The removal hook is the kernel's on_remove(world, e, reason), not on_removed. SimMovementSystem.of(world) returns world.movement. The system keeps the spec's public members path (SimPathService), sep (SimSeparation) and prof (SimMoveProfiles), and sets `ready` once init_world has run. SimMoveProfiles.build(data, tt = null) accepts a null tt.
- SimCompMove adds two hashed fields beyond spec 4.4: pack_t (last request_pack tick, rate limit 20 ticks) and side_t (last accepted sidestep tick, rate limit 20 ticks). GK_FACE stores the target angle in goal_x.
- EVENTS: sim_core has no STATE event code, so movement emits its own block-100 codes. EV_MEDIUM_CHANGED=101 is (a=unit id, b=1 entered water / 0 left, x/y in the header), for amphibious units only, at least 6 ticks apart. EV_STUCK=108 is (a=id, b=stuck count). EV_LAYER_CHANGED=109 is (a=id, b=new SimEntity.Layer, 2 = surfaced, 3 = submerged), emitted when request_layer completes. EV_MOVE_FAILED=100 is declared but never emitted; ORDER_FAILED comes from the order dispatcher.
- movement_defaults (decel_ticks_pct_of_accel 70, reverse_speed_pct 50) are constants in SimMoveConfig, because GameData does not expose them. The movement.json turn_mode_override does not exist, so turn modes come from the move class only.
- SEP_MAX_NEIGHBOURS is 16, not 12. With 12, a 30-unit pile left pairs permanently on top of each other, because units behind the 12th neighbour were never seen.
- SEPARATION differs from spec 5.4.4 in three ways. (1) It resolves penetration against the phase-A predicted positions (tick-start position plus the unit's own drive). Otherwise a wall-type neighbour (an enemy or an immobile unit) could be walked through, which broke S6. (2) The summed push is damped by 2/(overlaps+1) when a unit has more than one overlap; undamped, crowds threw units 40 cells away. (3) The coincident-pair direction is made opposite by id order; the spec's XOR formula gives both units the same direction.
- STEERING: TM_INSTANT follows the pivot heading-factor curve; the spec's f=256 lets a turn-rate-limited unit circle a close goal forever. Reverse needs OPT_REVERSE_OK and is allowed on the last leg (wp+1 >= n) rather than only after the path is consumed. An ARC unit whose goal is within 2 stop radii and more than 90 degrees off its heading counts as arrived (anti-orbit). The stuck nudge is skipped when neither lateral cell is passable, so a 1-cell lane stays blocked (S6). Waypoint advance is limited to 2 per tick.
- STUCK adds a crowd rule: on the final leg within 6 cells of the goal, 4 strides without closing in count as arrived (RS_OK). The spec's earlier arrive-near clause (blocked_t >= 8 with an idle friendly blocker) is not implemented. blocked_by is found by a spatial query just ahead of the unit (nearest unit, ties lowest id). Stuck count 8 gives MS_NO_PATH with RS_STUCK, goal cleared.
- PATH SERVICE: it keeps one avoid array per request slot rather than a shared avoid_pool. Request flags RQ_REPATH (a failure leaves the unit's state alone, so a failed stuck or validity repath does not fail the order) and RQ_ALT (alternative goal, result partial). The cache key adds goal_r; keys are 64-bit, held in a PackedInt64Array ring. An unreachable goal gets the ring-scan alternative goal, delivered with MF_PATH_PARTIAL and finishing in MS_ARRIVED with RS_PARTIAL. A partial path consumed while the goal is still in the same region triggers a repath, at most once per 20 ticks. A snapped POINT goal rewrites goal_x/y to the snapped cell's centre. Charged units are clamped to the tick budget (last_units <= last_budget). Diagnostics: last_units, last_budget, stat_searches, stat_hits, stat_delivered, stat_failed.
- FACADE: approach_entity stops when dist - target.radius <= range, not a footprint rect. follow ignores min_d. go_near uses path goal radius (range*5/8)>>10 cells. find_free_cell_near(world, cell, layer, max_radius) uses profile NP_TRACKED/NP_NAVAL/NP_SUB with clearance 1, treats a cell as free when it has no structure and no unit centre, and returns the lowest index of the first ring. eject_units_from_rect takes cell coordinates, moves matching units via set_pos with speed 0 and goal cleared, and has no MS_EVICT fallback. The air_* primitives and altitude() exist with the spec signatures but are inert until MV2 (air_at_goal returns false). This task only has these two exit helpers; economy's placement code detects eject_units_from_rect on the facade.
- init_world registers NO order handlers (MV2 does; a NOTE(MV2) marks the spot). Aircraft get a SimCompMove but the stage loops skip them.

**Tests:** ["tools/gd check --strict src tests -> 0 script errors, 0 lint violations; the only warnings are in other agents' files (combat/sim_targeting.gd, sim_production_system.gd, tests/bench/dbg_mapgen.gd, tests/sim/test_sim_orders.gd).", "tools/gd test move -> 72 files, 39 tests, 39 passed, 0 failed (341 checks): test_move_units (U8, U16, U19, steering helpers), test_move_path (LOS same-tick delivery, cancel/replace, group cache and attach, alternative goal, budget accounting and resumable searches, priority order, state ints), test_move_scenarios (S1, S2, S3, S5, S6, S7, S8, S9, S15/S16, S17, S20), test_move_facade (glide, stop, turn_to, pause/resume/near, approach/follow, sidestep, lifecycle hooks, dirty-graph deferral, find_free_cell/eject, reverse/precise/speed cap, 30-unit pile settles, determinism double run via SimTestKit.double_run).", "Measured: S1 arrives at tick 505 with the spd_q4 ramp 272..1632 at tick 6; S2 forest ratios 0.705 foot / 0.549 tracked; S3 ford ratios 0.701 / 0.648 / 0.502; S6 RS_STUCK 88 ticks after contact; S8 deep-water speed 70.93 vs 71.40 expected.", "tools/gd linux --arch amd64 test move -q -> 39 passed, 0 failed.", "python3 tools/py/xplat_determinism.py r

**Known gaps:**
- Not built (MV2): SimFormation, order handlers (T_MOVE, T_PATROL, T_FOLLOW, T_FACE, T_LAND, T_LOAD, T_UNLOAD, T_GARRISON), air movement and air_* bodies, cargo approaches, MS_EVICT and the exit/glide helpers beyond find_free_cell_near and eject_units_from_rect. Without formations, units ordered to one point pile up. The pile settles (tested) but sits at the 7/8 overlap tolerance, and pairs beyond the 16-neighbour cap can stay overlapped.
- Lanes narrower than two unit discs are passable by a determined mover, because the terrain rule only tests the unit's centre cell (spec D-4). The nudge and the wall-type separation stop this in a 1-cell lane but not in general.
- Adapter method names are assumed, as in the spec. Abilities must provide speed_units(e)->int, is_immobile(e)->bool, is_turn_locked(e)->bool and request_pack(e); combat provides suppression_speed_bp(e)->int. Each is optional and falls back if missing.
- The earlier arrive-near clause (blocked_t >= 8 with an idle friendly blocker) and movement.json turn_mode_override are not implemented. SimInvariants has no movement-specific checks.
- The bench is one dev-machine run on a loaded Mac, not the unloaded M-class of spec section 9. Targets not re-verified: peak total 8.8 ms and the weak-laptop column.

**Cross-module requests:**
- ECON: tests/econ/test_placement.gd::test_units now fails at its line 92 ('without an eject service a friendly unit blocks too'). Cause: SimMovement.eject_units_from_rect now exists, so SimPlacement's has_method detection finds it. Update that expectation, or make the no-service case use a world whose facade lacks the method. I left the econ test untouched.
- KERNEL (SC-12 owner): I made two small edits in kernel-owned test files. (1) tests/support/sim_test_kit.gd make_world now disables SimMovementSystem when the speed-300 MoveHandler stand-in is installed (movers=true), unless the caller passes real_movement=true; without this the S-CORE-1 goldens would change the moment a real movement system exists. (2) tests/sim/test_sim_types.gd: test_components_instantiate exempts SimCompMove like the econ and combat components, and test_entity_hash_details reads the component mask at index b0.size()-1.
- KERNEL: tests/sim/test_sim_determinism.gd::test_golden_scenario fails in the whole suite (checkpoint chain and final checksum differ, event digest and positions pass). Movement is disabled in that scenario, so this is not from my change; likely other domains' newly hashed component or system state. The goldens need regenerating. sim/test_sim_commands.gd currently errors because economy registers order types 30/31, which the kit's stand-in handlers also register; also not mine.
- DATA: expose movement_defaults (decel_ticks_pct_of_accel, reverse_speed_pct, stuck_repath_ticks) on GameData or DefBodyTable, and add a movement.json loader for turn_mode_override if wanted; movement hard-codes 70 / 50 / 40 until then.
- ABILITIES / COMBAT: provide speed_units(e), is_immobile(e), is_turn_locked(e), request_pack(e) on the abilities system and suppression_speed_bp(e) on combat (see known_gaps). Combat may already call SimMovement.move_to/move_to_range/stop/pause/resume/turn_to/is_moving/velocity; the air_* calls are inert until MV2. Combat should also read F_MOVING and SimMovement.velocity, since movement writes bits 16-19 of the entity flags each tick.
- MV2: register the order handlers in SimMovementSystem.init_world (marked NOTE(MV2)); build them on SimMovement.go_to/go_near/approach_entity/follow/stop/turn_to/state/result/ack; read state after SimOrder tick and ack terminal states (MS_ARRIVED, MS_NO_PATH). The path service already exposes request(e, goal_cell, goal_r, prio, flags, avoid) and counters (stat_searches, stat_hits) for the S4 group-search check; SimCompMove already has fslot_x/fslot_y/fslot_tick and speed_cap_q4 for formations and speed match.

## MG2:gen-layout

**Summary:** TM-08 (MG2) is done and green. Generator layout (starts, bays, gates, trunk roads, fields, neutrals, beaches, boulders), view layers, validation V1-V12, the repair/regenerate orchestration, the safe template and the time-sliceable job are all in place. Statistical run: 600 maps (20 cells x 30 seeds) gave 599 first-attempt/level-0 successes (99.8 %) and 600 valid, none needing the template. Generation at 192x192 incl. nav measured 250-330 ms (open/coast 8 slots) and 410-470 ms (urban 8 slots); 256x256 coast 8 slots about 500 ms, all on a loaded machine. Cross-platform: xplat_map_gen_full.gd gives identical hash chains on macOS / linux-amd64 / linux-arm64 (11 hashes). Job (threaded, and unthreaded in 1 us slices) is byte-identical to the direct call.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- PUBLIC API as specified: MapGenerator.validate_params/generate/generate_params/generate_report, MapGenJob.begin/step/progress_pct/result/cancel, MapGenValidate.validate/metrics, MapGenTemplate.make(params). Extras: MapGenerator.Runner (inner class, one atomic stage per step(); used by generate and MapGenJob), MapGenerator.make_runner(cfg, tables, progress), MapGenerator.sanitize(params) (a bad lobby config is clamped: family 0..2, slots to {2,3,4,6,8}, size rounded up to a multiple of 8 within [max(96,min_size),256]; generate never returns null except when terrain.json / map_gen.json cannot load). MapGenTemplate.make(params, tt=null, tables=null). MapGenValidate.validate/metrics/analyze take an optional trailing MapGenTables (default MapGenTables.load_default()); analyze(map, full, tables) returns {fails, metrics}; dock_sites(map, cx, cy, first_only) is public.
- PROGRESS: callback is progress.call(stage, pct) on the calling thread, pct monotonic 0..100 (a retry never lowers it). Stage ids: ST_HEIGHT 0, ST_CLASSIFY 1, ST_WATER 2, ST_CLIFFS 3 (MG1) and ST_LAYOUT 4, ST_FIELDS 5, ST_NEUTRALS 6, ST_FINALIZE 7, ST_VALIDATE 8, ST_DONE 9, declared in MapGenLayout and mirrored as MapGenerator.ST_*. Ranges: phase A 0-64, layout 64-70, finalize 70-92, validate then done 100.
- metrics() has one extra key: 'exit_ring_covered' (PackedInt32Array). Keys as specified: exits_per_start, credits_reachable_per_start, water_required (always false: everything is evaluated on the land-only guarantee set), route_pct, fairness_spread_permille, dock_sites (empty unless coast + start_near_water), neutrals (count).
- VALIDATION semantics that differ from the spec text: V1 checks only the vehicle-c2 component (the foot set contains it, so foot connectivity follows). V2 ring at Chebyshev 20: covered% is measured against the DRY ring cells inside the playable interior (rim and open sea are neither exit nor obstacle); pass = arcs>=2 or covered>=50% of those cells. V4 counts cells of TF_BUILD terrain (not SF_NOBUILD-filtered) within radius 14, as the spec text says, >= 450. V7 uses NP_WHEELED/SZ_2 fine A* (force_mode 1) start->field centre per (kind, rank) across slots, detour ratio = path_cost*1000/(10*max+4*min), pass = spread <= 150 permille. V10: a contested field (owner_start -1) counts for every start within 125 % of its nearest-start distance. V11 uses an own berth test (all berth cells DEEP, one water body of >= 100 cells) instead of MapBuildRules.check_berth, see CROSS_MODULE_REQUESTS. V6 minimum route pct comes from the tables (open 45, coast 35, urban 30).
- LAYOUT details: fields rows are [id (dense 1-based, creation order CONTESTED, START x2, NATURAL, NATURAL_RICH, FURTHER x2, images in ascending image index), centre cell, radius 3, cells, total, kind, orbit = image index, hint_cell (6 cells toward the owning start; nearest start for contested), klass (0 standard / 1 rich), owner_start = start index in published order, -1 for CONTESTED]. Deposit cells: the K cells of the 7x7 window with the smallest key, keys computed in the CANONICAL frame (offset back-transformed through the inverse image), so exact groups are bit mirrors and every image gets exactly K cells. A candidate field site is rejected when it only partly duplicates an earlier field of its kind (or, for non-contested kinds, coincides with its own images) so that every slot owns the same set; a FURTHER sub-field can therefore be missing for all slots on D4 maps (reachable credits 115 200 instead of 129 600). Site validity: only the centre must be dry (spec) but the disc may not touch bay water; disc is FORCE GRASS which turns water in it into land. Neutral spawns/lots: roads are not painted inside the start disc (SF_START radius) so the HQ yard stays buildable, and no trunk roads / polylines on urban maps; roads = 2 polylines per image (start->centre, start->nearest neighbour) of 3 points [x0,y0,...] in 1/16 cell = (doubled + size)*8.
- BAYS: outward axis chosen by |x2| >= |y2| (ties horizontal) per image; pocket 9 along x 7 across, centre 10 cells from the start, canal (7 deep + 2-cell shallow banks) from the pocket's far edge to the map edge; rectangles are painted in the image frame (image 0 transform of the already-transformed centre) so DN images keep dock-friendly axes. Bay cells are a 'protect' mask that fields and lots never overwrite.
- NEUTRALS: footprints and the harbor berth are read from game/data/balance/neutral_structures.json by MapGenLayout.neutral_dims()/harbor_berth() (static immutable cache, loaded on the constructing thread) because MapGenTables carries no footprints. Placement: rows are processed with civilian garrisons LAST (they would starve the single-item rows); per image the spec's ring sequence is walked (angle = base + j*700 + retry*341 alternating sign, 64 retries), then the ring widened by 8, then an exhaustive nearest-first scan (24 cells for the per-map salvage depot); acceptance is per image (spec: all images or nothing), which keeps counts equal for rotation groups. Lots are never rotated/swapped (w,h stay the def footprint). A row stops at the first item that finds no room. Urban garrison blocks reach about 50-60 % of the nominal 6+size/64 per slot; harbor terminals can be missing on tiny 3-slot coast maps (aggregate >= 75 % is asserted).
- VIEW LAYERS: heights = 2*mean of adjacent cell heights (plateau cells use the terrace height), corners within RIM_W ramp linearly to -64 at the edge; water_level_u = max(1, 2*t_water+1) on maps with water, 0 on dry maps (phase A's t_water is on the unclamped scale); deco = hash byte on forest/rough/cliff/marsh cells filled AFTER finalize (needs the kind layer), deco_seed = mix32(seed, 0xDEC0, 7); moisture from phase A; shore_dist from MapData.finalize. Scenery boulders (SF_BLOCK) are 1-3 cells, 3 (open) / 2 (coast) per map replicated per image, only where the 3x3 neighbourhood is unpainted grass/dirt; none on urban.
- TEMPLATE: dry GRASS/DIRT (arid biome: all DIRT) scatter on the folded hash, rim, layout at level 2 (17-cell discs, 9-wide gates), one 2-radius ROCK cluster per image at 0.35 R painted only on unpainted cells, no neutrals, no bays; its gen_params carry start_near_water = 0 (so V11 is not requested for a template).
- GENERATOR REPAIR LOOP as specified (MAX_ATTEMPTS from the tables = 2, levels 0..2, seed_a = seed for attempt 0 else mix32(seed, attempt, 0x51ED)); the report's 'failures' entries are strings 'attempt A level L: <fail names>'. layout failure text: 'field kind K/S not placeable' (START or NATURAL missing).
- MapGenLayout.lot_reject (PackedInt32Array, 6 counters) is telemetry only. A bench script tests/bench/bench_mapgen.gd (args: family slots size seeds [snw]) prints per-seed attempts/level/failures/ms.
- Test knobs: MERIDIAN_MAPGEN_SEEDS (env, default 2) sets seeds per cell of the matrix test; the reduced-scale run used 30 (600 maps).

**Tests:** ["tools/gd check --strict src/map tests/map tests/scenarios tests/bench -> lint 0 violations, 0 script errors, 0 warnings, RESULT OK.", "tools/gd test map -q -> 82 files, 80 tests, 80 passed, 0 failed, 0 errors (4383 checks) in 43 s. (The runner notes an unrelated broken file res://tests/scratch/test_dbg.gd from another agent.)", "tools/gd test test_map_gen_layout.gd -> 12 tests / 2537 checks pass: test_matrix_guarantees (4 families incl. coast+bay x 5 slot counts x seeds: validates, starts connected for FOOT/WHEELED/TRACKED/AMPH via nav regions, exits, fairness spread <= 150 permille, reachable credits >= 75000, dock site per start, deposits and lots reachable, sum deposit_max == sum fields.total, owned totals equal across slots, dry families have no water/beach/ford/marsh cells, neutral counts (>= 75 % aggregate), view layers), test_symmetry_of_layouts (exact groups <= 1 % mismatch, rotation groups <= 25 %), test_determinism_and_job_equality (double run byte identical incl. visual_hash; threaded job, 1-us-sliced unthreaded job == direct call; progress monotonic; cancel -> null; to_bytes/from_bytes round trip), test_sim_state_determinism (occupy order independence, nav validate), 

**Known gaps:**
- The spec's 200 seeds per cell and the D4 golden matrix ({4 families} x {2,3,4,6,8} x {min,128,192} x seeds {1,7,12345}) were not run; 30 seeds per cell and 9 pinned goldens instead.
- First-attempt/level-0 success is 99.8 % (599/600), just above the spec bar of 99 %; the one repaired map needed level 2 (V6 route fraction on a coast 3-slot 192^2 map, seed 40595).
- Neutral counts are below the nominal on some maps: urban garrison blocks reach roughly half of 6+size/64 per slot, the field hospital and harbor terminal can be missing on the smallest coast maps (harbor terminal absent on one of 30 seeds at 3 slots / 112^2).
- On D4 (8-slot) coast maps one of the two FURTHER fields per slot is not placeable (self-symmetric anchor); reachable credits are 115 200 per slot there (spec mentions the same figure for one batch).
- MapGenJob threaded mode calls Checksum.digest32_bytes (finalize/content_hash) which uses a shared static HashingContext; generation must not overlap other Checksum users on another thread (loading screen only).
- Urban layout spends up to ~330 ms in the exhaustive neutral scan fallback on 8-slot 192^2 maps (total still < 0.5 s).
- V7 fairness runs full fine A* for every (kind, rank, slot); it is cheap (about 10-20 ms per map) so no exact-group shortcut was taken.
- The generated map still has no footprints / neutral defs registered (map.set_footprint / set_neutral_def), as before: match setup must do that (MAPA request).

**Cross-module requests:**
- MAP owner (MAPA, map_build_rules.gd): MapBuildRules.check_berth tests the naval component with nav.region_size(NP_NAVAL, SZ_2, berth_cells[0]). The first berth cell always touches the dock footprint (land), so its 3x3 water clearance is 1 and region_size returns 0 -> PR_WATER_EXIT for EVERY real dock berth (found via V11: 0 valid docks on maps that visibly have one). Please test the best/any berth cell (max region_size) or fall back to map.water_size of the body (>= NAVAL_COMPONENT_MIN). MapGenValidate works around it with its own berth test (all cells DEEP, one water body >= 100 cells).
- Data owner (DB/DA): MapGenTables (map_gen.json) has no neutral footprints although terrain_movement 5.12.8 says they arrive through MapGenTables. MapGenLayout currently reads footprint w/h and the harbor 'berth' rectangle from neutral_structures.json (the authored data file) via a static cache; expose them through GameData/DefNeutral-derived tables if a single pipeline is required, and add neutral_structures.json to the data hash inputs of the generator (map_hash already changes with them because lots record w/h).
- App / match setup: after MapGenerator.generate or MapGenJob.result(), register footprints and neutral defs on the map (map.set_footprint, map.set_neutral_def for each id of map.neutral_ids = MapData.NEUTRAL_IDS_DEFAULT order), then clone_fresh() per world. The map handed back is finalized with all nav graphs prepared; view layers (heights, roads, deco, moisture, shore_dist, water_level_u) are filled.
- Kernel/economy: fields rows use owner_start = -1 for CONTESTED fields and orbit = image index; spawns rows are [index, cell, facing, orbit, index & 1, 0]; neutral rows [kind, top-left cell, w, h, variant, 0, orbit, 0] with kind = index into MapData.NEUTRAL_IDS_DEFAULT (garrison 0, substation 1, salvage depot 2, field hospital 3, observation tower 4, harbor terminal 5).
- Lobby / net: use MapGenerator.validate_params(family, size, layout_players) before start; MapGenJob.begin(cfg, null, true) for the loading screen (progress_pct 0..100, step(budget_us) polls, result() valid when step returned true).

## MV2:orders+air+exits

**Summary:** MV2 is done for TM-12, TM-13 and TM-15. MOVE, STOP, SCATTER, PATROL and FOLLOW run end to end through the command executor. Each unit gets a T_MOVE, T_PATROL or T_FOLLOW handler (plus T_FACE and T_LAND), and a same-tick group gets formation slots. Aircraft fly through SimAirMove (fly_to, orbit, hover, face, takeoff, land_at) after the ground phases in stage 6. Exit search, eject and the MS_EVICT drive-out are in SimExitMove. The handlers are registered in SimMovementSystem.init_world. `tools/gd check --strict` reports 0 script errors and 0 lint violations, with one warning in someone else's file, `tests/sim/test_sim_orders.gd`. `tools/gd test move` passes 67/67. The new scenario's hash chain is identical on macOS arm64, linux amd64 and linux arm64. Two other tests fail in the full suite, neither caused by these changes: `test_golden_scenario` and `test_units` (details under known gaps).

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Handlers: one class SimOrderMove(order_type) serves T_MOVE(16), T_PATROL(17), T_FOLLOW(18) and T_FACE(19). It uses the sim_core hook names can_issue / on_begin / on_update / on_end / on_target_lost, not the spec's begin / tick / cancel. T_LAND(20) is SimOrderAir. Orders are fresh per actor, so the group is recovered inside on_begin.
- Order numbers are sim_core's (T_MOVE 16 ... T_LAND 20). The spec's 64-66 for the cargo orders are not used because TM-14 is out of scope. MOVE / STOP / SCATTER / PATROL / FOLLOW stay the built-in executors of SimCommandSystem, and I registered no executor.
- Formation sort key packs (u, v, id) as (2^18 - u) << 43 | (v + 2^18) << 24 | id, with u and v clamped to +-(2^18 - 1). The spec's shifts (44 / 24, bias 2^20) overlap and overflow int64. The resulting order is the intended (-u, v, id) order and reproduces the worked example exactly.
- SimFormation.assign flags: bit 1 (F_SPEED_MATCH) caps every member's speed_cap_q4 at min(speed_base * 16). It is set from OF_SPEED_MATCH (MOVE flags bit1). SimFormation.SPEED_MATCH_DEFAULT is false because movement.json does not exist. The cap is reset to 0 in on_end of T_MOVE and at the start of an ungrouped leg.
- Formation slots share one path. SimCompMove gains route_x / route_y (hashed, default -1) as the path target when it differs from the goal. The facade gains SimMovement.go_to_slot(world, e, slot_x, slot_y, route_x, route_y, opts). The path is searched to the group target cell, goal_x/y stays the slot, and _steer cuts straight to the slot once it is within 6144 units and in line of sight. This gives S4 exactly 3 searches and 27 hits.
- SimPathService._deliver no longer replaces goal_x/y with the path's goal cell when route_x >= 0. This is a one-line edit in an MV1 file.
- Orbit heading sign: the spec formula `des = tangent - dir*corr` tilts the wrong way; its own text says 'outside the circle => aim inward'. I use des = radial + dir * (1024 + corr).
- Air state uses SimCompMove fields only (SimCompAir belongs to combat): air_mode, alt, alt_goal, orbit_x/y/r/dir, land_x/y/heading, air_phase, and g_t/g_n/g_x0/g_y0/g_x1 as take-off and landing counters. air_phase bits: 1 = at_goal latched (also set at touchdown), 2 = landing final leg, 4 = take-off roll done.
- Air events: the spec's STATE(ST_takeoff / ST_landed) is replaced by sim_core's EV_AIR_TAKEOFF = 102 and EV_AIR_LANDED = 103, declared as SimAirMove.EV_AIR_TAKEOFF / EV_AIR_LANDED (not in SimMoveConfig). Payload is a = unit id, with x/y in the header.
- Aircraft that spawn in the air: they spawn with the home layer AIR and air_mode AM_PARKED. air_fly_to / orbit / hover / face / land_at start such an aircraft flying at once, at cruise altitude and, for fixed-wing, at 60% speed. A parked aircraft on layer GROUND ignores those calls except air_takeoff.
- air_takeoff(ticks > 0) flips the layer at the ticks-th flight step after the call. air_land_at(ticks > 0) switches to AM_LANDING when the aircraft reaches the approach point (fixed-wing) or hovers over the pad (rotor), then touches down exactly `ticks` steps later. With ticks = 0, a fixed-wing lands physically (35% speed at touchdown) and a rotor descends 64 units per tick.
- T_MOVE on an airborne aircraft does air_fly_to and is DONE when at_goal latches. On a grounded aircraft it fails with Err.NOT_ALLOWED. STOP or cancel on a cruising or approaching aircraft calls hover (a fixed-wing circles). Combat may replace_handler if it wants missions.
- Failures: path failure gives Err.BLOCKED, unpacking timeout or no move gives Err.NOT_ALLOWED, and a lost follow target gives Err.NO_TARGET. Every failure also emits EV_MOVE_FAILED(100) as unit id . RS_* . order type, and the RS_* stays readable via SimMovement.result(e).
- T_PATROL keeps the bounce origin in arg / arg2 (recorded at the first begin while both are 0). A lone patrol swaps (x, y) with it on every leg end. Chained patrols rotate through the dispatcher's cyclic re-append. With a T_ATTACK_MOVE handler registered, the leg is issued as a child (issue_internal, QM_FRONT) and the parent (p1 = 2) ends when the child is gone.
- T_FOLLOW defaults are max 3072 and min 1024 (arg / arg2 when > 0). It re-issues the follow goal every 20 ticks if the goal ended. SimMovement.follow ignores the minimum distance (MV1 behaviour).
- SimExitMove.find_free_cell_near now follows spec 5.9. Profiles are GROUND = wheeled size 2, SURFACE = naval size 2, UNDERWATER = sub size 2, and AIR returns the cell. Cells are visited in the domain-wide ring walk, and a cell is refused if a same-layer non-F_NO_COLLISION unit or structure centre lies within 600 units. MV1's row-major, size-1 version is gone, so exit cells picked by production and economy may differ from before (all existing tests still pass).
- eject_units_from_rect excludes the unit being ejected from the 600-unit clearance test and searches 6 rings. A unit with no free cell starts MS_EVICT: a straight drive, ignoring cell weights, to the nearest structure-free cell outside the rectangle within 12 rings, for at most 40 ticks. The result is RS_OK, or RS_STUCK on timeout.
- Handlers register in SimMovementSystem.init_world, so any world with movement enabled now has real T_MOVE / T_PATROL / T_FOLLOW / T_FACE / T_LAND. SimTestKit.install_movers on a real-movement world then logs one 'already has a handler' error per type, and the real handler wins. This affects CombatWK.world; the combat tests still pass.

**Tests:** ["`tools/gd check --strict`: 0 script errors, 0 lint violations. One warning remains, in tests/sim/test_sim_orders.gd:64, which is not mine.", "`tools/gd test move -q`: 67 passed, 0 failed (2077 checks). The 26 new tests cover:", "- test_move_orders: U7 worked example, 13 units and id permutation, group_of from every member's view; S4 30-unit group through the MOVE command (3 searches, 27 hits, arrival at tick 493); no-formation goals; STOP / SCATTER; failed order and queue continuation; T_FACE and FOLLOW; S17; S18; S19 (bounce for 3000 ticks, chained cycle, child T_ATTACK_MOVE).", "- test_move_air: S10, including exact takeoff and landing ticks.", "- test_move_exit: S12, ring order, S13, S14, the MS_EVICT room.", "- test_move_determinism: double run plus chunked stepping.", "`python3 tools/py/xplat_determinism.py res://tests/scenarios/xplat_move_orders.gd`: identical hash chains on macOS arm64, linux amd64 and linux arm64 (26 hashes).", "Full suite: 654 of 656 passed. The 2 failures are not from these changes:", "- sim/test_sim_determinism.gd::test_golden_scenario. Its world disables the movement stage, and the chain already differs at tick 0. Other agents edited combat and econom

**Known gaps:**
- Air separation is not implemented. Airborne aircraft do not push each other, and the HL_AIR grids are unused. Aircraft ignore is_turn_locked. Parked aircraft cost nothing.
- TM-14 (cargo approach handlers) and TM-16 beyond the one determinism test are not done. movement.json (formation.speed_match, air.*, turn_mode_override) does not exist. The constants are in SimFormation and SimAirMove.
- Sidestep escalation is still MV1's. SimExitMove has no separate sidestep_request. The facade's sidestep is unchanged.
- A patrol whose child T_ATTACK_MOVE fails at once will re-issue a leg each tick (bounded at one per tick). There is no back-off.
- Formation groups larger than 200 split by ascending id into a second group at the next begin.
- Aircraft crash motion, fuel and pad choice belong to combat and economy. This code only flies to points.

# === Wave 2b (wf_8fef26ac-ddd) ===

## CBA:combat-defs+damage

**Summary:** CB-00/01/02 are implemented and pass their tests. That covers the constants, the compiled combat tables built from DefPlayerView / DefRoster / DefWeaponSlot, the three components, the SimCombatSystem skeleton with its hooks and read API, the lease slots, and the damage pipeline. The pipeline includes flush, EMP, suppression, alerts and coalesced EV_HIT. U-PIPE-1..9, the 50 % cap, type-specific resistances, the Trident packet rule, U-SUP-1..4, U-EMP-1..5 and the D-4 checksum-sensitivity check all pass, and a double-run hash test is identical. Movement, projectiles, targeting, death and aircraft are not touched, as scoped. The movement stack is not called by this slice, so no test double was needed and nothing had to be switched.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Damage type ids follow the frozen data vocabulary: DT_BULLET 0, DT_AP 1, DT_HE 2, DT_THERMAL 3, DT_RAIL 4, DT_KINETIC 5, DT_EMP 6. The spec's DT_EXPLOSIVE and DT_BEAM names do not exist. KIND_/LAYER_ numbers mirror SimEntity. Event codes EV_FIRE..EV_WEAPON_LOCK (200..220) and the HITF_* bits of EV_HIT.d are in SimCombatConsts.
- There are no DefWarhead, DefProjectile, DefWeapon, DefMount, DefCombat, DefDeath, DefAir or DefCarrier classes. They are replaced by SimCombatWarhead (fields as DefWarhead), SimCombatDef and SimCombatTables, all under src/sim/combat/. The def holds armor, tags, cflags, prio, needs_power, emp_susceptible, is_aircraft, pad_count, carrier_bays, resist_rows (stride 4), dir_rows (stride 5), static_resist [parent, sub], aps_count/aps_cooldown/aps_radius and `slots`. The mount table has stride MT=15, and columns MT_SLOT, KIND, ARC_CENTER, ARC_HALF, TURN, AIM_TOL, OFF_FWD, OFF_SIDE, BARRELS, INDEP, WCLASS, WH, MODE_MASK, REQ_DEPLOYED, FLAGS. The MT_REQ_DEPLOYED derivation is a guess: it comes from the deploy ability's deployed_slots_n, listed slots = 1 and others = 0 when the list is non-empty.
- Tables: `SimCombatTables.build(data, view_or_null)`. SimCombatSystem holds `tables` (per pid, null for vacant) and `neutral_tables` (base defs, used for neutral owners and defs outside the roster). `def_for(world, e)`, `tables_of(pid)` and `matrix(dtype, armor)` are the lookups. The matrix is a copy of data.damage.matrix_bp in `matrix_bp`, and tests may mutate it. Warheads are per table: `tables_of(pid).warheads[MT_WH]`, and `add_warhead()` lets powers/chain effects register more. Factories: `SimCombatWarhead.from_slot(slot, arch, nonlethal_mask, label)` and `from_packet(DefImpactPacket, DefSuperweapon|null, nonlethal_mask, label)`.
- The roster clone's weapon slots already have the static bible layers folded in, so pipeline callers pass static_bp = 10000 and base = the view's effective slot damage (SimCombatSystem.slot_stat -> DefPlayerView.effective_slot_value, or layer3.struct_stat_bp for structures). `slot_stat(world, e, cd, mount, DefEnums.Stat.*)` is public. It returns milli-ticks for RELOAD. Units outside the owner's roster fall back to raw slot values.
- Derived guesses because the data lacks fields: splash_inner = splash_r/4 (see the CROSS_MODULE_REQUESTS); EMP durations default to 160/360 ticks and the class mask to VEHICLE|AIRCRAFT|SHIP|STRUCTURE for a slot of damage type EMP, while superweapon packets read params weapon_disable_t / structure_shutdown_t; needs_power = SF_POWERED_DEFENSE, and is_functional then requires SimFlags.F_POWERED; emp_susceptible = structure with power < 0 or any of SF_POWERED_DEFENSE / SF_PRODUCTION / SF_RELAY / SF_STRATEGIC; garrison_fire defaults to 1; prio is armor-based (units 6..9, unarmed 3, armed structures 10, other structures 2); stance_default is aggressive for everything; CF_ENEMY_ONLY is not set at spawn (the summon code sets it).
- Directional rows: frontal_shield uses arc_a/2 rounded up (1365 -> 683) with filter FILTER(types of resist_mask, req DC_DIRECT, forbid SPLASH|INDIRECT|STRATEGIC) and layer 0. directional_armor makes a front row (FILTER_ALL_WEAPON, front_arc_a/2, +front_reduction_bp) and, if rear_increase_bp > 0, a rear row (center 2048, half 1024, -rear_increase_bp).
- SimDamage API: `compute(world, v, wh: SimCombatWarhead, base, dc, static_bp, tmp_bp, atk_team, atk_ground: bool, falloff_bp, from_bat, pkt_bp) -> int` (pure). `deal(world, v, wh, base, dc, dflags, atk_id, atk_pid, atk_ground, static_bp, tmp_bp, falloff_bp, from_bat, pkt_bp, ix, iy) -> int` is compute plus enqueue; projectiles must use it. It returns 0 and queues nothing when the matrix is 0. `taken_bp(...)` sets `world.combat.scratch_hitf`, and HITF_* is shifted << 8 into the queue's dflags. Also `flush(world)`, `apply_emp(world, v, base_ticks, atk_pid)`, `suppress_hit(world, v) -> bool`, `clear_suppression(world, v)`, `emp_class`, `emp_base_ticks`. The queue is `world.combat.dmg_queue` (stride 10, constants Q_* in SimDamage), not a static in SimDamage. The per-tick taken_cache is not used (fields kept, exempt): resistance is recomputed per hit.
- Resistance layers: L0 = def base rows and direction rows; L1/L2 = roster.static_sums(RESIST) parent/sub (all weapon types except EMP); L3 = view.research_resist_bp (permanent research, per damage type) + STAT_TAKEN leases + packet reduction. Conditional research resistances (fire-mode / frontal-arc / cond effects) are not applied yet.
- Kernel names win over the spec's signatures: hooks are `on_remove(world, e, reason)` and `hash_state(world, buf)` (there is no hash_into(cs)). `hash_state` writes scan_cursor plus the damage queue contents (stronger than the spec's empty-queue assertion). `rescale_hp(world, e, new_hp_max)` takes a world and delegates to world.set_hp_max. `kill(world, e, cause, killer_id, killer_pid)` is minimal: it sets CF_DEAD and calls world.kill (killer_id -1 becomes 0); the death sequence (wrecks, occupants, chain effects, crash) arrives with CB-07 via on_dying. There is no `projectiles` member yet, and try_salvage / wreck_can_be_salvaged_by belong to CB-07. `verify_indexes(world)` is an added debug/test helper. on_debug mode 5 (set hp) is implemented.
- The SimCompCombat.mods array is allocated lazily on the first lease; before that it is empty, and the hash writes size + contents for mnt, mods, aps_next and the air/carrier arrays. Lease identity is (key, stat). Eviction removes the earliest expiry with the lowest slot on ties, and a new lease that expires strictly before every slot is dropped. Expired slots are reused first, and refresh() empties them canonically as [0, STAT_NONE, 0, 0, 0]. Leases are also skipped for F_GONE entities. Entities with leases are kept in status_ids for upkeep.
- STAT_MARK filter: team bit `1 << (team & 7)` plus 256 for ground-only. Use `SimCombatMods.mark_filter(team, ground_only)`. Teams above 7 alias modulo 8.
- Mirrors: EMP sets F_EMP_SHUT | F_WEAPONS_OFF and expiry clears them; suppression sets and clears F_SUPPRESSED. is_functional() for structures relies on the power domain writing F_POWERED each tick, and the real power stage was observed clearing it, so tests set it themselves.
- EV_WEAPON_LOCK is emitted only for non-structure victims and only on the offline/online transition, not on re-hits. Alerts skip damage instances that deal neither damage nor EMP, and skip attackers that are SELF or ALLY to the victim owner.

**Tests:** `tools/gd check --strict src tests`: 0 script errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings in combat files (6 warnings remain in other agents' files). `tools/gd test combat -q`: 5 files, 52 tests, 52 passed (540 checks), identical on `tools/gd linux test combat -q`. `tools/gd test sim core -q`: 138 of 144 pass. The 6 failures come from other domains' work in progress (movement, economy, vision), not combat: test_sim_api::test_footprints, test_sim_determinism::test_golden_scenario, test_sim_types::test_components_instantiate / test_entity_hash_details / test_player_hash_details, and the test_sim_world::test_opts error at sim_movement_system.gd:94. test_components_instantiate still fails for the other domains' filled components; the combat comps are now excluded from its empty-stub assertion.

**Known gaps:**
- Not implemented (later tasks): P2 air/carrier, P3 scans, P4 weapons, P5 projectiles (SimCombatSystem.update carries TODO(combat) markers), the retaliation hook SimTargeting.on_damaged in flush, the death sequence / wreck / salvage API, and SimProjectiles.
- APS parameters (aps_count / cooldown / radius) are read from the def's interceptor ability only. Research changes to them (layer3.ability_param) are not applied yet; that belongs with interception in CB-04.
- Static EMP recovery reads the def param emp_recovery_bp (layer3.def_param / struct_def_param, base 10000, reduction = 10000 - folded). It is verified only for the no-research case: no test loads the two research effects.
- SAP defense_online (power reserve) is not consulted in is_functional. Conditional research resistances are not applied (see the CROSS_MODULE_REQUESTS).
- No cross-platform hash-chain scenario (tools/py/xplat_determinism.py) was added. Linux amd64 passes the combat tests.

**Cross-module requests:**
- data (DefWeaponSlot / DefWeaponArch / abilities): expose splash_inner (full-damage radius) per weapon; EMP durations (weapon_disable_t / structure_shutdown_t) for unit EMP weapons; an emp_susceptible flag on structures and a needs_power flag (combat derives them from SF_* flags today); a per-slot suppressive default; default threat prio and stance for defs; mount geometry (aim tolerance, muzzle offsets, arc center) if authored. Combat currently defaults all of these.
- data / DefLayer3: the accessors `cond_effects_of_unit` / `cond_effects_of_struct` exist but combat does not consume them yet. Please confirm the intended runtime resolution of conditional RESIST_MOD (fire_mode_mask, frontal_arc_a, ON_WATER) so combat can add the rows, and confirm that `emp_recovery_bp` (10000 base, MUL_BP) is the right key for Buried Command Lines / Resilient Mesh.
- kernel test owner: tests/sim/test_sim_types.gd::test_components_instantiate assumes every SimComp* stub hashes nothing. It now skips the three combat comps, but it still fails for the other domains' comps (move, econ, vision), so it needs a general allow-list. tests/support/sim_test_kit.gd CombatStub was made inert so it does not depend on this system; keep that when extending the kit.
- power: keep writing SimFlags.F_POWERED per structure each tick (combat's is_functional reads it for powered defences) and provide defense_online(world, pid) for the SAP reserve so combat can call it.
- abilities / zones: call SimCombatMods.apply / clear_key with mark_filter(team, ground_only) for STAT_MARK, SimDamage.clear_suppression for Coordinated Advance, and world.combat.heal for repairs. Do not write e.hp directly.
- orders / CB-03..07 (next combat tasks): projectiles and hitscan must go through SimDamage.deal (never write hp), and call `world.combat.note_status(id)` when they start a timer on an entity.
- map/app: still nobody registers structure footprints on a real MapData (KB report); combat tests use the SimTestKit map.

## ECA:econ-records+power+placement

**Summary:** ECA (E0, E2, E4) is implemented and green. It covers the economy constants and records, SimEconomySystem (stage 3), SimPowerSystem (stage 4), SimPlacement and SimStructureLife.
E0: SimEconConst holds every enum, RSN, ST, PC, PROD, QS, CR, K, CMD and EVT code; SimPlayerEcon, SimCompEcon and SimCompProd are filled, and SimCompWreck, SimCompTemp, SimPowerSlot, SimWarning, SimScheduled, SimImpactPacket and SimPlacementResult are new. All records hash through hash_into(buf) (DR-13).
E2: a ledger over the kernel credits (spend/earn with statistics and the 60 s income ring), the knob table, kernel hooks mapped onto on_spawn / on_dying / on_remove / on_owner_changed, and neutral income and power. It also does BUILDUP -> ACTIVE, sell and undeploy completion, the power balance and the shortage FSM. The SAP reserve follows the spec's worked timelines.
E4: placement rules 1-10, dock berth, the strategic limit, validate_hq_site and find_site, plus activate/deactivate, sell, undeploy, MCV deploy and the free Collector.
The HQ-only opening scenario (Generator -> Refinery -> Barracks -> Factory via real BUILD_START/BUILD_PLACE, brownout at half speed, sell refund) runs on a construction-queue test double, because the real production system (E3) is still a stub.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- LEDGER: credits, handicap scaling, unit_count, struct and rebuilder counters are kernel-owned (SimPlayer.credits, income_frac, unit_count, struct_count, rebuilders; world.add_credits / try_spend). SimPlayerEcon therefore has NO credits, income_bp, income_acc, unit_count, mcv_count or real_structure_count fields. Use economy.credits(pid) or players[pid].credits; q_can_rebuild reads the kernel counters. Handicap scaling (12/13/12/13 at 125 %) is the kernel's; CR_DEPOT is mapped to CASH_HARVEST so it scales too.
- spend() and earn() are wrappers over world.try_spend / add_credits. Progressive reasons (CONSTRUCTION, PRODUCTION, RESEARCH, REPAIR) spend silently (no kernel CASH event per tick); per-category statistics are updated in spend(). Other reasons emit CASH.
- Record hashing is hash_into(buf: PackedInt32Array), not checksum_into(ck), following the kernel SimComponent contract. SimPlayerEcon lives at p.econ (no world.econ_players). Systems must not hold the world, so the pid-only spec signatures (credits, can_afford, unit_cap_room, knob, q_*, supply, powered, ...) use a WeakRef set in init_world; the spec's world parameters stay where the spec has them.
- There is no world.life / world.placement member: the lifecycle helper is world.economy.life (SimStructureLife); SimPlacement is static. world.strategic = SimStrategicSystem.new() is assigned in economy.init_world (the stub has no update; power calls strategic.update only if the method exists).
- SimCompTemp.script is renamed script_kind (collides with Object.script). SimCompWreck and SimCompTemp exist but have no SimEntity slot (the kernel has none); E6/E7 decide where they hang. SimImpactPacket has no hash (scratch). ORD_* is not duplicated: the kernel SimOrder.T_* codes apply. KNOB_MODE / KNOB_DEFAULT tables are in SimEconConst; knob(pid, k), knob_of(pe, k, tick), set_knob_base(world, pid, k, v) and set_knob_temp(pid, k, v, until) are the API. set_knob_base implements the reserve-capacitors rule and resizes airfield pads.
- SimPlacement.find_site takes out_cell: PackedInt32Array ([cx, cy]) instead of Vector2i: a Vector2i argument cannot be an out parameter in GDScript. Extra API: SimPlacement.footprint_of_def, hq_def_of_mcv, entity_origin, exit_cell, can_eject_units, eject_units(world, pid, res); SimStructureLife.place(world, pid, s_idx, cx, cy, rot, paid_cost, res=null) spawns the BUILDUP structure (F_UNDER_CONSTRUCTION, paid_cost, eject, EVT_STRUCTURE_PLACED) and E3 should use it.
- Placement has no structure_rules.json (superseded). The apron is the producer's exit cell (DefStructure.exit_dx / exit_dy, rotated for a Dock): it must be passable ground, free of structures and outside the footprint, and a new footprint must not cover another producer's exit cell (only when the exit lies outside the footprint). The dock berth is the fw x 2 strip beyond the footprint on the exit side: all DEEP cells, one water body of >= 30 cells. I do NOT call MapBuildRules.check_berth (see cross-module requests). Deposit rule = map.field_of[cell] != 0. Power class is derived from DefStructure flags, queue_kind and power sign: COLLECTOR queue -> ECON, any queue -> PRODUCER, SF_STRATEGIC, SF_RELAY, SF_POWERED_DEFENSE, power < 0 -> SENSOR (Radar, Laboratory), else NONE. HQ = build_radius > 0; the MCV -> HQ pairing is DefStructure.deploy_unit.
- Unit blocking: enemy units, and any ground unit when no eject service exists, block with UNIT_BLOCK; friendly mobile units are ejected. The service is detected at runtime, SimMovement.eject_units_from_rect on the static facade or an instance method of world.movement (neither exists yet, so the fallback is active).
- Lifecycle: placement spawns must pass F_UNDER_CONSTRUCTION (BUILDUP, 30 ticks); a structure spawned without it is ACTIVE at once, and F_INITIAL adds EF_FREE. EVT_STRUCTURE_ACTIVE is emitted only for BUILDUP -> ACTIVE (not for structures that spawn active). An HQ deployed from an MCV is BUILDUP for DefStructure.deploy_t (60 ticks) instead of 30. BUILDUP -> ACTIVE, sell and undeploy completion run in economy stage 3 (SimStructureLife.tick_buildups is idempotent, so production may also call it in stage 2). The MCV spawns at the HQ exit cell (nearest free cell) with paid_cost = mcv_paid_cost. Killed structures unregister at stage 11 (on_dying), not at kill().
- Power: the balance is recomputed in stage 3 for dirty players and every 100 ticks (no incremental supply/demand), skipping F_GONE structures. Timing convention: shortage first seen at tick S makes defenses offline from S + 400; power first adequate at R refills the reserve when tick R + 1200 is evaluated (matches the spec's worked timeline). F_POWERED is refreshed on every structure every tick in stage 4. Neutral income is 250 mcpt paid in whole 50-credit payouts (every 200 ticks) through CR_DEPOT.
- The wrench repair loop and the SET_STRUCT_REPAIR effect are not implemented (E6 repair_step): the command only toggles repair_on, F_REPAIR_ON and EVT_REPAIR_STATE. init_player(world, pid, roster_idx, start_credits = -1, handicap_pct = 100) does not grant credits unless start_credits >= 0 (the kernel already did). Economy executors registered: SELL, SET_STRUCT_REPAIR, UNDEPLOY_HQ, with the RSN in cmd.detail and SimEconomySystem.err_of(rsn) mapping RSN -> SimCommand.Err.
- Kernel test edit outside my list: game/tests/sim/test_sim_types.gd, two edits following the combat agent's precedent. The 'empty stub hashes nothing' loop skips SimCompEcon, SimCompProd and SimPlayerEcon, and test_player_hash_details reads the component mask at b1[34].

**Tests:** ["tools/gd check --strict on all my src/sim files, tests/econ, tests/support/sim_econ_kit.gd and sim_econ_prod_double.gd: 'RESULT: OK (0 script error(s), 0 lint violation(s), 0 warning(s))'.", "tools/gd test econ: '== 72 files, 43 tests in 2.00 s: 43 passed, 0 failed, 0 errors, 0 skipped (3900 checks)' (the filter also matches other agents' files).", "My 7 files: test_econ_ledger (10.1-A: handicap 12 / 13 / 12 / 13, all-or-nothing spend, income ring after 12 buckets, unit cap, knob modes), test_power_grid (10.1-E: strict shortage 150 vs 150 / 180, SAP timelines 1400 / 2700, short outage 200 / 2000, Reserve Capacitors, EMP still consumes, non-SAP offline at once), test_placement (10.1-D: 7.5 / 8.5-cell radius, exact d2 67108864, overlap, deposit, debris, units, aprons, Dock at rotations 0-3, strategic limit, HQ site, find_site), test_structure_life (BUILDUP, sell refund 300 of 600, MCV deploy / undeploy value restored, free Collector, capture moves power, neutral +100 power / +50 credits per 200 ticks), test_econ_scenario (opening, brownout 800 ticks for a 400-tick Barracks, sell +250, double-run determinism), test_econ_hash (DR-13 reflection coverage of 8 record classes, checksum s

**Known gaps:**
- E3 (production, queues, research, rate table refresh, cap reservation) is not in this slice: the real SimProductionSystem is still a stub. The scenario uses the test double SimEconProdDouble (tests/support), which implements only the construction queue. E3 must call SimStructureLife.place(...) for BUILD_PLACE, SimPlacement.validate for enqueue and placement checks, and read power via world.power.production_rate_bp(pid).
- Not done (other tasks): harvesting, dock protocol and deposit table (E5); repair_step, capture, salvage, wrecks (E6, including the wrench loop); the strategic system (E7 / E8). SimCompWreck and SimCompTemp have no entity slot. The Refinery's Collector is spawned with EF_FREE but has no harvest behaviour yet.
- Not covered by tests: the puddle-dock case (< 30 water cells), airfield pad resizing, cap_reserved release on flush_queue, and a Barracks captured with a queued unit (owner_changed refund path).
- The strategic sell lock (launcher with a WARNING attack) is a hook: sell_locked(pid, eid) on world.strategic, if E7 provides it.

**Cross-module requests:**
- MAP (MapBuildRules.check_berth): its naval-component test uses nav.region_size(NP_NAVAL, SZ_2, berth_cell). A berth cell next to the shore has clearance 1, so region_size is 0 and every real dock is refused with PR_WATER_EXIT once a naval graph exists (reproduced on a 19x19 lake with a beach island). SimPlacement therefore checks only DEEP kind, one water_comp and water_size >= 30 itself. Please fix check_berth (for example use the water body size or a clearance-independent region) or tell me to switch back.
- MOVEMENT (MV2): provide static SimMovement.eject_units_from_rect(world, x0, y0, x1, y1, team) (cell rect, inclusive) as in terrain_movement 3.10; SimPlacement.can_eject_units / eject_units pick it up at runtime with no edit on my side. Also SimMovement.find_free_cell_near, which SimStructureLife.find_free_cell replaces with its own ring search for now. sim_movement_system.gd init_world calls world.combat.has_method without a null check (the test_opts error).
- ECB / production (E3): (a) enqueue and placement checks through SimPlacement.validate; place through world.economy.life.place(world, pid, s_idx, cx, cy, rot, paid_cost, res), which spawns with F_UNDER_CONSTRUCTION; (b) drive rate_bp from world.power.production_rate_bp(pid) and set pe.rates_dirty consumers; (c) unit cap = world.economy.unit_cap_room(pid) and pe.cap_reserved; (d) research completion should call world.economy.set_knob_base(world, pid, K_*, v) for the knob writers (this also handles the SAP reserve rule) and player.view.apply_research; (e) release cap_reserved and refund via world.economy.life.flush_queue(world, ent, pid, refund) when a producer is sold or captured; (f) production may call world.economy.life.tick_buildups(world) in stage 2 (idempotent); (g) drop tests/support/sim_econ_prod_double.gd from the scenario once the real construction queue exists.
- KERNEL / QA: regenerate GOLDEN_* in tests/sim/test_sim_determinism.gd and the xplat scenario after all domains land (players and entities now carry econ hashes). Also tighten tests/sim/test_sim_types.gd once the movement comps land (test_entity_hash_details expects e.move to be an empty stub).
- DATA: DefStructure carries no aprons, berth or pad cells. Exit cells (exit_dx / exit_dy) are used as the apron and a fixed 3x2 strip as the dock berth. If richer aprons or berths are wanted, extend the schema (DefStructure + validator). Real HQ data: deploy_t 60, exit (0,3), deploy_unit = MCV. DefTestKit.small_data() structures have exit (0,0) and no deploy pads, so my kit patches exit_dy.

## ECB:production+harvesting

**Summary:** E3 (SimProductionSystem) and E5 (SimDepositTable, SimOrderHarvest, SimEconomyDocks) are implemented on the real movement facade, so no test double is needed for movement.
Production covers: construction, unit and research queues with bp-tick payment, the power/knob rate table, prerequisite / funds / cap / exit / shutdown pauses, cap reservation, exit and rally, pads API, research knobs, and executors for 120-131.
Harvesting: collectors auto-harvest, unload at refineries through a FIFO dock, flee from raiders and mark the field dangerous. Deposits deplete, with opt-in regrowth.
One Collector with the free-Collector loop earns about 735 credits/min on a 10-cell field, with a steady 918-tick cycle (framework: 766/min, 941 ticks). The 3-minute total is about 1800 (band 1500-2400). The double-run determinism test is green.
`tools/gd test econ` is 69/70; the failure is ECA's stale `test_placement::test_units` (see known_gaps).
`check --strict` is clean on my files. I made small edits to `sim_economy_system.gd`, `test_sim_commands.gd` and ECA's `SimEconKit` users.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- INIT/SIGNATURES: SimProductionSystem extends SimSystem (stage 2), not RefCounted. There is no setup(world, rules, srules); registration of executors 120-131 happens in init_world (Callable to handle_command). All spec 3.3 signatures are otherwise as written. Extra public methods: research_prereqs_met(world, pid, r_idx), apply_research_knobs(world, pid, r_idx), airfield_pad_* (4), the 3.11 aliases construction_state/queue_of/queue_progress_pct/research_active/research_done, q_* queries (q_rate_bp takes no world and uses a weak ref).
- SimEconomySystem (ECA file) edited minimally: new members `deposits: SimDepositTable` (set up in init_world from world.map + world.data.economy) and `docks: SimEconomyDocks`; delegations dock_request / dock_unload_step / dock_release / dock_queue_len; q_collectors, q_deposit_nearest; update() also runs deposits.update + docks.watchdog; hash_state() appends deposits.hash_into; init_world registers T_HARVEST and T_RETURN_CARGO handlers and register_idle(harvest handler); init_player resizes field_danger to deposits.count.
- HARVEST NUMBERS come from DefEconomy / global.json, not the economy spec: capacity 600 (ability param capacity_cr, fallback data.economy.collector_capacity_cr), harvest 1 credit/tick (harvest_mcpt 1000, carried in h_timer), unload 6 credits/tick (unload_mcpt), unload_overhead_t 40 split 60/40 into dock-in glide (24) and dock-out glide (16). Cycle at 10 cells: about 918-980 ticks.
- DEPOSITS: SimDepositTable does NOT hold stock; the credits live in MapData cells (harvest_field / regrow_field / field_remaining), so deposit stock is covered by the map's deposit_hash. The table holds per-field geometry (deposit idx = index in MapData.deposit_fields()), class, cap, harvesters, last_harvest_tick, next_regen_tick, depleted, plus search_tick/search_used (global 6 searches per tick budget). Its hash_into appends nothing when count == 0 (keeps map-less goldens unchanged). No deposit_mask (SimPlacement already reads the map's field layer). Regrowth is implemented (40/60/100 per 200 ticks after 1200 idle ticks) but OFF by default (`deposits.regrow = false`, global.json deposit.regrow=false).
- ORDER ENCODING: T_HARVEST: OF_AUTO with arg 0 = auto; arg = deposit idx + 1 = manual field (also used by the rally target < 0); a player command (no OF_AUTO) resolves the field from x,y via SimDepositTable.idx_at (falls back to auto if not on a field). T_RETURN_CARGO: target_id = refinery id or 0. The T_HARVEST handler instance is also the idle handler (every idle Collector gets an auto order). A dying/ending order releases the field and dock (on_end incl. END_DIED).
- ENQUEUE PREREQ RULE: can_queue_structure accepts a structure whose missing prerequisite is itself already in the construction queue (spec 10.2 S1 queues the whole opening at tick 0); the head then sits in PAUSED_PREREQ until the prerequisite is ACTIVE. Units/research require the prerequisites at enqueue time.
- KNOBS: research knob writes live in a const table (SimProductionSystem.RESEARCH_KNOBS, 12 research ids of economy 5.8) instead of economy.json (no competing JSON). Applied through SimEconomySystem.set_knob_base at research completion.
- RATES: rate_bp[] is recomputed EVERY tick at the start of stage 2 from pe.power_state (data.economy.power_shortage_rate_bp) and the effective Barracks/Factory knobs (so it lags the power stage by one tick). PROD_CONSTRUCTION / PROD_RESEARCH follow the power rate only.
- PADS: pad cell = middle row of the Airfield footprint, pads centred along it (no structure_rules data); airfield_service_rate_bp = effective K_REARM_RATE_BP x boost, 0 unless the structure is online. on_structure_activated only (re)sizes pad_ent (the free Collector is already spawned by SimStructureLife.activate). Aircraft spawn on the lowest free pad; rally is ignored for aircraft.
- EVENTS: EVT_QUEUE_STATE is emitted on head-state change only (a=pid, b=producer id / 0 construction / -1 research, c=QS_*); EVT_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS and EVT_UNIT_CAP_REACHED on the transition into the paused state (no timers/state added). EVT_NO_REFINERY about once per 600 ticks (tick % 600 < 120 window, stateless). EVT_CREDITS_GAINED (whole unload session) is emitted by the dock when the cargo reaches 0.
- FLEE simplification: scan every 10 ticks (h_scan_tick) or on hp loss, armed non-air enemy unit/structure within 6 cells; loaded Collectors go straight to a safe Refinery (TO_REFINERY) with field_danger[f] = tick + 400 and h_flee_until = tick + 200; empty ones go to the safe Refinery/nearest structure and wait in H_FLEE. Foreign-file edits: tests/sim/test_sim_commands.gd now uses replace_handler for the fake order handlers (economy registers T_HARVEST/T_RETURN_CARGO) and expects WRONG_KIND for TRAIN(1,2,3) (production executor exists now).

New: `econ/test_prod_queue` (12 tests) and `econ/test_harvest_cycle` (15 tests), all green.
They cover 10.1-B payment 2/5/7, refund 1000, shortage 1000 ticks, Mobilization 880 ticks (6250 bp); 10.1-C queue cases; S1 opening READY 500/1331/1732/2533 within +/-5; real-data knobs and pads; 10.1-G cycle, choose_field, FIFO, regrowth; S3 raid; S7 income band; and a hash-coverage / double-run test.

`tools/gd test combat move data -q`: 276/276 pass.

## CBB:weapons+projectiles+targeting

**Summary:** CB-03/04/05 implemented and green: weapons (aim/slew, gates, salvo/reload/ammo, hitscan, beams, muzzle), projectile pool (bullet/missile/arc/bomb/strike/sweep, swept test, splash/falloff/friendly fire, APS + Trident hooks, pool-cap fallback, snapshot API, hashed) and targeting (filters, scoring, budgeted scan rotation, stances/leash, validation, retaliation/assist, overkill guard, independent mounts). SimCombatSystem.update now runs P3 scans, P4 weapons, P5 projectiles, then the flush (SimDamage.flush now calls SimTargeting.on_damaged once per victim). The data layer has no burst/accuracy/guidance numbers, so a private per-mount profile (SimWeaponProfile, SimCombatDef.prof) is derived from DefWeaponSlot + DefWeaponArch. Movement is real, but nothing here needed it beyond the F_MOVING mirror, so no test double was used.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Class layout: SimWeapons is an instance at cs.weapons (scratch only, not hashed); SimProjectiles is an instance at cs.proj (NOT world.projectiles); SimTargeting is all statics. There is no SimOrderCombat here (CB-06). Fire control (set_stance/set_target/set_ground_target/clear_target/can_attack) is on SimCombatSystem and delegates to SimTargeting. can_attack has an extra param any_mode=true (mode-gated mounts count for orders, not for auto acquisition/retaliation). clear_target(e, keep_auto) keeps only auto/retal/guard targets with an entity id; ordered/forced/ground targets are always cleared. It zeroes M_BURST but bumps M_CD to at least M_NEXT so an abandoned salvo still owes its cooldown; beams end in the next P4 (EV_BEAM_END reason 3/4).
- Derived profile (SimWeaponProfile.PF_*, SimCombatDef.pf(m,col)): PK_BEAM for proj_kind BEAM; PK_BOMB for BOMB; PK_MISSILE for direct MISSILE; PK_ARC for indirect MISSILE, ROCKET, GRENADE and indirect SHELL; PK_BULLET for direct SHELL and TORPEDO; everything else (bullet, pellets, rail, charge, field) is PK_HITSCAN. burst = hits_per_volley (interval 3 ticks for bullet/hitscan, else 4, clamped to reload). Accuracy: 10000 for homing archs (all current hitscan archs), otherwise 8000 with falloff 3000, move penalty 1500, shooter penalty 1000. The 56.4 % worked example matches the spec. PF_FLAGS come from DefWeaponSlot.interceptable (APS_TRIDENT -> APS|ZONE, TRIDENT -> ZONE) plus GUIDED for homing missiles and AIRBURST_ON_EXPIRE for air-only weapons. Missile turn 160 bat/tick; arc scatter_min = scatter/8, scatter_max = scatter; arc flight clamp 4..400; bomb fall 16 ticks; lead 10000 for homing direct shots and missile-artillery/cruise, else 0; WF_STATIONARY_FIRE -> settle 6 ticks; target mask TF_* = layer bits, plus TF_STRUCTURE when ground or water is targetable.
- Reload is counted from the salvo START, not from the last shot: M_CD = max(tick+1, salvo_start + reload_eff), because the balance sheets' reload_s is the full cycle (DPS = hits*damage/reload_s). reload_eff(pm, base, agg_bp) is SimWeapons.reload_eff, exactly the spec formula. Salvo abort after more than 2*interval+2 failed ticks sets M_CD = tick + reload/2. The ammo gate applies only when a salvo starts; ammo is spent at salvo start.
- Mount state reuse: M_NEXT doubles as 'last tick with a target' while no salvo runs (idle turret return); M_DOT doubles as the salvo start tick for non-beam mounts; M_BEAM = beam start tick or -1 (SimCombatSystem._init_mounts now initialises -1); for non-independent beam mounts M_TARGET holds the locked beam target. Beam ramp: 10000 rising linearly to slot.ramp_max_bp over slot.ramp_t (ticks), dot interval = reload_ticks, damage = max(1, mul(resolved damage, ramp)); no overheat by default (PF_BEAM_MAX=0); cooling after a stop = reload_eff.
- Damage: unit and structure weapon damage comes from cs.slot_stat (research applied), so projectiles carry a resolved base damage in an extra pool field `dmg` and dmg_bp is 10000; dmg_tmp is cc.agg_dmg_bp. An extra instance flag SimProjectiles.PI_AIR_SHOOTER = 64 marks non-ground shooters for STAT_MARK. Hitscan with hit chance >= 10000 (structures, homing archs) makes no RNG draw; a miss draws twice (hit roll, bearing).
- Events: EV_FIRE.b and EV_PROJ_SPAWN.b (pdef) carry the DefEnums.WeaponArch index (there is no DefWeapon/DefProjectile); EV_PROJ_END is emitted only for non-impact ends (reasons 0 expired, 1 APS, 2 zone, 4 sweep done); a detonation ends its projectile through EV_IMPACT (b = serial). The pool-cap instant fallback emits EV_FIRE with result 1 and no spawn/end events. EV_INTERCEPT.d for an APS kill is the interceptor's ready tick; for a zone kill it is 0 (charges unknown to combat).
- SimProjectiles API: launch(...) is the internal path used by SimWeapons. Public: spawn_remote(world, owner_pid, owner_id, proj_idx, wh_idx, ox, oy, tx, ty, delay_ticks, dmg_bp, inst_flags) where proj_idx is a SimCombatConsts.PK_* code (PK_STRIKE default, PK_ARC or PK_BOMB); spawn_sweep(world, owner_pid, owner_id, proj_idx, wh_idx, ax, ay, bx, by, delay, duration, width=3072, spot_len=3072, dot=5); detonate(world, wh, cx, cy, direct_id, base, static_bp, tmp_bp, pid, oid, iflags, dir_bat, ser, emit_impact) for chain effects/crash impacts; live_count(), is_live(slot), slot_of_serial(ser), snapshot(out) with SNAP_STRIDE 11 (serial, kind, pdef, x, y, px, py, heading, owner_pid, target, t_end). wh_idx is an index into cs.tables_of(owner_pid).warheads; SimCombatTables.add_warhead is how powers register theirs. Also public statics for tests/callers: swept_hit, arc_flight, scatter_radius, disc_radius, falloff_bp.
- Zone interface: combat calls world.zones.intercept_ordinary(world, owner_team, x0, y0, x1, y1, sx, sy, proj_flags) and intercept_packet(world, owner_team, x, y) as INSTANCE methods, via has_method/call (the stub SimZoneSystem has neither, so nothing is intercepted until the zone owner adds them). Vision goes through world.fog.entity_visible/decoy_identified.
- Targeting details: filters use TF_* masks derived per mount; candidate query is enemies only within R_acq (+2560 margin) of UNIT/STRUCTURE kinds; independent mounts (MT_INDEP, only when n_mounts > 1) pick their own target into mnt[M_TARGET] in the same scan; primary targets from scans use TS_AUTO. Structures use R_acq = range (no +2048). SimTargeting.validate returns VS_OK/VS_HIDDEN/VS_NONE each tick (hidden targets are kept for the give-up window but not shot). Retaliation and assist are SimTargeting.on_damaged; ASSIST last_assist_tick is initialised to NEVER in on_spawn. on_spawn also staggers scan_next = tick + id % interval. want_deploy/want_mode/want_surface are re-derived each tick (reset at the start of the entity's P4 pass).
- SimCombatSystem.slot_stat now caches damage/range/reload/proj_speed per SimCombatDef keyed on DefPlayerView.version; a new invalidate_stats() clears it (tests that edit weapon slots must call it, CombatWK.slot_set does). on_player_eliminated now clears targets of the eliminated player.

**Tests:** tools/gd check --strict: 0 script errors, 0 lint violations; the only warnings left are in other agents' files (tests/bench/dbg_mapgen*.gd, tests/econ/test_harvest_cycle.gd, tests/sim/test_sim_orders.gd). tools/gd test combat_: 82 files, 99 tests, 99 passed, 859 checks (includes the existing CB-01/02 tests). New files: test_combat_weapons (19 tests: U-GEOM-1, U-GEOM-2 vector and in-world, muzzle, U-REL-1, hit chance 5643, hitscan, salvo/ammo cadence [0,3,6,30,33,36], gates, want_* requests, beam ramp/overheat/cooling/target change, S-1 mirror duel with simultaneous death, idle turret return, determinism double run on a 16-unit brawl), test_combat_projectiles (14 tests: U-GEOM-3/4, U-PIPE-8 = 69, friendly fire/layers/annulus, U-INT-1 Ural, S-4, U-INT-2/S-5 Trident with a fake zone, S-2 howitzer, S-6 Helios, pool cap soft/hard/reserve, snapshot, checksum sensitivity of every pool field), test_combat_targeting (17 tests: U-TGT-1..4, filters, stances, validation, scan schedule, starvation rotation, urgent scan, independent AA mount, elimination). tools/gd test sim: 153/154 pass; test_sim_determinism::test_golden_scenario fails on a changed tick-0 checksum that predates or is independen

**Known gaps:**
- Not implemented: garrison fire from containers, WF_NEEDS_LOS, PF_BLOCKED_BY_HOSTILES, the 128/tick cosmetic EV_FIRE cap, SimCombatSystem.lock_weapons and clear_suppression wrappers (SimDamage.clear_suppression exists), the requires_surface_t 'surfaced this long' duration (only layer == SURFACE is checked), research-adjusted APS cooldown (uses the def's base aps_cooldown), garrison damage variant, aircraft attack-style/bomb release logic (CB-08), and all order handlers/chasing/hull turning (CB-06).
- P2 (air/carrier) is still a TODO in SimCombatSystem.update. Aircraft in TAKEOFF/LANDING are not scanned; PARKED/REARM/DOCKED aircraft are skipped in scans and weapons.
- ext_moving is not written by movement; combat reads it OR the F_MOVING mirror for stationary-fire and accuracy.
- Retaliation and assist require the attacker to be visible to the victim's owner via world.fog (default: everything visible).
- Sweep parameters (width, spot length, dot interval) are spawn_sweep arguments with the spec seed defaults, not data-driven.

**Cross-module requests:**
- DATA: DefWeaponSlot/units_*.json weapon instances should carry the numbers combat currently derives: burst and burst_interval, accuracy (acc, falloff, move and shooter penalties), aim_delay, hit radius, missile turn_rate/homing_delay/launch_delay, min/max flight, scatter_min, splash_inner, beam_max/overheat, sweep width/spot length/dot interval. SimWeaponProfile.derive is the only place to switch over.
- ZONES (abilities): implement instance methods SimZoneSystem.intercept_ordinary(world, owner_team, x0, y0, x1, y1, sx, sy, proj_flags) -> zone id or -1 and intercept_packet(world, owner_team, x, y) -> 0 or 5000; combat calls them via has_method. Call world.combat.proj.spawn_remote / spawn_sweep / detonate for Aurora, Atlas, Perun, Horizon, Helios and Counterbattery-type effects.
- TEST KIT OWNER: SimTestKit.install_movers (tests/support/sim_test_kit.gd) now logs 'order type 16/17 already has a handler' errors because the real SimMovementSystem registers those handlers; make it a no-op when the real movement system is present. Combat tests use CombatWK.world() and do not call it.
- MOVEMENT: optionally mirror SimCompCombat.ext_moving (currently combat also reads SimFlags.F_MOVING).
- ORDERS (CB-06): use SimTargeting.set_target/set_ground_target/clear_target/can_attack (also on SimCombatSystem), SimTargeting.rate(world, cs, e, t) for scoring, cc.anchor_* for leash, and read cc.want_* set by P4. Chase/turn_to decisions stay with the order handlers.
- DEATH (CB-07): SimDamage.flush calls cs.kill -> world.kill; hook on_dying there. Use world.combat.proj.detonate(..., PI_CHAIN) for chain blasts/crash impacts and SimProjectiles.PI_* flags for their damage class.
- KERNEL: tests/sim/test_sim_determinism.gd golden values need regeneration by the kernel owner (tick-0 checksum currently differs; not caused by combat).

## CBC:orders+death+air

**Summary:** CB-06/07/08 are implemented on the real movement stack, so no movement test double was needed. Combat orders and idle engagement (SimOrderCombat), the death sequence with wrecks, occupants, chain blasts and crash (SimDeath), and the aircraft sortie machine (SimAirSortie) are all in, wired into SimCombatSystem. The end-to-end scenario passes: two small armies (infantry plus tanks) fight to the death through real commands, with an identical checksum and event digest on a double run. `tools/gd test combat` passes; the combat, sim and support directories pass `check --strict`. The full suite has 5 failures and 1 error outside combat, listed under tests. Carriers (CB-09) and the CB-12 suite beyond one scenario per task are skipped as scoped.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
