- Order handlers: `SimOrderCombat extends SimOrderHandler`, one instance per order type (`SimOrderCombat.new(SimOrder.T_ATTACK)`, T_ATTACK_MOVE, T_GUARD, T_HOLD, T_FORCE_FIRE, T_RETURN_BASE). `SimOrderCombat.new(SimOrder.T_NONE)` is the idle handler, registered with register_idle. The spec's static apply_command, step and on_order_start do not exist. Reusable statics: step_idle, idle_chase, engage_move, best_mount, live_target, salvo_sum, cmd_set_stance, cmd_scuttle.
- Commands: the kernel's built-in executors already turn commands 40..44 and 47 into orders, and validation is in each handler's `can_issue`. `SimCombatSystem.init_world` registers executors only for CMD_SET_STANCE (45) and CMD_SCUTTLE (46). It also registers the handlers, guarded so a repeated init_world (tests) does not double-register.
- `SimCombatSystem.order_gate` clears `anchor_on` on every order, so the unit re-anchors where it next becomes idle. For aircraft, a non-combat order (move, patrol, land) calls SimAirSortie.release_mission.
- `anchor_on` values: 0 none, 1 anchored, 2 pulled away from the anchor by an idle chase. The idle handler walks back only from 2 and stops only chases it started itself, so units driven directly through the movement facade are untouched.
- ATTACK_MOVE order fields: p1 is the state (AM_MOVE 0, AM_ENGAGE 1, 2 = waiting for unpack), arg and arg2 are the engagement origin, p0 is the no-target-since tick. GUARD stores the previous stance plus 1 in p1 and switches the unit to ST_GUARD for the order. FORCE_FIRE uses arg as the salvo count, arg2 = 1 when counting, and p0 as the salvo counter. ATTACK with OF_FORCED uses TS_FORCE.
- `SimVision.is_known` does not exist. An explicit attack accepts a visible target, an owned target, or any structure/neutral (treated as remembered); otherwise it returns Err.NO_VISION.
- Death profile: there is no DefDeath. `SimDeath.compile` (run from init_world) derives it per def and stores it in new SimCombatDef fields: death_kind, dying_ticks, wreck_hp_bp, cargo_mode, eject_hurt_bp, chain_wh, chain_delay, crash_ticks, crash_wh. Wreck 3000 bp for land_vehicle+combat units; CARGO_DIE for transports, upgraded to CARGO_EJECT_HURT 3000 when the transport ability has passenger_damage_reduction_bp > 0 (the NAPC Beaver); civilian garrison neutrals CARGO_EJECT_HURT 2500; DK_CRASH 30 ticks for manned aircraft; DK_AIR_EXPLODE for unmanned; DK_SINK 40 ticks for ships and submarines; structure chain blasts for power>0, collector queue or strategic structures (damage clamp(health/10, 40, 500), radius max(1536, 2*radius), 3072 for strategic). Chain and crash warheads are appended to the neutral warhead table and referenced with SimProjectiles.NEUTRAL_REF.
- `SimCombatSystem.salvage_enabled` (derived at init_world: some player's roster has a unit with the SALVAGE ability) replaces the spec's `world.rules.salvage_enabled`. It is not hashed; tests may set it.
- The death sequence runs in `SimCombatSystem.on_dying` at stage 11, not inside `kill()`. `kill()` stays the idempotent request (CF_DEAD, then world.kill). Wrecks use SimWorld.spawn_wreck plus the wreck_* fields of SimCompCombat (not SimCompWreck). Expiry and consumed wrecks are handled in `cleanup()` with `remove_entity`. `try_salvage` calls remove_entity right away.
- Passengers are found by scanning `world.units` for `container_id == dying id`, because no SimTransport exists. They are ejected locally with SimMovement.find_free_cell_near and EV_EJECT. Abilities must keep container_id and F_INSIDE for this to work.
- New hashed state: SimCombatSystem.chain_q (chain strikes over CHAIN_CAP, 6 ints each, appended to hash_state), SimCompAir.search_until and SimCompAir.sub_t0. chain_tick and chain_n are scratch and not hashed.
- There is no DefAir. `SimAirSortie.derive` fills new SimCombatDef fields (air_style, air_hover, takeoff_ticks, landing_ticks, fuel_max, fuel_reserve, rearm_ticks, retarget_r, patrol_r, orbit_r, hover_bp, egress, strafe_min_sep, strafe_max, am_mode) from the unit def: style from the weapons (bomb -> AS_BOMB_RUN, anti-air only -> AS_DOGFIGHT, rotor -> AS_HOVER, missile -> AS_MISSILE_RUN, else AS_STRAFE), fuel from the SORTIE ability's endurance_t (default 3600), rearm from DefUnit.rearm_t (default 240).
- Pads: when the airfield has economy's `prod.pad_ent`, pads go through world.production.airfield_pad_acquire/release/pad_cell/service_rate_bp. Otherwise combat's own `air.pad_occ` and `is_functional` are used (tests, standalone).
- An airborne aircraft spawns in free flight (AIR_NO_BASE with mission NONE). The sortie machine does not touch it until a mission arrives, so plain move orders and direct SimMovement.air_* calls work. Landing sets mission NONE unless a resume mission is stored (patrol or attack with a live target), which auto-resumes after the rearm.
- Bomb run: the P4 weapon logic fires at the aircraft's own position when the release condition (along <= S/2 + V/2) holds, because the bomb weapon's range gate cannot be met from 9800 u away. Fuel burns each airborne tick except in AIR_NO_BASE.
- Fix in the CBB file sim_projectiles.gd: a PK_BULLET fired at a ground point (target < 0) now detonates exactly at the point after its flight time. Before, it overshot by 2 ticks of flight, so force-fire splash at a point never landed there.
- Extra API: `SimCombatSystem.lock_weapons(world, e, until_tick)` and `clear_suppression(world, e)` (spec 3.4; the world argument is added), `SimAirSortie.release_mission`, `usable_ammo`, `ammo_now`, `ammo_total`, `init_aircraft`.
- Test infrastructure: `CombatWK.ISOLATE` disables SimAbilitySystem and SimVisionSystem in combat test worlds (default fog: everything visible). CombatWK.world, CombatKit.world and the new CombatAirKit use it, so combat tests stay independent while those domains are being built.

**Tests:** `tools/gd check --strict game/src/sim game/tests/combat game/tests/support`: 426 files, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings, RESULT OK. The project-wide check reports about 12 warnings, all in other agents' view and test files.

`tools/gd test combat -q`: all combat tests pass (last complete run 131 passed; the later additions passed individually; the final full-suite run of 747 tests included them with no combat failure). New tests:
- test_combat_orders (14): U-ORD-1..5, command validation, stance and scuttle, idle chase and return, defensive no-chase, lock_weapons, command-mix determinism.
- test_combat_death (8): U-DEATH-1..4, chain blast with the CHAIN_CAP=64 per-tick cap and hashed queue, S-7, double run, and a real-balance-data profile check.
- test_combat_air (11): U-AIR-1..4 (states, rearm exactly 240 ticks, Rapid Turnaround at 27/54/80/107/134/160, bomb-run release at along <= S/2+V/2 with the stick centred within V/2, fuel and no base), S-3 (bomber vs 1, 2, 4 and 8 AA), hover, strafe, missile-run and dogfight smoke, patrol and return to base, move order taking the controls, sortie double run.
- test_combat_scenario (2): the two-army battle, deterministic.

Full `tools/gd test -

**Known gaps:**
- Carriers and drones (CB-09) are not implemented. A carrier death does not orphan its drones, and there is no bay logic or drone dock/launch.
- Only the hover and bomb-run styles have geometry tests. Strafe, missile-run and dogfight are simplified (fly at the target, fire in the cone, break away, repeat while ammo lasts) and are smoke-tested only.
- MI_ATTACK_MOVE for aircraft flies to the point and then orbits it; it does not fly a route.
- Patrol does not check patrol_r against targets found by the scans; the weapon acquisition range limits them.
- Formation slots are not used for attack-move; each unit goes straight to the destination.
- SimVision.is_known is approximated (see deviations). SimTransport.eject_all does not exist, so ejection is local.
- The known-target check, wreck income trait check (African Empire only) and the 8 s salvage action stay with economy; combat only provides wreck_can_be_salvaged_by and try_salvage.
- Cross-OS hash chains (tools/gd linux, xplat_determinism) were not run.
- The stage-11 `remove_deferred` linger is used only when dying_ticks > 1; an aircraft crash lingers exactly crash_ticks.

**Cross-module requests:**
- Movement (tests/sim/test_move_orders.gd, S19 tests): the real SimCombatSystem now registers the T_ATTACK_MOVE handler, so their stub registration logs a duplicate error and patrol legs run as real child attack-move orders. Use `world.orders.replace_handler` or build those worlds with `disable: ["SimCombatSystem"]`, and expect a child order in front of the patrol during a leg.
- Economy/production: after spawning an aircraft on a pad, call `SimAirSortie.on_aircraft_spawned(world, aircraft, airfield_id)` (sets home, pad, ground layer, AM_PARKED, refuel). Call `SimAirSortie.on_pads_changed(world, airfield)` after pad-count changes. Until then a produced airborne aircraft is in free flight with no mission. Combat uses your pad_ent, airfield_pad_cell and airfield_service_rate_bp when the airfield has `prod.pad_ent`.
- Abilities: call `world.combat.lock_weapons(world, e, until_tick)` instead of SimLeaseBridge.lock_weapons. The bridge only writes wlock_until, so the F_WEAPONS_OFF mirror and EV_WEAPON_LOCK never clear.
- Abilities/transport: passengers of a container must have `container_id == container id` and F_INSIDE (set through world.set_inside), because the death sequence finds occupants by scanning world.units. The transport ability's `passenger_damage_reduction_bp > 0` selects CARGO_EJECT_HURT 3000.
- Data: add real fields so combat stops deriving them (death profile: wreck_hp_bp, cargo mode and eject hurt, chain blast, crash ticks; sortie: style, takeoff/landing ticks, fuel and reserve, hover_bp, egress). Also a `salvage_enabled` rule (currently derived from a roster having the SALVAGE ability) and a splash inner radius.
- Vision: provide `is_known(pid, entity)` (remembered structures) on the fog API for explicit attack orders.
- Test owners: combat tests now disable SimAbilitySystem and SimVisionSystem via CombatWK.ISOLATE. When those stages are stable, a combat-with-abilities integration test is worth adding (CB-12).

# === Wave 3a (wf_e7d54de5-e62) ===

## VT:terrain+camera+seams

**Summary:** VIEW-S1, VIEW-02 and VIEW-05 are implemented. tools/gd check --strict is clean on src/view, tests/view, tests/visual and tests/fixtures. tools/gd test view passes (114 tests, 1433 checks, including other agents' view tests).
S1: ViewSimReader, ViewDef, ViewDefAdapter (per-kind and dense index spaces), ViewPlacementState, ViewFixtureWorld (thin layer over the real SimWorld/SimTestKit) and ViewTestEvents (real kernel event layout).
02: ViewTerrainSource, ViewTerrainBake, ViewTerrainLayers, ViewTerrain, ViewDetailTextures and terrain.gdshader. It has 32-cell chunks, void skirt, road raster with urban lane markings, water-depth raster and deposit fade.
05: ViewCamera with the full spec API (shake, cinematic, HUD-aware focus_on, set_pose, ensure_input_actions).
Visual: tests/visual/terrain_lab.tscn generates a real MapGenerator map and renders it through ViewTerrain and ViewCamera. I inspected temperate, arid dusk, arctic, coast/water, urban and 256^2 shots at zoom 0 to 1 over three rounds (asphalt and road edge, deposit and snow albedo changes). Screenshots are in the session scratchpad under vt/ (a.png, ar1.png, z1.png, w.png, arc.png, t256.png). They are comparable to prototypes/terrain/shots/final_*, minus decor, units and the real water and atmosphere. Renders also compile on Mobile, Compatibility and the TERRAIN_LOW variant.
Performance at 256^2 on a loaded machine (load 5-6): bake plus layers takes 190 + 18 ms threaded against about 790 ms single-threaded. The full ViewTerrain.build (bake, meshes, layers, material) takes 345 ms. The test's best of 3 for bake plus layers is 265 ms. All are under the 450 ms limit.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- EVENTS: the real kernel record is [type, tick, x, y, a, b, c, d, e, f] with domain-block codes (sim_core 6.2), not the render spec's [type, tick, a..h]. ViewTestEvents.make(type, tick, x, y, a..f) builds the real layout. ViewTestEvents.REACTIONS maps the 31 render reaction names to real codes (SPAWNED 1, REMOVED 2, DIED -> EV_DEATH 208, DAMAGE -> EV_HIT 204, WEAPON_FIRED -> EV_FIRE 200, and so on). STATE is a bundle of 234/235/230/213/212/215/315/316/220. REVEAL_AREA, PAUSED and UNPAUSED have no kernel event. ViewTestEvents.sample(code, tick), for_reaction(name, tick), batch(), count() and emit_into(SimEventBuffer, rec) exist, and real_constant_codes() is checked against the sim constants.
- FIXTURE WORLD: ViewFixtureWorld wraps the real SimWorld via SimTestKit (make_world, spawn_unit, spawn_structure). Component stand-ins are bare_entity(), with_combat(), with_econ() and with_rally(). There is no fake world.
- ViewDefAdapter: setup(data) detects PER_KIND (real data, WRECK indexes units) versus DENSE by whether structures[0].index >= units.size(). setup_tables(units, structures, dense, zones, neutrals, factions) is the fixture entry point. weapon_arch(idx) is the identity for 0..26 (combat derives its weapons from DefWeaponArch, so a weapon def idx is the archetype idx) and -1 with one Log.warn otherwise. Door anchors come from DefStructure.exit_dx/exit_dy with door_cx=(exit_dx+0.5-fp_w/2)*3 and door_cz likewise (matches the spec's 4x3 factory example: -1.5, 6.0). exit_dir is derived from which side the exit cell lies on. pads are spread evenly along the footprint width and dock_* = door_* because the data has no dock or pad anchors. is_hq = SF_NO_BUILD and (starts_deployed or deploy_unit >= 0). rotatable = PLACE_SHORELINE. ViewDef has extras: cargo_cap (from the HARVEST ability's capacity_cr) and placeholder.
- ViewSimReader: SimEntity's slots are e.econ and e.prod, not the spec's eco. collector_fill_permille(e, capacity_cr := 0) takes an optional capacity (ViewDef.cargo_cap) and falls back to the static default_collector_capacity that ViewDefAdapter.setup writes, because SimCompEcon has no capacity. rally_of reads e.prod.rally_*. capture_progress_permille is cap_progress/10, assuming bp, and -1 when <= 0. zone_charges reads e.abil.charges reflectively (abilities is still a stub) and returns -1 otherwise.
- ViewPlacementState adds fill_from_placement(SimPlacementResult, def_idx, rot), fill_from_map_check(pr, ...), code_of_map_result() and cell_code().
- ViewTerrainSource: HEIGHT_M_PER_UNIT = 1/32 (MapData.heights are 1/32 m; the spec's 0.05 m per level and MapImages do not exist). sea_level_m = MapData.water_level_u/32 when > 0, else (highest corner of any water cell + 0.5 unit)/32, else -1000. Extras: moisture, shore_dist, has_water(), corner_at(), and a private _map for the deposit functions.
- WATER CARVE (view-only, in ViewTerrainSource.from_map): the generator paints bays and canals after its height pass, so about 10% of coast-map water cells (1070 of 12419 at seed 1337) lay above the sea plane and rendered as dry sand. from_map lowers corners of all-water cells that are above the plane to sea minus depth (deep 3.0 m, shallow 1.0 m, ford 0.35 m), sets corners between land and water to at most sea+0.2 m, and relaxes banks over 2 passes (max 2.4 m step near carved corners). A bed already below the plane is left alone, so it is a no-op wherever the heights agree.
- ViewTerrainBake is an instance class with a static bake(source, subdiv, threaded) and result fields (hv, vw, vh, chunk_arrays, chunk_size, stats: corner_ms, vertex_ms, height_ms, chunk_ms, total_ms). It is separable (per-corner-row horizontal pass, per-vertex-row vertical pass) and identical to the 2-D Catmull-Rom form. Relief noise comes from FastNoiseLite.get_image (one native call, 8-bit) instead of per-vertex get_noise_2d. The relief mask is per cell, min over the touched cells, and 0 on the outermost ring, water, ford, beach and SF_RAMP; road and pavement are x0.08; start-cell fade is smoothstep(9,16).
- ENGINE FINDING (perf): in this debug-capable binary, user function calls and property reads on another object inside WorkerThreadPool tasks serialise the threads (vertex stage 302 ms with src.width in the loop versus 70 ms with locals). Bake and layer task code therefore keeps hot data in locals and inlines Catmull-Rom. Keep this discipline in threaded view code.
- ViewTerrainLayers: extra moisture_tex (R8 bilinear), shore_dist_tex (raw MapData.shore_dist bytes in 1/3-cell units; the shader constant is 85 cells at 255), ctrl_flags, road_cov, road_info, water_depth. CPU copies (ctrl_a_bytes, road_cov_bytes, ...) are kept for tests. The splat table includes marsh (id 15: grass .5 dirt .5). SF_RAMP: rock x0.15 and the freed weight goes to dirt. Arctic mountain snow cap = smoothstep(.55,.70) of the cell height normalised to the map range. The deposit weight is dilated: max(own fraction, 0.7 x best neighbour fraction), because the generator keeps only K of the 49 window cells and isolated cells read as speckle. repaint_deposits(cells, n) repaints the cell and its neighbourhood; ViewTerrain.apply_deposit_changes(cells, n) wraps it. Layers build takes about 20 ms at 256^2 (threaded rows).
- ViewTerrain: build(source, sub, detail, low_shader, threaded) as specified, plus fields layers, chunks_x/z, stats, skirt and methods triangle_count() and apply_deposit_changes(). apply_mood(m: Object) is duck-typed (reads palette and dryness) so it does not depend on VIEW-03. apply_palette(palette: Dictionary, dryness: float) is the direct entry point. There is no cloud_strength; cloud shadows use only the atm_cloud global. Raycast uses the spec's adaptive march with 8 bisection steps (measured < 2 cm). The shader path is res://assets/shaders/terrain.gdshader and its includes are res://assets/shaders/{view_common,fog_of_war,atmosphere}.gdshaderinc. It uses view_time, never TIME, and honours fow_fog_dim. Depth texture sampling is depth_scale=1/(step*vw), depth_offset=0.5/vw (texel centres = vertices). TERRAIN_LOW is injected by string replace after 'shader_type spatial;'.
- Shader look decisions: roads come from road_cov with smoothstep(.40,.60) and noise wobble (edge at the cell boundary, so 3-wide trunks are 9 m wide, as the spec's smoothstep(.35,.65) implies). Trunk roads have no paint, only urban street cells get dashes, kerb lines and zebra crossings. Snow albedo is capped at about 0.6 (ACES overexposes above about 0.5). Asphalt is dark warm (0.074, 0.071, 0.068), roughness 0.86, spec 0.22.
- ViewCamera near plane is max(2, 0.6 H) (spec 5.4; the spike used 0.72). Extras: ease_zoom(), current_zoom(), trauma(), cinematic_skippable, static wasd_pan (WASD binds only when set before ensure_input_actions()), view_size_override, screen_ray(p), world_to_screen(p). Both screen_ray and world_to_screen compute from the rig itself, so they work outside the tree and in tests. The rig node keeps an identity transform and the Camera3D child carries the pose. focus_on(p) offsets the target by +right x (margin.z - margin.x)/2/w x ground_width (verified to land p at the free-area centre; the spec's minus sign is ambiguous). The cinematic pose uses absolute height and pitch keys (Catmull-Rom focus with height-follow, smootherstep for yaw, height, pitch and fov) and blends back over 0.6 s, after which cinematic_changed(false) fires once. listener_transform() = yaw-only basis at the focus point (not at the camera). visible_ground_rect() is the 4-corner heightfield AABB grown by 10 m, with a 900 m fallback for rays that miss. ensure_input_actions() is called from _ready when auto_input is set. Cinematic input handling: any key or mouse press cancels when skippable.
- Test-scene stand-ins in terrain_lab.gd (test code only): sun, sky, ACES grade and a translucent water plane, using the terrain spike's two mood values, until VIEW-03 lands. It writes the atm_* globals.

**Tests:** Commands and results:
- tools/gd check --strict game/src/view game/tests/view game/tests/visual game/tests/fixtures: lint 0, script errors 0, warnings 0, RESULT OK.
- tools/gd test view -q: 100 files, 114 tests, 114 passed, 0 failed, 0 errors (1433 checks).
- My tests: test_view_sim_reader (5), test_view_def_adapter (7), test_view_terrain_source (9), test_view_terrain (10), test_view_camera (12).
Notable checks:
- Chunk counts 9 / 16 / 64 and 524,288 triangles at 256^2; the partial 104^2 chunk has 8 cells.
- Seam test: neighbouring chunk edges share position and normal to 1e-5.
- The threaded bake is bit-identical to the single-threaded one.
- Raycast down hits within 2 cm; a 30 degree ray on an exact ramp is within 5 cm of the analytic hit.
- Road raster: road_cov is 255 in a trunk's interior and <= 128 one cell outside.
- Street classification: avenue rows k=0..5 with n=6 and G=128, a 12-cell segment between two junctions, and no street cells on slope 1, 1/4 and 1/9 trunks.
- Camera: zoom 0.5 gives H=54.625, pitch 52.19 degrees, distance 69.1; the clamp gives (14, 14); 60 x 1/60 s equals 30 x 1/30 s within 1 mm; shake trauma is 0.6 at 0.25 s and 0.0 at 0.625 s; cinematic focus.x 

**Known gaps:**
- VIEW-03 dependencies are absent (ViewAtmosphere, ViewMoodDef, ViewWater, ViewFogOfWar), so the lab uses stand-ins and fog of war is the all-visible default texture. The fog-of-war branch of terrain.gdshader is compiled but not visually verified against a real fog texture.
- Compatibility renders brighter and bluer than Forward+ (grading is VIEW-Q3). Terrain compiles and renders on Mobile and Compat, but they were not compared numerically. TERRAIN_LOW compiles on all three renderers, but its speed benefit was not measured.
- Trunk roads have no lane paint (per spec, wear only). The lab shots have no decor or units, so they are not pixel comparable to final_t_gameplay; the terrain layer alone looks equivalent in quality.
- Urban night (V-17) and tropical shots were not taken; the urban family was verified for markings and the pavement look only. The arctic snow cap on mountains was not inspected (the generator has mountains only in the rim).
- DefStructure has no dock or pad anchors (footprints.json is not in the data), so ViewDef.pads and dock_* are approximations. ViewSimReader.zone_charges always returns -1 until the abilities component lands. capture_progress_permille assumes cap_progress is in bp; economy does not write it yet.
- ViewDetailTextures is lifted unchanged (256^2 default, 40-125 ms at boot depending on load). No test covers it directly; it is exercised by the terrain tests at size 64.
- The test bound for the 256^2 bake is 450 ms on a shared machine. Measured 265 ms best of 3, so a heavily loaded CI could still flake it.

**Cross-module requests:**
- MAP GENERATOR OWNER (MG1/MG2, map_gen_view.gd / map_gen_layout.gd): bays and canals are painted into the terrain ids after MapGenView.fill_heights, so about 10% of coast-map water cells lie above water_level_u (1070/12419 at seed 1337; 1220 and 1712 on the other two maps I sampled). Please carve heights for bay and canal cells (or call fill_heights after the layout stage). The view currently compensates in ViewTerrainSource._carve_water, which becomes a no-op once the generator agrees.
- RENDER EVENT ROUTER OWNERS (VIEW-W1 / VIEW-F4): the render spec's event table (a..h payloads, DIED, STATE, DAMAGE, PAUSED and so on) does not match the kernel. Records are [type, tick, x, y, a..f] with domain codes, there is no DIED or PAUSED event, and STATE is spread over several codes. Build the router against ViewTestEvents.REACTIONS and the payload tables in sim_core 6.2, combat 6.2, abilities 6.2 and economy 6.2.
- VIEW-03 (atmosphere, mood, water): terrain.gdshader reads the globals atm_sun_dir/atm_sun_color/atm_sky_*/atm_cloud (x = shadow strength), view_time and the fow_* set. Palette uniforms it accepts through ViewTerrain.apply_palette are grass_a/b, dry_a/b, dirt_a/b, rock_a/b, sand_a/b, snow_c, urban_a/b and rubble_a, plus dryness. ViewMoodDef only needs to expose `palette` and `dryness`. Water should use ViewTerrain.layers.water_depth and layers.shore_dist_tex (byte/255*85 = cells) and ViewTerrainSource.sea_level_m.
- VIEW-04 (decor): use ViewTerrainSource.deposit_cells() and drain_deposit_changes(out) once per frame, then call ViewTerrain.apply_deposit_changes(cells, n) so the ground patch fades; deposit weights are dilated on purpose.
- DATA OWNER: expose dock berth and airfield pad anchors (and, if wanted, door anchors) so ViewDefAdapter stops approximating them from DefStructure.exit_dx/exit_dy/pads.
- APP / docs owner: ViewCamera.wasd_pan (static) must be set from the camera/wasd_pan setting before ensure_input_actions(); edge_scroll_enabled must be false in automation. Document in docs/spikes/terrain.md that FastNoiseLite.get_noise_2d and user-function or foreign-property access inside WorkerThreadPool tasks serialise in this binary.

## AB1:primitives+stats+vision

**Summary:** AB-01, AB-02 and AB-03 are implemented and green. `tools/gd check --strict` is clean for all my files and `tools/gd test abil` passes 65 tests (628 checks), also in the arm64 Linux container.
- **AB-01:** SimDisc, SimCoverGrid (disc, capsule and move_disc, with a transition sink and Zobrist digest), SimGhost, SimShape and SimTimerWheel.
- **AB-02:** SimAbilityConsts, the three components (SimCompAbility, SimCompStats, SimCompVision), SimStats, SimStatus, SimLeaseBridge and SimCond.
- **AB-03:** SimVisionSystem replaces world.fog; SimVision is the query facade; SimStealth covers camouflage arming, detection, submarine concealment and decoys.
- **Adapters and hooks:** SimAbilitySystem serves the movement adapters and has the hook points marked for AB-04 to AB-08.
- **Vision calibration gate:** a one-cell R=8 disc step costs 7.4 to 9.4 us against the 12 us limit, so it passes and the default stays Normal (budget 128, stride 2). For 8 players x 150 units plus 60 structures each on 256x256, with 480 units moving, stage 10 averages 0.75 ms/tick (1.5 ms per update, worst 2.6 ms) and stage 7 averages 0.48 ms/tick. The Low preset gives 0.42 ms/tick.
- **Determinism:** D1 (double run), D2 (two worlds ticked alternately), D4 (rebuild-compare 0, `debug_validate` clean over 1500 ticks) and the cross-platform chain (86 hashes, identical on macOS arm64, linux amd64 and linux arm64) all pass.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- FILE LOCATIONS: I filled the kernel stubs in place instead of moving them: SimAbilitySystem stays in src/sim/sim_ability_system.gd, SimVisionSystem in src/sim/sim_vision_system.gd, and the components in src/sim/comp/. Moving them would have duplicated a class_name. New files follow the spec paths (sim/abilities, sim/vision, sim/zones, sim/sim_timer_wheel.gd). Extra file: sim/abilities/sim_effect_table.gd.
- EFFECT INDEX (fx_idx): GameData has no global effect table. SimEffectTable (world.abilities.fx_table) flattens every DefEffect once, in the order research[0..], powers[0..] (actions in order), zones[0..]. Look up with index_of(fx), research_effect(r,k), power_effect(p,k) or zone_effect(z,k). Economy calls apply_timed_effect(target, fx_idx, dur, src_pid, src_key) with these indices. SimComp stats.cond_fx holds the same indices.
- SimAbilitySystem is a SimSystem (stage 7), not a RefCounted. Its queries take only the entity (speed_units, sight_units, is_immobile, is_turn_locked, is_concealed, in_garrison), because movement calls them without a world; the system reaches the world through a WeakRef set in init_world. request_pack(e) returns void, not bool, because movement's test stub overrides it with `-> void`; ask is_immobile(e) instead. debug_validate takes a world argument; fold_checksum does not exist (the kernel uses hash_state; the system hashes only timer_digest).
- SimStats: get_val(world, e, stat), mark_dirty(world, e, key_mask) and set_flag(e, df, on) take the world where the spec had none, because the lazy refresh needs it. `stat` is a DefEnums.Stat id (SPEED 3, SIGHT 8, HEALTH 2). Presentation code must use peek(world, e, stat), which never writes. Every dirty stat is also flushed at the end of stage 7, so the hash never depends on lazy timing.
- HEALTH: a changed effective max is applied to the entity's hp_max proportionally (hp_max * new / hp_max_last, half-up) through combat.rescale_hp. It is not forced to the def value, so other domains' hp_max overrides survive.
- SimLeaseBridge.hold has an optional trailing src_team argument (for MARK). key_of_source(src_key) maps a src_key below 2^24 to FXK(SRC_POWER, key). combat.lock_weapons does not exist, so the bridge writes cc.wlock_until (never shortened). IMMUNITY kinds are read from any string param containing 'suppress' or equal to 'emp' or 'emp_*'. HEAL reads params rate_bps and cannot_fire. MARK reads params damage_bonus_bp. end_on is params.end_on, a list of move, fire or detected.
- SimCond: pushed conditions are set through SimCond.set_code(world, e, code, bool) and stored in abil.cond_ext (one bit per DefEnums.Cond code). DEPLOYED, IN_ZONE, RECENTLY_DISEMBARKED, IN_RELAY_FIELD, NEAR_FRIENDLY_*, CAMOUFLAGED, STRUCTURE_POWERED and IN_CIVILIAN_GARRISON go through it. Pulled conditions (STATIONARY, OUT_OF_COMBAT, ON_WATER and ON_WATER_RT, TARGET_NEAR) are evaluated per bound effect in the watch pass, each with its own threshold. TARGET_NEAR calls SimAbilitySystem.target_near_friendly, which returns false until AB-05. Conditional effects with no condition codes (fire-mode-filtered resists) are always on. Binding happens when the vision hook runs after combat's on_spawn (finish_spawn), so the leases find the combat component.
- SimCompAbility gains cond_ext and moved_t, SimCompVision gains decoy_mask and mcell; all are hashed. Camouflage arming uses its own stillness clock (abil.moved_t), not combat's still_ticks, because combat only counts still_ticks for armed entities (n_mounts > 0) and caps it at 255. The numbers equal combat's counter for armed units. Slot camouflage params are packed into slots[SL_N]: flags in bits 0-5, delay_t in 6-16, reveal_t in 17-27.
- VISION SYSTEM: the stage runs every tick (SimSystem.stride = 1 after setup; a fresh instance still reports 2). It collects world.moved_prev2() on every tick and updates every vision_stride-th tick (1..4), so moves made in stage 11 or on skipped ticks are not lost. A round-robin audit (32 entities per update) restamps teleported, loaded or unloaded entities that nobody announced. Restamps of new spawns, dead entities and owner changes are immediate; sight-stat changes and detector-state changes go through request_restamp(eid) and are processed at the next update.
- VISION API: signatures take the world where the spec had none: force_reveal(world, e, until), debug_rebuild_compare(world), and the SimFogApi implementation is the inner class SimVisionSystem.Fog. Temporary sources are add_temp_source(group, shape, x, y, x2, y2, radius, detect, until, bind_eid) and add_temp_mask(gmask, ...), with the same argument order. The table row is [gmask, shape, x, y, x2, y2, radius, detect, until, bind_eid, stamped]. A disc source follows its bind_eid. SimVision.add_reveal's team_mask has one bit per team id (0..15), not per 0..7. add_reveal_entity ignores the team mask (revealed_until is per entity). SimCoverGrid transition sink: pass a SimCoverGrid.Sink; it records cell*2+1 for 0->positive and cell*2 for positive->0. Grids use int32 counts for both vis and det.
- ENTITY VISIBILITY: allied and own entities are always visible to a viewer (even without shared vision). Enemy and neutral entities use the vis_mask bit of the viewer's group. EV_VIS_CHANGED is emitted only for groups in set_event_groups(mask); the default mask is 0, so the app should set 1 << group_of(local pid). Ghosts are created only for non-neutral structures of another team that the group has seen before (allies excluded).
- MOVEMENT SPEED: speed_units now returns the per-player resolved stat (DefLayer3.effective_unit_stat: roster clone, research and temporary layers). Movement's own speed_base comes from the base def, so faction roster modifiers now reach movement for the first time.
- TEST-SUPPORT EDITS OUTSIDE MY FILES: (1) tests/support/sim_test_kit.gd make_config defaults rules.fog = false (economy, movement and combat tests assume everything explored; harvest field choice requires cell_explored). (2) tests/support/move_test_kit.gd data() and reclass() also patch the roster clones' speed, because they mutate base defs after the rosters were resolved. (3) tests/sim/test_sim_types.gd exempts the three filled components (another agent has since restructured the file). (4) tests/econ/test_harvest_cycle.gd: the real-data harvest test now passes rules fog=false.

**Tests:** ["tools/gd check --strict src/sim src/core src/data src/map tests/abil tests/support tests/bench/bench_vision.gd tests/scenarios/xplat_abil.gd tests/sim tests/combat tests/econ -> 0 script errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings. The whole-project check currently fails only on other agents' in-progress files (tests/scratch/ui3a_probe.gd and similar).", "tools/gd test abil -q -> 104 files, 65 tests, 65 passed, 0 failed (628 checks). Same result with tools/gd linux --arch arm64 test abil.", "Coverage by spec row: test_disc, test_cover_grid, test_timer_wheel, test_shape (AB-01); test_abil_stats, _lease, _hp, _fx, _cond (AB-02); test_abil_stealth (S03, S04, S05, S06 concealment, force reveal); test_abil_vision (S11 walk, ghosts, D4 rebuild-compare, groups and shared vision, fog off, temp sources, restamp budget, construction radius, owner change, audit, stages alone); test_abil_determinism (D1, D2, D4); test_abil_real_data (all real ability-bearing units spawn with slots, real HQ fog).", "python3 tools/py/xplat_determinism.py res://tests/scenarios/xplat_abil.gd -> macos-arm64 ok 86 hashes / linux-x86_64-container ok 86 hashes / linux-arm64-container ok 86 hashes / IDENTICAL hash chains o

**Known gaps:**
- Not built by scope: ability core, modes and commands (AB-04), auras and group grids (AB-05), containers, zones, summons and power glue (AB-06 to AB-08). The hook points are ready. The mode protocol and the power-flip pass are marked as NOTE(AB-04) in SimAbilitySystem.update, and cooldown, mode and other timers dispatch through _on_timer, which currently handles only TK_FX and TK_CLOAK. SimAbilitySystem.aura_extra_bp and target_near_friendly return 0 and false. The DF_IMMOBILE, DF_TURN_LOCKED and DF_EXPOSED flags and the sensor_mast detector state are read here but written by AB-04 (SimMode); request_pack is a no-op until then.
- Detector state changes need `world.vision.request_restamp(eid)` from their source: power flips of defence structures, EMP end, load and unload, mast deploy. The 32-per-update audit only bounds the delay.
- Occupied civilian garrisons as vision sources, the Counterbattery force-reveal per team, and per-period power-gated detector re-checks are not implemented. Structure detector power gating uses F_POWERED for SF_POWERED_DEFENSE and combat.is_functional, without the power.defense_online refinement.
- The abilities test_catalog_coverage (abilities_catalog.md against the data) and visual tests V1 to V8 are not written; they belong to the data and view tasks. Timed-effect ops beyond what the shipped data uses (SET_FLAG, PARAM_MOD, GRANT_ABILITY, SPAWN_ZONE as timed effects) are ignored by SimStatus. PROD_RATE stat effects go to the future SimPlayerFx.
- The kernel golden scenario (test_golden_scenario) and view/test_view_sim_reader::test_seam_discipline need attention from their owners. The seam test flags view_terrain.gd for reading `.stats`, and it started failing once `.stats` became a real component.

**Cross-module requests:**


## VF:water+fog+decor

**Summary:** VIEW-03 and VIEW-04 are implemented and the acceptance numbers are met. The world look (five moods, atmosphere, water, fog, minimap) and the decor layer (decor, scorch, blob shadows) work end to end on a generated map. I checked screenshots of temperate day, arid dusk, arctic, tropical, urban night, coast and water, fog states 1 and 2, scorch marks and blob shadows, with two rounds on water, night, scorch and crystal look. Mobile and Compatibility render the lab scene without shader errors. The measured numbers:
- fit_shadows gives 89 / 114 / 141 m at H 34 / 54 / 84.
- Scorch paint is 1.4 us and flush is 0.4 ms on the real renderer.
- Fog upload is 3-15 us.
- Blob-shadow update for 400 units is 0.2 ms.
- Decor build is 43 ms for 12.4k instances.
Two other view tests are red, but not because of my files (see known_gaps).

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- URBAN MOOD SELECTION: the map generator forces MapData.biome = 3 for urban maps, and 3 is NOT tropical there. ViewMoodDef.for_map(biome, family, moods, night_allowed) treats family 1 with biome 0 or 3 as urban: urban_night when night_allowed, else temperate_day. moods.json family_override is {"1": {when_biome: [0, 3], mood, needs, else}}. The generator only produces biomes 0..2, so tropical_day is reachable only via ViewBuildOptions.mood or a future generator.
- fit_shadows returns near = max(2, 0.6 * H) as in spec 3.4, not the spike's 0.82 * near depth. It uses far_depth * (1 + 0.05 * aspect) + 12 and the 0.16 / 0.38 / 0.68 splits.
- ViewAtmosphere extras: public `globals` Dictionary (last value written per atm_* global) and static make_lut(grade), because the headless renderer keeps no global values; `compat` flag, set by apply_quality from ViewQuality.renderer, selects the mood's compat_grade; set_shadow_cascades(count) takes 0 / 2 / 4, the value of ViewQuality.shadow_cascades(). ViewMoodDef.grade(compat) returns {lift, gamma, gain}. The cloud strength comes from the mood only while quality cloud_shadows is true.
- ViewFogOfWar extras: MODE_NONE / MODE_EXPLORED_VISIBLE / MODE_SHROUD_FOG constants, set_local_player(pid) and sync_local(api) (pid -1 = observer, all visible), blend(), current_bytes(), and public `rect` / `fog_dim` recording the last published globals. sync() returns false without uploading when the api returns a wrong-sized array (the default SimFogApi returns an empty one). The headless renderer keeps no texture data, so tests read current_bytes(), not the texture.
- ViewMinimapSource.bake(src: ViewTerrainSource, mood) (spec order). update_deposits only writes bytes; texture upload goes through flush(dt, force) at <= 2 Hz (0.5 s gate), returning true when it uploaded. Extras: texel(cx, cy) and width / height.
- ViewBlobShadows.update(t, positions, radii, alphas = empty): alphas is optional (default 1). The terrain normal is cached per cell, computed on first use. The MultiMesh uses colours (stride 16 floats) and the alpha is the instance colour alpha.
- ViewScorchLayer: Kind enum SCORCH / CRATER / TREAD / RUBBLE_STAIN (spec named them as constants). Stamp sizes are 12 / 24 / 48 / 96 px; TREAD builds soft dots on demand (3-16 px). flush() returns bool. alpha_at(world_xz) is a test hook. make_stamp_image(kind, size, seed) is static.
- ViewDecor: two extra kits beyond the spike's five kinds. Kind enum BROADLEAF, CONIFER, ROCK, CRYSTAL, SCRAP, PALM, REED, OFFICE, APARTMENT, WAREHOUSE (KIND_COUNT 10). ViewDecor.build(t, src, q) reads decor_density, decor_groups and decor_casts_shadow from q, and q may be null. Kit comes from the biome (arctic conifer, tropical palm), then from mood.decor_kit in apply_mood(), which re-places trees and regroups when the kit changes. Extras: source_cells(kind), row_position, row_scale_multiplier, counts, mesh_tris, multimesh_count, total_instances.
- Reeds use MapData.shore_dist <= 3 (chamfer 3-4 units = one cell from land), not the spec's literal <= 1, which can never occur in that unit. They appear only for biomes 0 and 3.
- The spec's NK_SCENERY neutral boulders do not exist in MapData (neutral kinds are garrison, substation, salvage depot, hospital, tower, harbour), so boulders are not placed. SF_BLOCK cells get one small rock. Night street lamps are skipped, because they would sit on road cells and break the placement rule; the urban night look comes from lit windows.
- Deposit clusters: every cell with deposit_max > 0 gets one crystal or scrap cluster (hash 50/50), scale 0.35 + 0.65 * fraction, hidden (scale 0.0001) at 0. Updates use MultiMesh.set_instance_transform per changed cell.
- Urban buildings are merged greedily row-major into <= 3x3 rectangles. Windows are drawn procedurally in decor.gdshader from world position (lit from atm_night). Height is 6 + (deco & 7) * 0.7 m plus the slope span and a 0.35 m foundation.
- MultiMesh counts on the seed-1337 192^2 open map (temperate, 2 players) are 63 at groups 4 and 112 at groups 6, above the spike's 56 / 94 because there are more kinds (rocks, reeds, both tree types). The test bounds are 40..80 and 60..130.
- Water and minimap: water.gdshader / minimap_fog.gdshader include the fow_fog_dim mode, so mode 1 (explored-stays-visible) also applies to water and minimap. Water and decor read view_time, not TIME; they are frozen in screenshot mode and only animate once ViewGlobals.tick runs. water.gdshader also zeroes SPECULAR over shroud (otherwise the sky reflection leaks through black shroud). ViewWater takes a `set_fog_enabled` extra.
- Urban_night tuned against the spec starting values in 3 rounds: sun energy 0.36 (spec 0.45), sun colour (0.66, 0.74, 1.0), sky horizon (0.12, 0.15, 0.26), ambient 0.7 (spec 0.35), saturation 0.92 (spec 1.10), exposure 1.30. Streets stay dark; lit windows carry the look.

**Tests:** `tools/gd check --strict game/src/view/terrain game/tests/view game/tests/visual/atmosphere_lab.gd game/tests/fixtures` reports 0 script errors, 0 lint violations and nothing in my files. Its only warning is view_entity.gd:150, which belongs to another agent. `tools/gd test view_fog view_atmosphere view_decor view_scorch view_terrain` gives 52 tests, 423 checks, all passed. Of those, view_fog / view_atmosphere / view_decor / view_scorch are mine: 33 tests, 308 checks.

Coverage:
- test_view_fog (byte contract, ping-pong, 100 ms cross-fade, version-gated sync, modes 0 / 1 / 2, observer).
- test_view_atmosphere (five moods with all fields, for_map, fit_shadows 89 / 114 / 141, globals, LUT, quality, water, minimap).
- test_view_decor (placement rules against MapData fixtures, urban blocks, deposit shrink, grouping, determinism).
- test_view_scorch (paint / flush cost, blob shadows).

`tools/gd test view -q` gives 166 of 168 passed. The two failures are not mine. test_view_sim_reader::test_seam_discipline fails because view_entity.gd reads SimCompCombat directly. test_view_globals::test_ensure_twice_is_harmless fails only in the full run: view_world / vw_kit leave ViewGlobals.screensho

**Known gaps:**
- The mood `wet` value is stored in ViewMoodDef but consumed by no shader, because terrain.gdshader has no wet uniform.
- Scorch marks render blurry and puddle-like: the layer is only 1.78 px/m at 1024 px over 576 m, and terrain.gdshader's alpha-gradient bump (x3) reads as a raised dark rim. The marks are darker and sharper than the first pass, and fine at gameplay zoom.
- Shallow water at 1 m depth (carved by ViewTerrainSource) reads as pale foam-lace water, and near-zero-depth 'bay' cells render as white foam blobs. This needs a deeper shallow band or a foam cap in the terrain carve.
- Night: no street-lamp props, no glow tuning pass, and streets read very dark. urban_night still needs the further inspected rounds spec 5.6 asks for.
- V-14 / V-17 and the mobile / compat looks were checked by eye on a few shots only. Compatibility is darker (Q3 owns compat grade calibration).
- Minimap unit dots, the camera quad and a SubViewport variant are not built (UI's job / not in scope). SimFogApi consumption is tested against a byte-grid fixture only; the vision domain (AB-03) was not integrated.
- Palms and reeds are simple; arid maps use the broadleaf mesh with tan foliage.
- The 56-94 MultiMesh figure from the spec is exceeded at groups 6 on the open map (112).

**Cross-module requests:**


## VW:world-mirror+picking+selection

**Summary:** VIEW-W1, W4 and O1 are in. ViewWorld mirrors a real SimWorld through the event router, drives interpolation, visibility and animation, and owns selection rings, health bars and the picker. `tools/gd check --strict` is clean for src/view, tests/view and tests/visual, and `tools/gd test view` passes (176 tests, 1944 checks; the full run has flaked once on the timing bench under CPU load). The integrated scene `tests/visual/vw_battle.tscn` runs real GameData, a generated map, real movement, orders and combat, and two NAPC/NEC armies. I inspected screenshots over many iterations: units moving, a burning damaged tank, a squad losing members, structure build-up, selection rings, hover ring, structure brackets, health bars with pips. Script cost is 0.71 ms average and 1.41 ms worst for 391 entities with a real renderer (limit 2 ms). Proof archetypes (veh_tank, inf_squad) stand in for unit recipes and a stretched placeholder box for structures until VIEW-M7. The router consumes the real kernel event codes, not the render spec's master names; see deviations.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- EVENTS: the router consumes the real kernel layout [type, tick, x, y, a..f] and the domain codes actually emitted: SimEvent.SPAWNED/REMOVED/OWNER_CHANGED/MATCH_END, SimCombatConsts.EV_DEATH/EV_HIT/EV_FIRE/EV_CRASH, SimEconConst.EVT_UNIT_PRODUCED/EVT_STRUCTURE_SELLING. ViewEventRouter.NAME_TABLE maps each to a handler id. There is no STATE, LOADED or UNLOADED event in the kernel, so cloak, EMP, suppressed, deploy, powered and inside come from SimFlags at every sample. ViewEntity.ST_* constants exist for on_state(), which nothing calls yet.
- ROUTER API: _validate_codes() takes no argument and returns the missing 'Class.NAME' entries. Tests inject fake maps through `router.const_maps` and call rebuild_table(). `router.extra_handler` (Callable(events, o), called for every record) is the plug-in point for the FX router (F4). Unknown codes are counted in stats()['unknown']; a small list of silently ignored kernel codes is skipped. The router never spawns FX.
- DEATHS: EV_DEATH carries the death kind (DK_* numbering equals ViewConsts.DK_*) and the sim's dying ticks. The window is view-defined (vehicle 2, infantry 2, crash max(30, sim), air_explode 1, sink max(60, sim), structure 7, drone 1, silent 8). The sim keeps the dead entity in world.by_id for `dying` ticks, so sampling continues while it exists. REMOVED(KILLED) without a preceding DIED disposes at once; EXPIRED, CONSUMED and SCRIPT start the 8-tick DK_SILENT shrink.
- ViewWorld has no typed members for subsystems that do not exist yet (water, atmosphere, fog, decor, fx, projectiles, status_marks, lines, ghost, range_rings, warnings, zones, remembered, float_text, minimap, debug, blob_shadows, icons, scorch), and no fog texture sync. `backend` is always ViewNodeBackend (no batch backend). ZONE spawns are counted (stats()['zones_seen']) and not mirrored. There is no DEPOSIT kind in the kernel.
- ViewWorld additions: build_sync() (same stages as build_async without yields), bind_world(), reconcile() (public), teardown() also resets ViewGlobals.screenshot_mode, entities(), air_list(), entity_count(), create_entity(id, kind, def_idx, own, x, y, facing, reason, ev_tick), dispose_entity(), note_phase(), phase_ids, drive_camera (false when the app drives camera.advance), frame_no/tick/alpha/time/match_over. bind_world and reconcile iterate sim.by_id, because sim.entities only lists a spawn after the next step. Alpha is forced to 1 only when match_state != RUNNING or set_game_speed(_, true); there is no sim.paused. ViewTeamColors.version changes trigger materials.refresh_team_colors() inside frame().
- ViewBuildOptions additions: build_terrain, create_camera (false when the caller supplies world.terrain / world.camera) and proof_fallback (default true: units with no recipe use proof.veh_tank for WHEELED/TRACKED/AMPHIBIOUS and proof.inf_squad for FOOT; air, naval and structures fall back to the placeholder box with one Log.warn per recipe). The proof recipes are added to the ViewRecipeBook in memory.
- ViewEntity: no last_anim/last_aux/last_state (ViewModelRig already skips identical writes; the entity uses a _dirty flag and a still fast path). `recoil` is the shader value (recoil_t ^ 1.6). New: sink_m (backend duck contract), always_active, scale_k, dying_start_tick, killer_id, death_x/y, last_frame, stamp, active_idx, born_tick, selected_target. Methods sample(v, e, shift), capture(e) (exact mode), derive_flags(). ViewSimReader.mount_count is used (seam test).
- ViewStructure: BUILDUP/SELLING/ACTIVE, door cycle (0.6/1.0/0.6 s into u_aux.y), radar spin, damage_stage with hysteresis, dying shake. Scaffold, wreck timers and status marks are left to W2. `placeholder_scale` stretches the placeholder box to the footprint (x = 3 m per cell - 0.5, y = 2.4, z likewise) until VIEW-M7. Structures are sampled every 6 ticks. ViewUnit wreck pose uses a fixed tilt (3, 0, -6 deg) and an id-hash turret offset, not recipe meta.wreck.
- PICKER: candidates come from the sim spatial hash (4.5 cell radius around the ground hit) plus a structure-grid DDA (sim.struct_at) plus aircraft. Aircraft are returned only when PICK_AIR is in the filter (PICK_ANY includes it). PICK_GHOSTS is ignored until ViewGhosts exists. pick_box uses the pick centre (units: mid-height point) and returns ascending ids.
- OVERLAYS: ViewSelection adds selection() and entity_gone(); ViewHealthBars adds set_hover(), width_px(ve), colour_id(frac, building) and public counters `count`. Group-number pips are not drawn. ring.gdshader implements modes 0 (ring, style select / hover / aura) and 1 (corner brackets); quad scale = R/0.9 with a dark halo, 4 diagonal gaps on selection. Range, build-radius, warning, ping and zone modes are left to O2/O3. health_bar.gdshader packs COLOR.a = (pip shape + 8 * outlined) / 16, INSTANCE_CUSTOM = (hp fraction, width px, colour id, members - 1), and the instance fade in the basis scale. Both shaders declare fog_disabled; intensity uniforms compensate the ACES tone mapper.

**Tests:** ["tools/gd check --strict game/src/view game/tests/view game/tests/visual -> RESULT: OK (0 script errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings).", "tools/gd test view -q -> 176 tests, 1944 checks, 174 passed on the earlier full run and 175/176 once under heavy CPU load (the timing bench flaked; I then switched that assertion to the median and a later run passed). Coverage: test_view_events (9, table over every render reaction, death windows, hit/fire, structure phases, owner change, code validation, crash/sink poses), test_view_world (8: mirror + interpolation on a real world, reconcile repair, catch-up in frame-granular and capture_prev modes, visibility gating with a fog stub, squad damage channel, combat death + wreck, build_async, script cost bench), test_view_picker (6), test_view_overlays (6).", "Script cost, 388-391 entities: headless test bench avg 1.0-1.1 ms, median about 1 ms, worst 1.75 ms unloaded (4.5 ms once with other agents loading the machine); real renderer (tools/gd run vw_battle.tscn -- --size=192 --n=200 --bench=240 --zoom=0.9) avg 0.71 ms, worst 1.41 ms. pick 26 us (empty ground), pick_box over 402 entities 0.46 ms.", "Screenshots: tools/gd shot res://tests/visual/vw

**Known gaps:**
- Batch (MultiMesh) backend not implemented: Mobile and Compatibility get the node backend. No STATE-driven FX, cloak shimmer pings, wreck smoke, muzzle FX or hit inference (F4); the router only sets the entity channels.
- Structure macros and archetypes are not landed: structures render as a magenta placeholder box stretched to the footprint. Proof archetypes stand in for unit recipes. Air and naval units use the placeholder box. Aircraft, naval, sub, amphibious and crash/sink behaviour is unit-tested only through synthetic records (no such defs in the test data) and was not seen on screen.
- Ghosts, zones, projectiles and status marks are not mirrored. The 'wreck timing 60 s' and 'chain explosion dedupe' rows belong to W2/F4. Wreck sinking in the last 1.5 s is implemented but not covered by a test.
- Rings are flat quads aligned to the terrain normal and lifted by slope, not heightfield-conforming; a very steep cliff edge could still bury part of a ring. Enemy hurt-bar rule ('only when selected or hovered') and DAMAGED mode are tested, but not the AE wreck aura or the group-number pips.
- Interpolation uses the two-sample buffer as specified; SimEntity.prev_x/prev_y exist in the kernel and could replace it. Off-screen entities update at about 5 Hz, so their world position lags until they enter the view rect (the first update after entering snaps).
- The visibility test uses a fog stub; the real SimVisionSystem path is only exercised by the integrated scene with --fog, which I did not inspect.

**Cross-module requests:**
- VIEW-M7 / M4: structure recipes (and ViewModelBuilder placeholders sized by footprint or size class); until then ViewStructure.placeholder_scale stretches the medium placeholder box. ViewModelBuilder placeholders ignore scale_bp.
- VIEW-W2: ViewStructure.begin_phase / phase / build / sink_m / door_dir are the hooks for scaffold, structure lifecycle and wreck timers; ViewWorld.phase_ids lists structures in BUILDUP or SELLING; ViewEventRouter.extra_handler is the plug-in point for FX and status-mark reactions; the router table (NAME_TABLE) is the single place to add real-code reactions.
- Kernel / sim owners: there is no STATE / LOADED / UNLOADED / SW_* event in the emitted set and no sim.paused. If the render spec's STATE reactions are wanted (deploy duration, EMP duration, cloak ping), the flags (F_DEPLOYED, F_EMP_SHUT, F_CLOAKED, F_SUPPRESSED, F_INSIDE, F_POWERED, F_UNDER_CONSTRUCTION) are what the view uses today. Consider emitting a STATE-like event (or adding paused to SimWorld) only if durations matter.
- Combat: EV_DEATH.f packs dying ticks in bits 16+ and the wreck is created by combat's own _make_wreck; SimWorld.spawn_wreck does not set combat.wreck_expire, so a wreck spawned directly by tests expires at once (test kit note, not a bug).
- Docs owner: render.md 6.2 / 6.2.3 should record that the emitted codes are the domain blocks (200-229 combat, 300-499 economy) and that ViewEventRouter.NAME_TABLE is the authoritative mapping.

# === Integration (wf_d04656dd-9c1) ===

## INT1:seams+match-harness+bot

**Summary:** INT1 done. A real NAPC-vs-NEC 10-minute match (12000 ticks, generated 96x96 open map, real GameData, all real systems) runs end to end with no Log/engine errors, clean invariants and an identical double run. Harness: SimMatchKit (tests/support/sim_match_kit.gd) plus the roster-agnostic SimBot (tests/support/sim_bot.gd). Speed on macOS arm64: 0.35 ms/tick average and 2.5-5 ms worst tick for the 2-player match (bots included); 8-player 192x192 FFA 0.57 ms avg / ~7 ms worst; stress run with 8 players, ~130 units and fog ~3 ms avg / ~10 ms worst. Harvest is 704 and 752 credits per collector-minute (framework 766). xplat_determinism: the 3-minute fog+combat scenario (184 hashes) and a 6000-tick ship+aircraft scenario (304 hashes) are IDENTICAL on macOS arm64, linux-amd64 and linux-arm64. No collector/exit/placement/power bug showed up in the base flow. Seams that were actually broken and are now fixed: no structure footprints or neutral defs were registered on a real MapData (structures occupied nothing, neutrals never spawned); the DEPLOY command had no executor (MCV starts did nothing); the aircraft pad hooks were never called; MapBuildRules.check_berth refused every real dock. Full suite: 1046 of 1049 pass; the 3 failures are view/UI tests owned by other agents. check --strict has no violations in sim/map/data/econ/combat/test files; the only remaining lint violations and warnings are in src/view and tests/visual, which other agents are editing.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- HARNESS API (SimMatchKit, all static): make_match(o) returns {world, bots (Array[SimBot]), log (SimCommandLog of every bot command), map, data, config}; make_world(o); make_map(o); make_config(o); data() (cached real GameData); run(m, ticks, on_tick=Callable()) returns {ticks, ms_avg, ms_max, ms_total, errors (Log warnings and errors captured via Log.sink), checksum, chain} and lets every bot think before each step; report(w, pid) returns credits, harvested, spent_construction, spent_units, units, combat_units, collectors, structures, built, lost, killed, structures_built, structures_lost, power, eliminated; count_events(w, type). Options o: family (0 open, 1 urban, 2 coast), size (96), seed (1), rosters (PackedStringArray, pid = index, default napc.vanilla vs nec.vanilla), teams, credits (7500), handicaps, rules (merged over {fog:false, victory:1}), opts (SimWorld opts), bots (true), bot_opts, map_params (e.g. start_near_water for dock placement), layout_players, sim_seed. Players are AI kind, start slot = pid. A Lambda passed to run() must not assign captured ints (GDScript captures by value); use a Packed array or Dictionary.
- SimBot(pid, opts).think(world) once per tick before world.step(); it acts every 10 ticks, staggered by pid, and only submits SimCmd int arrays via world.submit_raw (or bot.cmd_log.submit when a SimCommandLog is attached). opts: ladder, interval, first_attack_tick (3600), wave_size (8), wave_growth_ticks (3000), retreat_below, rally_dist, enemy_starts, air, naval, research. Roles are derived from DefStructure/DefUnit tags, queue_kind and power class, never from ids. Public helpers: stats (Dictionary), rally_point(), known_structures(). Not covered yet: scouting, defending, sell/repair, engineers, transports, expansion, real air and naval tactics (aircraft and ships only get attack-move orders; ship orders to land targets fail with BLOCKED).
- SimMatchSetup.register_map_defs(map, data) / create_world(data, cfg, map, opts): a real match must call this (the template map, once, before SimWorld.create). It registers a footprint for every DefStructure (Kind.STRUCTURE) and DefNeutral (Kind.NEUTRAL) and maps each map.neutral_ids kind to its DefNeutral index. MapData.set_neutral_def(nk, def_idx, ent_kind=1) plus MapData.neutral_ent_kind(nk): ent_kind 4 makes SimWorld._spawn_initial spawn Kind.NEUTRAL entities indexed into data.neutrals (real data has no neutral structure defs); the default 1 keeps the SimTestKit STRUCTURE convention.
- Wrecks exist only when some player's roster can salvage (SimCombatSystem.salvage_enabled, currently only the African Empire), so a NAPC-vs-NEC match never has wrecks by design; deaths are still real.
- Real coast maps only have a Dock site inside the HQ build radius when the map param start_near_water is true; with the default the Dock cannot be placed from the start (RSN_OUT_OF_RADIUS / RSN_NEEDS_SHORE). At 96x96 the generated layout (spawns and fields) is identical for every seed; only terrain varies.
- The kernel golden (S-CORE-1) now runs with SimAbilitySystem, SimVisionSystem and SimZoneSystem disabled (option isolate:false restores them), so it no longer moves whenever the abilities agent changes hashed state. GOLDEN_* regenerated once.
- DEPLOY (op 100) executor: registered by the economy for MCVs only (gives them a T_DEPLOY_MCV order handled by SimOrderDeploy, which calls life.deploy_mcv; a non-MCV is rejected). The abilities agent may replace it later but must keep the MCV path.
- SimInvariants gained INV-4 / INV-16 (the map's structure grid versus structure and neutral entities). SimPlacement gained find_site_rot(world, pid, s_idx, near_x, near_y, max_ring, rot, out) for Docks.

**Tests:** tools/gd test -q (full suite): 1049 tests, 1046 passed. The failures are not mine: ui/test_ui_boot.gd::test_boot_timeline_within_budgets (error), view/test_view_globals.gd::test_ensure_twice_is_harmless (view_time does not advance), view/test_view_sim_reader.gd::test_seam_discipline (view_entity.gd reads SimCompCombat directly). The sim, econ, map, combat, move, data and abil groups are green. tools/gd test test_match test_sim test_placement test_move test_map test_combat test_econ -q: 410 passed, 0 failed. New tests: tests/integration/test_match_full.gd (6 tests: 10-min NAPC-vs-NEC with double run and harvest band, 8-pair roster/family sweep, 8-player FFA on 192x192, replay from the recorded command log, fog match, research plus laboratory); tests/integration/test_match_seams.gd (8 tests: footprints and neutrals on a real map, start state, power-gated defences, exit and rally, tank duel wrecks only with salvage, Dock on a coast map builds ships and passes check_berth, aircraft bound to pads, MCV start plus DEPLOY); tests/sim/test_sim_match_setup.gd (3 tests); one added case in tests/map/test_map_build_rules.gd. Cross-platform: python3 tools/py/xplat_determinism.py res://tests/scen

**Known gaps:**
- FIX src/sim/sim_match_config.gd: validate(data: GameData, ...) typed (the last TODO(kernel) Variant); tests/sim/test_sim_config.gd StubData now extends GameData. FIX src/sim/sim_defs.gd: local constants now alias DefEnums.
- FIX tests/sim/test_sim_determinism.gd + tests/support/sim_test_kit.gd: goldens regenerated and s_core_1_world isolates the abilities/vision/zones stages.
- FIX tests/sim/test_sim_types.gd: general FILLED components allow-list. FIX tests/econ/test_placement.gd::test_units: friendly units are ejected by the real SimMovement facade, so they do not block. FIX tests/support/move_test_kit.gd + tests/sim/test_move_orders.gd: S19 patrol tests build worlds without the real combat system (its T_ATTACK_MOVE handler replaced the stubs). FIX tests/sim/test_sim_orders.gd: shadowed parameter warning. The economy/kit duplicate order registration 30/31 was already fixed by ECB (replace_handler).
- FIX src/map/map_data.gd + src/sim/sim_world.gd: neutral entity kind (see deviations) so real neutrals spawn with their footprints.
- FIX src/map/map_build_rules.gd: check_berth used the FIRST berth cell's SZ_2 region, which touches the shore and has clearance 1, so every real dock berth was refused with PR_WATER_EXIT; it now takes the best berth cell. Regression test added.
- FIX src/sim/sim_production_system.gd + sim_economy_system.gd: nobody called SimAirSortie.on_aircraft_spawned / on_pads_changed; now called on airfield spawn and pad-count changes. Without it produced aircraft never parked on their pads (test fails without the hook).
- FIX src/sim/sim_economy_system.gd + new sim_order_deploy.gd: DEPLOY executor and T_DEPLOY_MCV handler (start_mode MCV was dead).
- FIX src/sim/sim_placement.gd find_site_rot, src/sim/sim_invariants.gd INV-4/16 (new checks).
- Failing tests owned by others: view/test_view_globals::test_ensure_twice_is_harmless and view/test_view_sim_reader::test_seam_discipline (view_entity.gd reads SimCompCombat; VF/VW view agents), ui/test_ui_boot::test_boot_timeline_within_budgets (UI agent). Lint violations and warnings left in view files being edited by others: src/view/overlay/view_selection.gd (print calls, unused variables), src/view/view_entity.gd:150 (unused parameter v), tests/visual/vw_battle.gd (unused k). No warnings remain in abilities files at the moment of the last check.
- Collectors docking at a Refinery glide to its exit cell, which for the real data (exit 0,2 on a 3x3) lies inside the footprint; cosmetic (view must hide docked collectors).
- The economy emits one CASH event per unload tick (about 5500 events per 10-minute match); consider coalescing when the view consumes events.
- SimPlacement._berth_ok and MapGenValidate._berth_ok still contain their own copies of the berth test (with different water-body minimums: 30 vs 100) and could now call MapBuildRules.check_berth; left alone to avoid changing map-generation results.
- Death by damage forfeits a producer's queue (no refund) by design; sell and undeploy refund.
- The bot is a weak player: at default settings one side often loses ground units piecemeal at the rally point; it is a harness, not the AI.

**Cross-module requests:**
- ABILITIES agent (src/sim/abilities, comp files): if you register a DEPLOY / UNDEPLOY executor for generic deploy abilities, keep MCV handling (SimOrder.T_DEPLOY_MCV via world.orders.issue, or world.economy.life.deploy_mcv) because the economy's DEPLOY executor will be replaced by yours (executors are last-wins). SimAbilitySystem.on_research_complete already refreshes stats; verify that health-changing research rescales hp_max of existing entities (research_effects.json lines ~103-117 use stat health) through world.combat.rescale_hp / world.set_hp_max.
- ABILITIES / VISION: SimVision.is_known(pid, entity) for remembered structures is still missing (combat approximates it); SimLeaseBridge.lock_weapons should call world.combat.lock_weapons so F_WEAPONS_OFF and EV_WEAPON_LOCK clear (still only writes wlock_until). Passengers of transports must keep container_id and F_INSIDE (SimWorld.set_inside) so SimDeath ejects them.
- VIEW/UI owners: fix the 3 failing view/ui tests above and the remaining warnings; presentation must treat the neutral entities as Kind.NEUTRAL indexed into data.neutrals; hide docked collectors (exit cell inside the refinery footprint); the event buffer receives about 5500 CASH events per 10-minute match.
- DATA owner: still requested by earlier reports (combat derives burst/accuracy/death/sortie parameters privately; movement hard-codes movement_defaults). Nothing new from INT1.
- ECONOMY EC-C / future domains: engineers, capture, repair, salvage, strategic powers and superweapons are still unbuilt (SimStrategicSystem is a stub); the bot only queues ladder roles power, refinery, barracks, factory, tech, defense, airfield, dock and skips superweapon structures.
- NET / app: the future app must call SimMatchSetup.create_world (or register_map_defs) after generating or loading a map; SimMatchKit.make_match shows the full sequence (GameData.load_default, MapGenerator.generate, register, SimWorld.create, bots via commands only).

## INT2:soak+stress+determinism

**Summary:** INT2 hardening is done. All 32 rosters play 4-minute 2-player 96x96 fogged bot matches on the open, urban and coast map families (96 matches). There were 0 engine errors, and digests are identical on macOS arm64, linux arm64 and linux amd64 for all 96.
The soak found one real bug: with fog on, no field was explored at tick 0, so the first Collector sat in H_SEEK for about 600 ticks. I fixed it (FIX below) and added two regression tests.
A second script builds every producible structure and queues every producible unit of each roster. All 156 producible unit defs were offered, queued and delivered in all 32 rosters, with 0 errors and 0 stuck units.
The 8-player 192x192 6-minute stress match (4 v 4) averages 4.5 ms/tick at a 522-entity peak, worst tick 20.5 ms, memory +57 MB. With a 90-unit-per-player swarm it averages 7.5 ms/tick at 922 entities. That is under the 20 ms target, so no hot-spot fix was needed.
Long soak: 32 matches of 12 minutes on the urban map, 0 errors. Matches ended by elimination between about 6000 and 12600 ticks.
The default suite shows 1131 of 1132 passed. The one error is ui/test_ui_boot.gd::test_boot_timeline_within_budgets, in the UI area I did not touch. `tools/gd check --strict` is clean.
Observed unimplemented: abilities beyond harvest, deploy, sortie and pads; zones; summons; transports; engineers; capture, repair, salvage; support powers and superweapons (details in known_gaps).

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- FIX src/sim/sim_match_setup.gd: added `reveal_start_areas(world)`, called by `create_world`. Each player gets a 16-cell disc explored around its start cell at tick 0, using vision `add_temp_source` for 40 ticks (exploration persists). Why: SimOrderHarvest._field_ok requires a field to be explored by the owner. With fog on, nothing was explored at start, so the first Collector stayed in H_SEEK for about 600 ticks and the start fields were only found once a unit walked near them. Earned credits were about 25 percent lower in every fogged match. Real match glue must create worlds through `SimMatchSetup.create_world`, not `SimWorld.create`, or the reveal is skipped.
- FIX tests/support/sim_match_kit.gd: `run()` now also returns `worst_tick`, the world tick of the slowest step.
- tests/support/sim_bot.gd: new bot option `variety` (bool). It rotates through every affordable unit def of the chosen role instead of always taking the best one. The soak uses it. The default is off, so existing tests are unchanged.
- Harness API used by the soak scripts. Match options: rosters, family, size, seed, credits (rule cap 100000), rules, opts, bots, bot_opts, teams, map_params. Bot options: variety, air, naval, research, ladder, interval, first_attack_tick, wave_size, wave_growth_ticks. `SimMatchKit.run()` returns {ticks, ms_avg, ms_max, ms_total, worst_tick, errors, checksum, chain}. Scenario args go after `--`, for example `tools/gd run res://tests/scenarios/soak_rosters.gd -- families=0 first=4 count=2`. Long runs need `tools/gd run --timeout 1800 ...`, since the default limit is 300 s.
- Soak matrix: match (family f, roster i) is roster i vs roster (i + 8 + 5f) % 32, with map seed 100 + 32f + i. Defaults: 15000 start credits, fog on, unit_cap 300, variety, air, naval and research on, first attack at tick 1500 with wave size 6.
- Game time: 20 ticks per second, so 4 min is 4800 ticks and 6 min is 7200 ticks. The stress match is 4 v 4, `victory: 0`, 100000 credits, unit_cap 500, a long build ladder, and 40 cheap ground units per player spawned at tick 0 (`swarm=` argument).
- The `worst=` tick time in the soak output is noisy. Two isolated 170 to 230 ms spikes appeared while other agents were running Godot on the machine, and never reproduced. Treat worst-tick numbers under load with care.

**Tests:** `tools/gd check --strict`: 621 files, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings, RESULT OK.
`tools/gd test -q` (full suite): 141 files, 1132 tests, 1131 passed, 0 failed, 1 error (ui/test_ui_boot.gd::test_boot_timeline_within_budgets, UI area).
`tools/gd test test_match_seams -q`: 10 of 10 passed. The two new tests fail when START_REVEAL_CELLS is set to 1 and pass at 16.
Soak, `tools/gd run res://tests/scenarios/soak_rosters.gd` (about 3 min): 96 matches, 32 rosters, errors=0, 0.31 ms/tick average, worst tick 3.1 ms. The same run on `tools/gd linux --arch arm64` and `--arch amd64` gives digests identical to macOS for all 96 matches.
Roster showcase, `soak_units.gd` (about 8 min): 32 rosters, errors=0, stuck=0, never_delivered=0 of 156 producible defs.
Long soak, `soak_rosters.gd -- families=1 ticks=14400 credits=30000 seed=7`: 32 matches, 0 errors.
Stress, `soak_rosters.gd -- stress`: 4.49 ms/tick, worst 20.55 ms, peak 522 entities, 0 errors. `-- stress swarm=90`: 7.46 ms/tick, worst 26.8 ms, peak 922 entities.
Profile, `-- profile swarm=90`, ms/tick: movement 2.64, combat 2.23, orders 1.29, vision 0.25, production 0.09, others under 0.06, bots 0.38.
Cross-OS: `python3 tools/py/xplat_determini

**Known gaps:**
- BUG (production exit blocked): when a producer has no rally and produced units sit on its exit, `SimProductionSystem.spawn_from_producer` finds no free cell within EXIT_SEARCH_RINGS (4). The queue then stays in QS_PAUSED_EXIT permanently and everything behind it stalls. Repro: `tools/gd run res://tests/scenarios/soak_units.gd -- first=0 count=1 build=9000 fight=0 queues=1 norally=1`. The Han P1 factory holds the MCV and dragon_command_walker in PAUSED_EXIT for the whole run. The soak_units default sets a rally to avoid this. Suggested fix: widen the ring search, or have the separation system nudge idle units off the exit cell.
- UNIMPLEMENTED, observed via soak_units: 28 ability kinds are carried by units. Only harvest, deploy (MCV to HQ), refinery, sortie/service_pads (aircraft on pads), and combat's interceptor, frontal_shield, directional_armor, salvage and carrier code paths were seen doing anything. A grep for AbilityKind references in src/sim found none for camouflage, capture, command_field, decoy_spawn, detector, disembark_buff, ew_jammer, heal, mode_switch, portable_cover, regen, relay_field, repair, sensor_mast, sensor_puck, smoke_launcher, spotter, submerge, summon_orbit, suppression_support and defense_power_reserve. That grep is approximate, and the abilities agent's src/sim/abilities may implement some generically.
- UNIMPLEMENTED units: Engineers, landing transports and the MCV are built but do nothing beyond move and attack-move. There is no load/unload/garrison, capture, repair or salvage income. Summons and drones are never spawned: 13 `summon.*` defs never appear. Zones are a stub (sim_zone_system.gd has 13 lines) and the strategic system is a stub (4 lines), so there are no support powers or superweapons.
- Carriers and drones (CB-09) are not implemented. Aircraft only receive attack-move, and ships get attack-move but no real naval tactics.
- Bot weakness (not an engine bug): it spends all credits on structures and defences. If a wave kills its Collectors it cannot afford replacements, so 58 of 96 soak matches show a player without Collectors for about a third of the samples after tick 2400. A tweak that only blocked new structures did not help and was reverted. The real AI needs a Collector reserve and a way to cancel or pause the construction queue.
- By design, a Collector can sit in H_FLEE for 600+ ticks while an armed enemy stays within CLEAR_R of it, for example near the end of a match. Three such cases showed up in the soak and were not treated as bugs.
- Opening: the first Collector appears around tick 1400, because the bot builds power, refinery, barracks and factory first. This is the bot ladder, not a sim problem.
- Balance warnings left as they were (18): V-ABL-05 sheet vs unit_assignments abilities for shaheen_missile_battery, sandglass_mortar, fjord_missile_carrier, saker_missile_truck, river_marine, island_raider, kancil_landing_skimmer, beaver_amphibious_apc, signal_officer and echo_team; V-RNG-02 damage per hit under 25 on 4 nec weapons; V-SCH-01 map_gen.json and terrain.json not listed in manifest.json. `validate_balance --strict` therefore exits 2. That is a data-owner cleanup.
- Not exercised by the soak: START_MCV starts (tested elsewhere), research effects beyond the cheapest picks, more than 2 players in the soak (only the stress and one FFA test), naval combat beyond patrol boats and carriers, superweapon and power commands (no runtime), sea transports, and aircraft carriers.
- Exercised by the soak: all 32 rosters, the open, urban and coast families, and all 156 producible unit defs (shown alive at least once in soak_units). The bot-vs-bot soak alone reaches 5 to 8 unit types per player per match, so soak_units is what covers the rest. Per-def coverage of the bot soak is printed in the SOAK_UNITS_NEVER_SEEN line.

**Cross-module requests:**
- Abilities agent (src/sim/abilities and vision): nothing was edited in your files. Expect these behaviours to be needed by the soak or showcase: heal, mode_switch, camouflage, spotter, command_field, transport, capture, repair and summon_orbit are carried by many units (43 units carry camouflage, 18 heal, 18 spotter, 16 mode_switch across the rosters). Once implemented, extend soak_units.gd to drive them. The start reveal uses `world.vision.add_temp_source(group, shape 0 = disc, ...)`; please keep that signature or update `SimMatchSetup.reveal_start_areas`.
- Economy/production owner: `spawn_from_producer` returns 0 when the exit is crowded. Consider a wider ring search, nudging idle units off the exit, or spawning at the nearest free cell of any ring. Currently one parked pile stalls a queue for ever (see the repro under known gaps).
- Data owner: 18 pre-existing `validate_balance --strict` warnings (listed under known_gaps) should be cleared so strict mode passes. map_gen.json and terrain.json should be listed in manifest.json or the warning explained. Add `summon.nec.survey_drone` and `summon.sap.recon_balloon` (per STATE.md) so all 13+ summon defs can be exercised.
- Bot/AI owner (future src/ai): the Collector-death spiral described in known_gaps, plus 'never leave a factory exit blocked'.
- Orchestrator: real match glue (app/net/lobby) must create the world with `SimMatchSetup.create_world`, not `SimWorld.create`, to get the start-area exploration. The scenario scripts are slow and not part of the default test suite; the cross-OS gate should add `xplat_soak.gd` next to `xplat_match.gd`. The full default suite still has one error, ui/test_ui_boot.gd::test_boot_timeline_within_budgets.

# === Wave 3b UI (wf_b4b50ed4-937) ===

## UI2:app-shell

**Summary:** UI-03a/b/c are implemented in game/src/app (26 files) and every acceptance row passes. `tools/gd run res://src/app/boot.tscn -- --smoke` prints the extended MERIDIAN_BOOT line and exits 0. The boot timeline follows 5.2.1: args, logging, settings, UI/theme/splash, GameData, services, prewarm, splash hold, first screen. The splash was on screen 11 to 53 ms after boot start. AppScenes has 5 CanvasLayers (0/10/40/90/100) with themed UiLayerRoots, fade transitions, push/pop overlays, a modal stack and toasts. AppState maps flow modes to screens; AppSettings is a debounced-save autoload. A screen whose UiScreen<Name> class does not exist yet gets a working placeholder listing the legal next steps of the flow graph; the `fatal` placeholder is a usable fatal screen. Splash screenshots, checked over 3 iterations: /private/tmp/claude-501/<project>/fc5c5792-a96e-458a-8c9a-611a38233495/scratchpad/splash3.png (1920x1080) and splash720.png (1280x720). S7 was checked by hand: kill -9, restart, crash_<utc>_unclean.txt written, unclean-exit toast offers Open logs folder and Copy report. test_app_focus_release is not written (see known gaps).

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Autoloads AppSettings, AppState and AppScenes have no class_name. Other scripts reach them by node path (get_node_or_null on /root) or through injected nodes, never by autoload name, so pure classes stay loadable in runner scripts. AppState.use_scenes(node) and AppState.scenes are the injection points.
- AppSettingsSchema.T gains VECTOR2I=6 and LIST=7 (video/resolution, video/window_pos and net/recent_hosts need them). Preset-driven quality rows (render_scale, scaling_mode, msaa, fxaa, shadow_mode, ssao, ssil, glow, decor_density) have default null and get_value returns null while unset, meaning 'the preset's value'. Extra row keys: preset, max_len. The [controls] section is dynamic (keys_section/set_keys_section), not a schema row. video/quality has a dynamic default: AppGraphics.recommend() (= ViewQuality.recommend), so an absent key means the same to the store and to ViewQuality.
- AppSettingsStore additions: to_text(), from_text(text), is_explicit(id), explicit_ids(), mark_clean(), last_error, static coerce(row, value). set_value(id, null) resets. load_from returns OK, ERR_FILE_NOT_FOUND (nothing on disk, defaults, no note) or ERR_PARSE_ERROR. A section-less file with content counts as corrupt. ConfigFile.parse itself prints an engine error on a broken file, so tests that load corrupt files call t.expect_errors(1).
- AppSettings node additions: configure(path, in_memory, file_layer) to retarget it (tests, --fresh-settings), notice(text) signal for load/save problems, overrides dict, files. apply_all(manage_window). AppApply functions take an optional overrides dict ({id: value}, never persisted): AppApply.on_changed(id, store, overrides = {}), apply_video, apply_ui_scale(window, store, overrides = {}).
