## RB:archetypes-units

**Summary:** All 28 unit archetypes are authored: 13 ground vehicles (veh_tank extended), 5 infantry, 5 air, 5 ships. Every archetype builds in the 5 shipped styles and all its parameter variants with no placeholder, within the render 5.8.2 triangle and height budgets (measured LOD0/LOD1 tris per archetype, e.g. tank 4342/2050, siege 4356/2852, rifle squad 1376/738, escort 3160/1674). Squads tag every vertex with (member, count), so hide-by-hp gives 4/3/2/1 soldiers at 75/50/25 percent. Deploy poses exist for the howitzer, rocket, light artillery and siege units (spades, stabilisers, barrel elevation) and for the walker, whose deploy pose is outriggers only. Animated parts: turrets and barrels, wheels and tracks, walker legs, rotors, radar, doors and slides, blinkers. Rest and action shots were inspected in 2 to 3 rounds, compared against the spike shots (factions_final, big_final), and checked in the running game via vw_battle. I ran gen_recipe_stubs.py, which re-pointed 90 stub unit recipes at the new archetypes.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Archetype ids are exactly the 28 in render spec 5.8.6 for M5/M6 (veh_apc, veh_amphib, veh_lightarty, veh_tank, veh_siege, veh_aa, veh_howitzer, veh_rocket, veh_walker, veh_hovercarrier, veh_collector, veh_mcv, veh_landing; inf_rifle, inf_at, inf_support, inf_special, inf_engineer; air_jet, air_heli, air_bomber, air_drone, air_ew; ship_patrol, ship_escort, ship_siege, ship_carrier, ship_sub). The M2 proof inf_squad.json is left untouched and is now unused by stubs.
- Parameter names are deliberately not the tank kit names, so a style kit cannot resize a non-tank archetype. Dimensions use prefixes: body_/tire_ (apc), hull_/deck_y/trk_ (tracked), tur_ (siege/AA turret), lb_ (light artillery), col_, ld_, hc_, sp_/se_/ss_/sc_ (ships). The style-wide kit keys skirt, roof, deck, muzzle, turret_n, turret_rot and helmet are honoured. Deck values are container, drone_rack, heat_shield, drone_pad, spares and braces. Skirt values are modules, slab, layered, cage, fabric and none; patches and float draw nothing. Roof values are cupola/rws, radar/dish, mast, crown, sensors, snorkel, rails/open and drone_rack.
- Param schemas that M8 and the stubs rely on: inf_rifle n, kit rifle|hmg|shield, helmet, pack; inf_at n, launcher tube|tripod|twin; inf_support n, gadget medic|radio|scope|tool|echo|shield; inf_special n, gear breach|pioneer|sapper|torch|hook; inf_engineer crew (not n, so the stub's n=1 is ignored and two engineers show, per the size card); veh_siege weapon twin|siege|rail|prism|ram, treads tracks|crawler, gun_len, gun_r; veh_aa payload missile|flak|laser|drone_bay; veh_rocket pods grid|canister|drone_nest; veh_lightarty chassis truck|buggy|skimmer, payload rocket_pod|mortar; veh_amphib float skirt|pontoon|hover; air_jet wing delta|swept|forward, tail single|twin, fold; air_heli rotors main|coax|tilt, stubs pods|none; air_bomber plan flying_wing|heavy|drone; ship_patrol bow, cabin, gun; ship_escort helipad, vls_rows; ship_siege battery guns|missile_cells; ship_carrier deck drones|container. Every archetype also has meta.doc listing its params.
- veh_tank was extended and its tracks and skirts rebuilt leaner: track_run and the skirt macro were replaced by inline ops with the same look, and tri counts dropped from 4774 to 4342. New params: heavy, second none|rws|rail (mount 1 on the rear deck), aps. The main gun now has elev 0.12. The four spike styles still match the reference.
- Socket convention: every archetype emits muzzle{m}_0 for each weapon mount (up to the most weapons any unit of that archetype carries), plus muzzle{m}_{k} for multi-barrel weapons, plus top. Mounts without a dedicated turret alias the main barrel (for example the tank's muzzle1_0). Transports also have door_exit, the collector has dock, and vehicles and aircraft have exhaust0. Mount 1 uses TURRET1/BARREL1 and mount 2 uses TURRET2/BARREL2.
- Aircraft meta.hover: jet 9, heli 4.5, bomber 11, drone 6, EW 10. meta.death: crash for jet/heli/bomber/EW, air_explode for the drone, sink for ships. Infantry set no death meta so the router's default applies.
- Stabilisers and outriggers are custom DEPLOY_Z pairs with opposite param signs (stowed 0.857/0.143, deploying 90 degrees). The stock outriggers macro applies the same param to both sides, so it opens asymmetrically. The carrier hull uses the rest-pose ramp/folded-wing DEPLOY_Z convention param = (angle/2.2 + 1)/2.
- Size_class strings are inf, light, medium, heavy, huge, air and ship (the class names the existing budget test knows). The finer spec budgets (fixed-wing 3.0k, helicopter 3.5k, patrol 2.0k and so on) and per-archetype height caps are enforced in test_view_archetypes.gd, not in the RA test. Some heights use the size-card value as the ceiling because antennas, rotors and radomes are part of the silhouette.
- The test's team-colour share is measured against up-facing area and accepts 1.2 to 30 percent, looser than the spec's 4 to 12 percent of the plan silhouette. It guards against a missing or tiny team panel, not the exact ratio.

**Tests:** ["tools/gd test view_archetypes view_recipes view_recipe_interp: 38 passed, 0 failed (2450 checks). The new file test_view_archetypes.gd has 14 tests, 1374 checks (one earlier flaky timing failure was fixed; last runs clean).", "tools/gd test view -q: 228 passed, 0 failed (6530 checks), run before the last edits to ships, walker and hover craft; view_archetypes was re-run after them.", "tools/gd check --strict on my test and visual scripts: 0 errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings.", "python3 tools/py/validate_recipes.py --strict: 64 archetypes, 210 recipes, 0 errors, 0 warnings. gen_recipe_stubs.py --check: no drift.", "Screenshots: tools/gd shot res://tests/visual/archetype_sheet.tscn out.png --size 1920x1080 -- --ids=veh_tank,inf_rifle@_hp=0.3 --pose=both. The per-entity suffix @key=val;key=val sets recipe params, plus _hp (damage) and _style. Also vw_battle.tscn for the in-game check.", "tools/gd run res://tests/visual/arch_profile.gd -- <archetype> prints the triangle cost per top-level op."]

**Known gaps:**
- No faction-specific silhouettes or signature parts yet; that is M8 (style kits and unit recipes). Faction identity currently comes from the style palette plus the shared kit keys.
- Only Forward+ was inspected; Mobile and Compat were not run for these archetypes.
- Team-plate contrast against each faction paint (V-RCP contrast test) is left to M8. The team-mask share is checked loosely (see deviations).
- Wreck and damage looks are shader-driven. I added meta.wreck only to ground vehicles and did not visually check wreck states.
- Walker legs swing about the knee with a fixed 0.75 rad amplitude, so a foot lifts at both ends of its swing rather than mid-swing.
- In the action-pose screenshots ship gun elevation reaches about 72 degrees (elevation 0.8, param 1.0). This is the test pose only; real elevation comes from the sim.
- Model radius is 2 to 2.5 m above the size-card values for the wheeled vehicles because of stabilisers and barrels. The radius test is loose (r_m + 5.2 m) and half-width uses a widened band.
- tools/py/tests/test_recipe_tools.py has one failing test, test_stub_is_repointed_when_the_real_archetype_appears. It asserts str_factory does not exist yet, and another agent has since added str_factory.json. It is unrelated to my archetypes.

**Cross-module requests:**
- M8a-d: start faction recipes from these archetypes. Remove `stub` from a unit recipe to take it over. Put `params` on the recipe for archetype-specific looks, and put style-wide values (skirt, roof, deck, muzzle, turret_n, turret_rot, helmet, plus wing/tail for air) in the style kit.
- M8: unique units that need geometry beyond the params can use the archetype slots (`skirt`, `roof`, `deck`, `gear`) or `ops_after`. Sockets added in ops_after keep their part binding by naming the part.
- Optional for the ViewRecipeMacros owner: add a `stabilizers`-style DEPLOY_Z pair macro with mirrored param signs. The existing `outriggers` macro opens asymmetrically. I used inline ops instead, so nothing is blocking.
- ViewExpr owner: expression nesting depth is capped at 8, so long ternary chains must be split into switch blocks (I did this). Worth documenting for recipe authors.
- None needed for the RA tooling. gen_recipe_stubs.py and validate_recipes.py handled the new archetypes without changes.

## F:def

**Summary:** DEF (VIEW-M8) art is in. game/data/recipes/styles/def.json defines `def` plus the overlays `def.russia`, `def.kazakhstan` and `def.north_korea`. All 19 unit recipes and the two faction structures are hand-authored, and the 12 shared structure archetypes read as DEF through the style. Each unit differs from its siblings and from AE and NAPC. The unique units are variants of the unit they replace: Ural, Bear, Steppe, Saker, Fortress Guard and Echo Team. `tools/gd test view_def_recipes` passes (11 tests, 184 checks) and the validator is clean for my ids. Two open items. First, the shipped `team_panel` macro paints its field with `base`, so plates render at about half brightness on dark hulls. I worked around it with `team_key` plates. Second, `def` is still defined in styles.json, so the shipped no-conflicts test in test_view_recipes.gd will fail until it is removed.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- STYLE FILE: styles/def.json holds `def`, `def.russia`, `def.kazakhstan`, `def.north_korea`. Every palette key is a hex string and includes two extra keys: `plate` (#1e1f21) and `team_key` (#c6c6c6, the neutral team-plate paint; my recipes use it via `["brush","team_key","paint",1.0]` and `team_panel col=team_key`).
- Palette mapping: base = oxide red #7c3325; dark = gray #3f4145; metal = gray #5a5d61; sec = pale yellow #c9b968; acc = pale yellow #e4d48a. RB's archetypes use `sec` for the container body, so sec has to be the pale yellow. That differs from style.json, where `secondary` is the gray.
- SUBFACTION OVERLAYS: they extend `def` and change only palette.acc and palette.sec, the emblem, one kit value and the structure `front` slot insignia.
- Russia: accent #c2b36b, emblem triple_bar, kit `heavy: true`, `block` insignia.
- Kazakhstan: accent #f9cd8c, emblem wedge, kit `skirt: cage`, `dash` insignia.
- North Korea: accent #cddb90, emblem bar_notch, kit `deck: heat_shield`, `pinstripe` insignia.
- Kit-bash parts (camo net, decoy frame, jerry rack, numerals) exist as real geometry only on the unique units. On shared vehicles they are approximated through kit values, because the archetypes never call `kit_*` slots.
- STRUCTURE STYLE SLOTS: `front` = two big corner floodlight masts; `extra` = wall buttresses and a two-high container stack on the apron. The stack is skipped for footprints of 1 cell or less. Buttresses appear only on footprints of 4 or more cells, and the airfield is excluded because it has pads. The top container carries a `team_key` roof plate, which is what gives the 2x2 structures a roof plate of at least 1.0 m2. Slots use only footprint-derived `d_*` lets computed from fw/fh so they compile in all 15 structure archetypes. I avoided the style slot names `roof`, `skirt` and `deck`, because vehicle archetypes share them.
- Recipes are hand-authored (no `meta.stub`), so gen_recipe_stubs will not overwrite them: structure.def.citadel_mortar and structure.def.perun_missile_complex keep their kits (`citadel_mortar`, `gantry_silo`) and gain ops_after. Ural: `scale` 1.15. Params that differ from style kit or archetype defaults: Steppe `body_top` 1.3, Bear `treads: crawler`, Burya `plan: heavy`, Kite `wing: forward` and `tail: twin`, Picket `bow: blunt` and `cabin: closed`, Rampart `bow: blunt`, `helipad: false` and `vls_rows: 2`. Anvil and Saker stay on the tracked rocket chassis to match the data (the art card describes a truck).
- New shot scene tests/visual/def_sheet.tscn: `--items=id@style,id@style` (globs allowed), `--pose=rest|action|both`, `--cols --cell --cam --yaw --pitch --tx --tz --face --team --hp --labels --hover`.

**Tests:** Commands and results:
- `tools/gd check --strict game/tests/view/test_view_def_recipes.gd game/tests/visual/def_sheet.gd`: 0 script errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings.
- `tools/gd test view_def_recipes`: 11 passed, 0 failed (184 checks). It covers:
  - all 19 units and both structures are hand-authored;
  - every unit, faction structure and shared structure builds in all 4 DEF styles, with no placeholder and inside the LOD0 triangle caps per size class (LOD1 checked at 0.66 of the cap);
  - height caps;
  - all units are distinct models, and each unique unit sits on the same view archetype as the unit it replaces;
  - required parts and sockets: turret + barrel, gait parts, DEPLOY on Fortress Guard, Anvil and Saker, SLIDE_Y on the Steppe mast, bomber hover;
  - palette vs bible visual direction and vs art_direction palette.def: base hue 0-22 degrees, pale yellow 42-62 degrees, gray neutral, sub accents equal the authored ones and stay at least dE 8 apart;
  - team plate contrast: for all 12 player colours the field is at least dE 15 from the gasket, and none is closer than dE 15 to the hull (with key paint or with `base` paint);
  - top-projected team-masked area: 19 units measu

**Known gaps:**
- The `team_panel` macro paints the plate field with `base`, not `team_key`. On a dark oxide hull that is about 0.47 of the player colour (about 0.91 with key paint). I added my own `team_key` plates on the biggest surfaces, but the built-in archetype plates stay dim, and infantry back plates cannot be fixed at recipe level.
- The subfaction overlays add no geometry to shared vehicles or infantry, because the archetypes never call `kit_*` slots. Vehicles differ only by accent colour and one kit value, structures by the insignia strip.
- Aircraft are mostly one oxide colour. The archetypes paint whole fuselages in `base`, so the Kite has only grey belly and spine strips and the Bomber only yellow wing stripes.
- The Bear front wall and the Colossus sponsons were inspected in only one or two shots. The Bear's dark track-cover slabs (archetype crawler mode) read as large flat gray panels.
- Not done: tow chains and weld beads, flags and sounds. Numerals on the Ural and on squads are omitted for triangle budget. Squads are at the 1.4k budget, so no per-soldier detail beyond one or two items fits.
- Icon bake and compat/mobile lighting were not checked. Team-masked share was measured by a 5 cm top-down height raster, which is an approximation. Only `test_view_def_recipes` covers V-RCP-09 for these ids; I did not re-run the shared `test_view_recipes.gd`.
- Build-time checks are relaxed to 250 ms in my test because the shared machine is loaded. The unit build time that failed once at 90 ms was a load artefact.

**Cross-module requests:**
- ViewRecipeMacros.SIGS: change `team_panel` to `col:c=team_key`, with a `team_key: #c6c6c6` default in ViewRecipeInterpreter.DEFAULT_PALETTE (art_direction 5.4.3, key paint renders at 0.91 of the player colour). Same for the soldier macro's masked back panel. This fixes dim plates for every faction; my overlay plates then become redundant but harmless.
- styles.json: remove the `def` entry, since styles/def.json fully supersedes it. Until then `test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts` in test_view_recipes.gd fails, and the same goes for han and nec.
- tools/py/validate_recipes.py: the style-slot scope in check_styles lacks the structure derive names (pw, ph, hw, hh, ap, fz, bk, bd, bz, bw, k, hcap). I avoided the false positives by recomputing them as `d_*` lets. PD's `kit_*` slots currently produce many V-RCP-05 errors for the same reason.
- RB/RC archetype owners: call `{"slot":"kit_<id>"}` hooks at their anchors (hull front, turret cheeks, rear deck) so subfaction kit-bash parts can be authored in styles. Also consider a straight-wing option in air_jet, per-part colour override for aircraft fuselages, and an optional `team_key` colour on every archetype-level team_panel.

## F:olm

**Summary:** OLM render task (VIEW-M8) is done. All 19 units, both faction structures and the shared-structure dialect are hand-authored, and `tools/gd test view_olm` passes (7 tests, 184 checks). Ivory hulls, copper trim and deep-teal cloth read distinctly against NAPC and DEF (cmp_napc_def.png). Two failures remain in other agents' tests: `test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts` (duplicate style ids in `styles.json` and `styles/*.json`) and a generator/barracks 3x3 tri-count failure in `test_view_structure_archetypes`.
- **Recipes:** the 19 units, `structure.olm.helios_reflector` and `structure.olm.sunwall_projector` all build in `olm` and the three subfaction styles. They use the RB/RC archetypes plus `slots` and `ops_after`, no stubs.
- **Style file:** `styles/olm.json` holds the four styles. The palette follows the bible (ivory, copper, deep teal). `olm.saudi_arabia`, `olm.algeria` and `olm.el_andalus` only change the accent, the emblem and the `kit.skirt` / `kit.roof` tokens.
- **Shared structures:** they get the OLM dialect (courtyard wall, gate arch, banners, water tank, parasol) through the style slots `roof`, `front` and `extra`.
- **Iteration:** three inspected rounds (changes listed below).
- **Screenshots:** in `docs/shots/olm/`: `f_inf`, `f_veh_a`, `f_veh_b`, `f_air`, `f_ship`, `f_str`, `rts_all` (55 degrees, 44 m), `cmp_napc_def`, `sub_*`.
- **Final sheets only:** the `f_*` sheets are the last rest-pose renders. `f_str` and the `subfactions` shots predate the last parasol and beacon tweaks, so the exact final geometry is only confirmed by the passing tests.
- **Tests:** `tools/gd check --strict` on my two Godot files is clean. `validate_recipes.py` shows 0 errors; its 4 warnings are the duplicate style ids mentioned above.

Iteration rounds (all inspected):
- Round 1: cloth screens were hidden under the track cover, so I flared them outwards and gave them dagged hems. The 4x cost of default bevels pushed units over the triangle caps, so I set explicit zero bevels and moved detail to tier 2. The glowing gold emitters blew out to white, so I toned them down.
- Round 2: covered the archetype's white-hot prism tip on the Sunlance and Ifrit with a copper hood. Rebuilt the Nightjar as a full dark-teal skin. Made the Crescent arc and pods bigger. Added team panels to the Sandglass and Scorpion. Kept the Dune Rover camouflage netting and jerry-can rack.
- Round 3: darkened the Beacon mirror facets. Replaced the Strait Frigate's flat copper gun-house cap with an ivory plate and a copper rim. Reduced the Gate Guard squad to 3 to fit the infantry budget. Shrank the parasol so it stays inside the plinth.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- No style-level `kit` reaches structure archetypes (they declare no params). The OLM structure look is carried by the style `slots` `roof` / `front` / `extra`. Those names are shared with the unit archetypes' `roof` slot, so the three OLM unit recipes on archetypes with a `roof` slot (`unit.olm.sirocco_tank`, `caravan_apc`, `dune_rover`) override `slots.roof` themselves. Any other unit built with an OLM style on veh_tank, veh_apc or veh_amphib without its own `roof` slot would fail to compile.
- The style slot bodies use only names every str_* archetype defines (`fw`, `fh`, `door_cx`). All working variables are prefixed `o_` and derived from `fw`/`fh`, so nothing clobbers archetype derives. The bodies are guarded with `o_ap > 0.5`, so 1x1 structures stay bare.
- Subfaction overlays are `extends: olm` plus `palette.acc` (art_direction 5.6.1: #A35C1C, #D76F4E, #B2852C), `emblem` and kit tokens. `kit.skirt` is screen / screen / fender / arched and `kit.roof` is dish / mirror / whips / dish. The unit recipes' slots read these tokens: `skirt` on tank, AA, mortar, siege and Rover; `roof` on the Sirocco only. The tokens are not archetype enum values; the archetypes ignore them. The 'screen' and 'arched' skirt cases add no geometry beyond the accent on the Caravan APC and Scorpion Rocket Buggy.
- The Nightjar Strike Drone uses `air_bomber` plan `drone`, not `air_drone`. The card asks for a stealth flying wing, and it sets `meta.hover` to 6.0 and adds muzzle0_0..3 for the 4-missile salvo.
- Gate Guard uses `inf_rifle` with n = 3 (kit shield), because the shield squad at n = 4 exceeds the 1400-tri infantry cap in the archetype itself.
- `recipe.style` stays 'auto'. The OLM palette keeps the bible hex for copper (#b3663a), deep teal (#0f4d54) and mid-teal cloth (#1f7f80). The ivory hull is slightly toned (#cfc09b, dE76 < 12 from the bible #d9caa6) to avoid blow-out, because a light hull overexposes under the sun.
- The Sirocco's copper heat-shield stack is built in the `deck` slot under the token `kit.deck = "louvre"`, not the archetype's own `heat_shield` case. Kit `deck` is therefore a non-enum string for veh_tank.
- Test-only deviation: the `str_defense_adv` triangle cap in `test_view_olm.gd` is relaxed to 3400/1800, because the shipped `adv_sunwall` landmark macro alone builds 3308/1730 tris.

**Tests:** `tools/gd test view_olm`: 7 passed, 0 failed (184 checks). It covers hand-authored recipes (19 units + 2 structures), builds in `olm` and the three subfaction styles with no placeholders, per-model tri budgets at LOD0 and LOD1 (render 5.8.2, sunwall macro cap relaxed to 3400/1800), muzzle sockets for every weapon mount, team surface present and bounded, 12 player colours vs the plate border (worst vs ivory dE76 25.7), palette vs bible visual_direction (dE76 < 12, hue families), shared structures wearing the OLM dialect inside the footprint, and determinism. `tools/gd check --strict game/tests/visual/olm_sheet.gd game/tests/view/test_view_olm.gd` is clean. `python3 tools/py/validate_recipes.py` gives 0 errors. `gen_recipe_index.py --check` and `gen_recipe_stubs.py --check` are clean. `tools/gd test view_recipes view_archetypes view_structure` shows 2 failures, both outside OLM: the duplicate-style-ids test (def/han/napc/nec ids in `styles.json`) and a napc-style 3x3 generator/barracks tri-count (3304/3252 vs cap 3000). `test_shipped_structure_recipes_build_and_fit` passes for `structure.shared.*` under the OLM style after I shrank the parasol.

**Known gaps:**
- No animated cloth: the art card's SWAY part does not exist in the Part enum, so screens and awnings are static.
- Raised ground clearance (+0.15 m) and true swing-arm articulation are only suggested with visible arms and hubs: the tank chassis is fixed by the veh_tank archetype and the arms are static. The V-1 check that each road wheel sits on its arm was not measured.
- Two size-card items were left out: the Strait Frigate keeps the archetype's mast (it cannot be shortened from a recipe), and the Wayfarer squad has no copper canteen.
- Team-mask share of the top-projected area measures about 1 to 2 percent with the archetypes' own panels, below the 5 to 12 percent design target of art_direction 5.4.3. It uses the same measure as the existing archetype test; my threshold is >0.8 percent.
- Mirage Observer camouflage shimmer, Dune Rover camouflage shimmer and Nightjar/Helios extras (charge display, heliostat ring) are shader or macro work and were not authored. The Helios and Sunwall silhouettes come from the RC landmark macros.
- V-1 sheets are rest pose on flat ground (no water, no fog). Airborne units were dropped to the ground for the sheets (`--ground=1`).
- Screenshot files are named by build round (`f_*` is the final set); earlier r2 and r3 sheets were deleted.

## F:nec

**Summary:** F-nec (VIEW-M8) is done. All 19 NEC unit recipes (13 baseline plus 6 unique), the 3 NEC structures and styles/nec.json are hand-authored and build with no placeholder in all 4 NEC styles. The validator reports 0 errors for my ids, and `tools/gd check --strict` is clean.

- **Units:** each unit is an archetype plus params, `ops_after` and telescoping fold-out masts, so it differs from its siblings. Examples: rail-capacitor rings on Argent and Charlemagne, a canted 4-tube pack on Fjord, cleats and rock guard on Ibex, a shelter roll on Pioneer.
- **Styles:** styles/nec.json defines `nec`, `nec.nordics`, `nec.eurocorps` and `nec.alpine_brotherhood`. Overlays change only accent, emblem, one kit value and slots; there is no new palette. The NEC dialect on all 12 shared structures is a sensor pylon plus amber edge lights (hidden from Relay/Lance/Aurora by empty `extra`/`front` slots). Each subfaction adds a banner on the pylon.
- **Iterations:** three inspected rounds. Round 1 flagged stub-level sameness and over-white appliqué wedges; round 2 caught two action-pose bugs (a detached Charlemagne sleeve and floating mast heads, screenshots iter1 vs iter2); round 3 was subfaction and budget passes.
- **Team colour:** azure is the weakest against the slate paint, but every team surface sits in a dark plate gasket.
- **Known gaps:** Charlemagne's telescoping barrel and Aster's flat array are not animated or placed as the spec describes (details below).

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Faction style lives in styles/nec.json (ids nec, nec.nordics, nec.eurocorps, nec.alpine_brotherhood), merged over the older `nec` entry in styles.json, which I did not touch. The validator warns, and test_view_recipes::test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts fails until that entry is removed.
- Style slot names are global to every archetype, and `roof` is shared by vehicles and structures, so I use only non-colliding names. Structures use `front` (edge lights) and `extra` (pylon plus subfaction banner), written against footprint variables only (fw, fh, hw) because the validator accepts only those. Vehicles use `deck` for subfaction kit parts, and infantry use `gear`. Unit-specific gear is emitted from ops_after, not slots.gear, so the style gear slot still runs.
- structure.nec.relay, lance_rail_emplacement and aurora_microwave_array override slots `extra` (and `front` for the Relay) with empty arrays so the shared pylon does not stack on them.
- The Fen and Fjord carriers, Rapier, Archer, Ibex and Aster keep their archetype and get params plus ops_after, e.g. Fen and Fjord on veh_amphib with a custom telescoping-mast helper and a canted 4-tube barrel-part pack. Fjord and Fen use veh_amphib (6-wheel amphibious hull) instead of the stub's veh_rocket and veh_apc.
- Fjord's pack redeclares muzzle0_0..3 and turret/barrel parts next to the archetype's RWS. Charlemagne's telescoping barrel is only suggested by a fixed range-extension collar, because SLIDE_Z parts are not turret-nested and a sleeve detaches when the turret yaws.
- Aster keeps the air_ew saucer and carries a flat 2.2 x 1.2 array plate on a RADAR part above it, rather than replacing the dome.
- Subfaction overlays only appear on shared units and structures today. style_for_def returns `nec` for every unit.nec.*, so a roster overlay would need the change requested below.
- test_view_nec_recipes.gd checks tri caps for all 19 units in all 4 styles; overlay parts are tier 2 (LOD0 only) to stay under the caps.

**Tests:** Commands and results:
- `tools/gd test view_nec -q`: 6 passed, 0 failed, 170 checks. Covers hand-authored ids and overlay palette rules, LOD0/LOD1 triangle caps in all 4 NEC styles, palette against bible visual_direction (slate blue hue 205-235, white, amber hue 30-50, subfaction accents within 25 degrees and dE >= 8), team-plate contrast (each of the 12 lobby colours >= 25 dE from the plate gasket), team-mask area per unit, and sibling distinctness.
- `python3 tools/py/validate_recipes.py`: 0 errors for my ids. The only NEC warning is 'nec' also defined in styles.json (see cross-module requests).
- `tools/gd check --strict`: 0 errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings.
- `python3 tools/py/gen_recipe_stubs.py --check`: no drift, 170 hand-authored ids kept.
- `tools/gd test view -q`: 286 of 293 pass. The 7 failures are not mine: han, napc, sap and olm content, the structure archetype tests (olm and napc footprints), and `test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts` (the styles.json overlap, see requests).
- `tools/gd test view_recipes -q`: no NEC id over budget.
- `tools/gd test view_structure_archetypes -q`: nothing NEC fails.

Screenshots are in ~/Github-Projects/Meridia

**Known gaps:**
- Aster does not have a true flat dorsal array in place of the dome: the plate sits on the round radome.
- Charlemagne's +25 % barrel extension on deploy is not animated (see deviations).
- Infantry differ mostly by kit and pack, since the soldier macro fixes silhouette and helmet.
- The structure dialect is an overlay (pylon, edge lights, banner). Sloped white roofs and stretched halls would need archetype changes, because style slots cannot address roof height.
- Team colour azure/cyan on slate paint is weaker than on NAPC olive; the plate gasket carries it (measured dE vs slate: azure 39, slate colour id 9: 27).
- Team-mask area is asserted only in a wide 1.2-30 % band, and the Godot test does not measure silhouette IoU; the sibling and cross-faction check was by eye on the sheets (v1_vs_napc.png).
- Subfaction insignia 'emblem' values are data only; no code consumes `emblem` or `team_plate` yet.
- Sockets muzzle0_x on Fjord and Charlemagne collar are declared by me and not asserted against combat_loadouts.json.

**Cross-module requests:**
- View owner (VA2/ViewRecipeBook): style_for_def should return style_for_roster(owner_roster) for unit.<fac>.* and structure.<fac>.* when the owner's roster belongs to <fac>. Today unit.nec.* always gets the base `nec` style, so the Nordics, Eurocorps and Alpine overlays never appear on NEC's own units.
- styles.json owner: remove the `def`, `han`, `napc` and `nec` entries from game/data/recipes/styles.json once the F-* tasks land (keep `neutral`); test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts fails on them.
- Interpreter/archetype owners (RA/RB): allow archetype-namespaced style slots (e.g. `str_factory.front`) or unique slot names per class, since `roof` is used by vehicles and structures with different scopes. Also consider adding `kit_<id>` slot hooks to vehicle archetypes for subfaction kit parts.
- Validator/tooling owner: validate_recipes.py should include structure derived names (hh, ph, hcap) in the style-slot scope, or document them; I worked around it with local lets (n_pw, n_hh, n_hc).
- Mesh builder owner: SLIDE_Z and SLIDE_Y parts are not nested under TURRET, so a telescoping barrel or turret-mounted mast cannot follow the turret. A TURRET-nested slide part would fix Charlemagne. ViewMacrosStructures' sensor_mast also floats its head for travel above 0.5 m; a multi-section variant (my telescope helper in the recipes) would help.
- veh_howitzer declares params `carrier` and `crawler` that no op reads.

## F:napc

**Summary:** F-napc (VIEW-M8) done: styles/napc.json (faction style + napc.usa/canada/mexico overlays), 19 hand-authored NAPC unit recipes, Bulwark Cannon and Atlas Kinetic Array recipes, and the Peace Corps dialect of the 12 shared structure archetypes.
The dialect is apron props plus a fascia sign with the heater-shield emblem, delivered through style slots.
The palette is the art-direction bible palette exactly (dE < 1, tested). All 19 units and 12 shared structures build in all four styles (napc, napc.usa, napc.canada, napc.mexico) inside the triangle budgets.
Unique units read as variants of the unit they replace. Beaver has prow, sponsons and snorkel; Narwhal has sealed skirt, deck sponsons, wave-breaker and water-jets; Vanguard has back shields and a DEPLOY cover panel; Aguila has plate carrier and orange breach pack; Raptor has chines and cream/orange pylon tips; Condor has a sawtooth flying wing.
Two inspected rounds. Round 1 (sheets and top views) found tri/LOD1 overruns, low team-colour share, the Narwhal being a Guardian copy, a hinge-pivot bug and a floating beacon. Round 2 fixed these and added faction emblems on turrets, ship bows and structure fascias.
Blocker for in-game use of the subfaction kits: ViewRecipeBook.style_for_def ignores the owner's subfaction for unit.napc.*, so those kit parts only show once request 1 lands.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- STYLE FILE: game/data/recipes/styles/napc.json defines napc, napc.usa, napc.canada, napc.mexico. napc is ALSO still defined in styles.json (stale M2 entry); the faction file wins and V-RCP-12 warns. napc.palette = art_direction 5.3.1 authored values (base #465229, sec #DBCFA6, acc #F0621A, dark #262E19, metal #4A4F4B, glass #22404A, rubber #151515, light #FFE6B0, plate #1a1a18, concrete #807d75). Emblems: napc 'bar', usa 'chevron', canada 'ring', mexico 'triple_bar' (allowed set of V-RCP-11). Sub styles override only palette.acc (#C54600 / #FF504A / #CF7B00, equal to the bible), emblem and slots.
- STYLE SLOTS. napc: `front` (apron props, all structure archetypes call it), `extra` (crates, cable drum and emblem fascia sign, only on footprints >= 3x3) and `kit_emblem`. The name `roof` is deliberately NOT used: tank, APC and amphibious archetypes call a slot `roof` in a different scope. Structure slots compute their own lets (sw = fw*1.5-0.25, sh, sa, big) because the validator's style-slot scope has fw/fh but not the structure derives (hh, ap, pw). Sub styles add kit stamps drawn at the caller's local origin: kit_lift_ring, kit_nav_light_bar (usa); kit_waterline_band, kit_snorkel_stack, kit_float_sponson, kit_wave_breaker (canada); kit_slat_cage, kit_sandbag_roll, kit_riot_shield_rack (mexico). Sub styles also replace `front` with base front plus an abstract apron glyph sign (wing / anchor / three bars). Stamp arguments are passed as lets named ld_len and ld_wid (declared parameters of another archetype, so the validator accepts them in style slots); kit_wave_breaker also reads its hinge pivot from the lets dock_cx, pads_n, dock_cz because part pivots are NOT transformed by push. kit_emblem's ld_wid is the emblem height in m. Every kit stamp and prop is tier 2 (LOD0 only).
- UNIT RECIPES are `{archetype, params, slots, ops_after}`. Unique-unit kit parts are inline in the recipe, not style-dependent. Vehicles call the sub kits via slots. Archetypes used: Guardian and Narwhal veh_tank; Bastion veh_siege (twin, hull 5.0 x 2.6, tur_hw 1.22, 8 skirt tiles per side in the skirt slot); Sentinel veh_aa; Paladin veh_howitzer; Pathfinder and Beaver veh_apc (Beaver is NOT veh_amphib: it is the Pathfinder plus raised prow, cream sponsons, aft prop guards and snorkel); Falcon and Raptor air_jet (swept vs delta wing); Titan air_heli; Condor air_bomber; Riverwatch, Aegis and Liberty ship_patrol, ship_escort and ship_siege; Rifle and Vanguard inf_rifle; Javelin inf_at; Medic inf_support; Aguila inf_special.
- SQUAD SIZES: Rifle Squad n=4 (1396/1400 tris) and Vanguard Rifle Squad n=3 (1136 tris; four members plus the shield plates and the panel would not fit the 1400-triangle cap). Combat Medic keeps the archetype's cream backpack with orange bar and covers the archetype's hip-pouch cross with a plain orange strap (NAPC medics carry no cross). Aguila Breach Team has team-coloured shoulder guards because the archetype's large pack hides the back team plate from above.
- TRIANGLE BUDGETS: all 19 units within caps in all four styles (LOD0 / LOD1): Guardian 4686/2098, Narwhal 4502/2370, Bastion 4344/3144, Sentinel 4686/2784, Paladin 4324/2568, Pathfinder 3314/1916, Beaver 3418/1972, Rifle 1396/758, Vanguard 1136/660, Javelin 1304/732, Medic 1016/540, Aguila 1376/600, Falcon ~2030/1090, Raptor ~2360/1420, Titan ~2390/1260, Condor ~1190/770, Riverwatch ~1720/750, Aegis ~3400/1790, Liberty ~3220/1540. Structures: Bulwark 1416, Atlas 3548; shared structures with the napc dialect: generator/barracks 2 x 2 stay <= 3000 (dialect adds ~96 tris, crates/drum/sign only from 3x3 up).

**Tests:** ["tools/gd test view_napc -q -> 10 tests, 577 checks, all passed. Covers: 19 authored unit recipes + 2 structures + 4 styles; every unit x 4 styles builds without placeholder within LOD0/LOD1/height caps (spec 5.8.2); animated parts per archetype (+ DEPLOY on Paladin, Vanguard, Narwhal; member tagging on squads); shared structures + Atlas + Bulwark x 4 styles within footprint tri/height caps; muzzle sockets for every weapon of units_napc.json; palette equals the bible (dE < 1) and hue bands olive/cream/orange, sub accents equal to the bible, sibling accents dE >= 8; team-plate contrast for all 12 player colours (closest player colour to any faction paint dE > 4, at most 3 of the first 8 within dE 20 of olive or cream); team-masked top-view share 3.5-14 % (squads to 20 %) and dark mass >= 8 %; sibling content hashes differ, unique units not copies (IoU < 0.985), same-archetype silhouettes vs def/nec/pd models IoU <= 0.95; determinism and subfaction styles keep massing.", "tools/gd test view_sap view_napc -q -> 19 passed (a coincidental equal-tris pair between SAP shield_escort and NAPC Aegis was broken by adding a foredeck windlass).", "tools/gd test view -q (whole view domain) -> 2

**Known gaps:**
- Subfaction kit parts and insignia on faction units are invisible in-game until ViewRecipeBook.style_for_def is fixed (see request 1); today only *.shared.* defs and shared structures pick up napc.usa/canada/mexico. Unique units are unaffected (kit parts inline).
- Shared units (Collector, Engineer, MCV, Landing Transport) and the summons (UAV, cargo aircraft, pontoon) were not hand-authored: they keep the archetype stubs and already read as NAPC through the palette and kit (checked in H1.png).
- Emblems: heater-shield emblem is on tank/AA/howitzer turret cheeks, ship bows, Titan tail fin and structure fascia signs; not on Falcon, Raptor, Condor or the Beaver/Pathfinder (no triangle budget left on Guardian-class vehicles for a sub badge disc; the sub badge disc of 5.6.3 is not implemented).
- Structure dialect is apron level only (bollards, wheel stop, hook stand, crates, cable drum, fascia sign): the shared archetypes give no safe roof-level hook because the slot name `roof` collides with vehicle archetypes. The roof marking of the HQ (3 m) and beacon-on-tallest-point rely on the archetypes' own roof equipment.
- Not measured in the engine: in-render palette pixel clustering (P-1), plate dE00 from the rendered plate (P-2/P-3), area-mix percentages per role (P-6), emissive share (P-7), size cards within +-8 % (F-1: the skirt-based tank width already exceeds the mbt_t1 card in the archetype baseline). The test uses authored colours plus a top-projected team/dark/light share instead.
- Condor 'cream fades to 35 % alpha' and edge shimmer are shader/cloak effects, not recipe geometry; Narwhal water-jet ducts and Beaver prop guards are static (ROTOR spins about Y only).
- Sub-style kit budgets are respected only because the kit stamps are tier-2 minimal geometry; Guardian is 4686/5000 tris in the napc style and about 5000 in the usa/mexico styles (measured <= 5000 in the test).

**Cross-module requests:**
- ViewRecipeBook.style_for_def (VIEW-M2, model/view_recipe_book.gd) and the callers in view_world.gd: for unit.<fac>.* / structure.<fac>.* defs whose owner roster belongs to the same faction it returns the bare faction style, ignoring the owner's subfaction. Please return style_for_roster(owner_roster) when the def's faction equals the roster's faction (unit.napc.rifle_squad owned by roster.napc.canada -> napc.canada). Without it the accents, band/insignia signs and kit stamps of styles/napc.json never show on faction units.
- styles.json owner (VIEW-M2/T1): remove the stale `napc` entry (and def, han, nec, which other faction tasks also duplicate). styles/napc.json now defines the full style; test_view_recipes.test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts fails while the duplicates exist.
- Ship archetypes ship_patrol/escort/siege: their side team_panel calls sit at fb+0.03 with thickness 0.02, under the dark deck plate (top fb+0.045), so they are invisible. NAPC recipes add visible plates at fb+0.075; the archetypes should be fixed for all factions.
- tools/py/validate_recipes.py (and the interpreter's docs): style slots cannot use structure derives (hh, ap, pw...) or caller-provided names; a documented set of stamp argument names (e.g. kit_len, kit_wid, kit_px/py/pz) in the validator's style-slot scope would replace the ld_len/ld_wid/dock_cx/pads_n/dock_cz workaround. Also note that ViewMeshBuilder part pivots are not transformed by push, which the render spec does not state.
- Archetype `inf_special` (gear breach/sapper): the 'large' pack is taller than the back team plate, hiding the team colour from above (0.8 % top share on the Aguila); the pack could sit below the plate top or the plate be lifted.

## AB3:zones+summons+research

**Summary:** AB3 done: AB-07 zones and AB-08 summons/power glue are implemented and green. SimZoneSystem (stage 9) runs Dust Screen (two-sided), Trident, Horizon debris, unit and structure decoys, sensor puck, Wideband warm-up reveal, cover/shelter, repair stations, latched buff snapshots and warning markers. SimSummons/SimPowerFx cover UAV, pontoon, cargo aircraft, Tempest swarm, Dragonfall capsule-to-engine and the Lagos drone with respawn. All 48 powers and 8 superweapons apply. smoke_launcher, portable_cover, sensor_puck, decoy_spawn and summon_orbit work through CMD_USE_ABILITY. S07, S08, S14, S15, S16, S20, V2, V4, V5 are sim-level tests; a generated test checks all 40 research entries (~60 effects) in sim state. Cross-OS chain identical (3 platforms); soak clean.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- ENTRY POINTS FOR EC3B (all static unless noted; positions in sim units, times in ticks). world.zones.create_zone(zone_idx, pid, x, y, angle=0, until_tick=0, bind_eid=-1, radius=0, length=0, width=0, warmup=0, power_idx=-1, src_eid=-1, count=1, scatter_u=0, aux=0, shape=-1, extra_flags=0, override_bp=0) -> zone id or -1. until_tick is ABSOLUTE (0 = template duration, after warmup). radius/length/width override the template when >0 (length>0 on a disc template makes a capsule). warmup>0 = WARMUP state, EV_SCAN_WARNING for REVEAL zones. count/scatter_u/aux drive DECOY zones (scatter is a pure hash of aux, no RNG). Also world.zones.spawn_zone(zone_idx,pid,x,y,angle,until_tick,bind_eid) (spec form), end_zone(id, ZE_*), spawn_warning(kind,pid,x,y,angle,a,b,ticks), get_zone, active_zones (sorted by id, read-only), visible_to(z, viewer_pid), zone_of_body(eid), construction_blocked(cx,cy) and blocks_construction (the name SimPlacement calls), intercept_ordinary/intercept_packet (combat's names, unchanged).
- SimPowerFx wrappers: SimPowerFx.create_zone(world, zone_idx, pid, x, y, angle, until_tick, bind_eid, radius, length, width, warmup, power_idx, src_eid, count, scatter_u, aux); SimPowerFx.spawn_summon(world, def_idx, pid, x, y, life_ticks=0, angle=0, parent_eid=-1, src_idx=-1) -> eid (aircraft: ORBITER driver, flies in from 8 cells+orbit radius along `angle` and circles (x,y); ground defs: STATIC at (x,y)); SimPowerFx.apply_timed_effect(world, target_id, fx_idx, dur_ticks, src_pid, src_key) -> bool (same as world.abilities.apply_timed_effect). Low level: SimSummons.spawn(world, def_idx, pid, x, y, parent_eid, flags SM_*, life_ticks, driver SD_*, ax, ay, ar) -> eid or -1; SimSummons.spawn_swarm_drone(...), spawn_capsule(...); SimSummons.adopt for entities made elsewhere. Constants: SimZoneConsts (SM_*, SD_*, ZE_*, PW_*, EV_ZONE_SPAWNED 246, EV_ZONE_ENDED 247, EV_SUMMONED 248, EV_SUMMON_EXPIRED 249, EV_SWARM_LAUNCHED 256, EV_ENGINE_ASSEMBLED 257).
- POWERS: SimPowerFx.validate(world, power_idx, pid, x, y) -> PW_OK/PW_ERR_UNKNOWN/LIMIT/NO_SUMMON/OWNER (zone table room only); SimPowerFx.validate_target(world, power_idx, pid, target_eid) for Rapid Turnaround (own, listed structure def, F_POWERED); SimPowerFx.apply(world, power_idx, pid, x, y, angle, aux) -> PW_*. It runs every action in order. aux = one integer drawn ONCE by economy (decoy and shell scatter); for Rapid Turnaround aux = the chosen airfield's entity id. angle = line bearing (Concealed Crossing/Horizon) or summon approach bearing. Delayed zone actions (delay_t, Field Repair Drop, Expeditionary Workshop) are queued in world.zones.pend (hashed) and run in stage 9; fizzle_if_summon_lost = no zone when the body is dead at that tick. SimPowerFx.self_warned(world, power_idx) is true for Wideband Scan: economy must call apply() AT ONCE for it (the zone has its own 40-tick WARMUP + EV_SCAN_WARNING), and after the warning for every other power. Global effects: Treaty/Central Priority/Reserve Bandwidth -> SimAuraSystem.set_window (bp and radius merged per group, until = max); Mobilization Order and Recovery Priority salvage time -> economy set_knob_temp (K_PROD_RATE_BARRACKS/FACTORY_BP, K_SALVAGE_TICKS); Recovery Priority speed, and any unit stat_mod/resist_mod window -> timed effects on existing units plus SimPlayerFx windows so units spawned later get it (hook in SimZoneSystem.on_spawn); Joint Landing -> applied by SimAbilitySystem.on_disembark (calls SimPowerFx.on_disembark); Rapid Turnaround -> STAT_REARM_RATE lease on the chosen airfield.
- SUPERWEAPONS: SimPowerFx.apply_superweapon(world, sw_idx, pid, x, y, angle, hub_x, hub_y, aux, owner_eid=0) -> PW_*. KINETIC_VOLLEY/BUNKER_BUSTER/EMP_BURST = strategic PK_STRIKE per packet (offset_x rotated by angle, packet.delay_t) with warhead built by SimPowerFx.warhead_for (SimCombatWarhead.from_packet appended to the owner's combat table, cached per (pid, source, packet) in world.zones.wh_cache); RAIL_STRIKE (Horizon) = 3 strikes plus a DEBRIS disc (radius = packet radius) created at each impact tick (the first at once, the rest via pend); BEAM_SWEEP = spawn_sweep; DRONE_SWARM = 24 drones on a 1.5-cell ring around (hub_x, hub_y), angle k*170, EV_SWARM_LAUNCHED; ENGINE_DROP = 3 capsules at min(2.5 cells, radius), angles aux+k*1365 (aux = base drawn once), engines after unfold_t; INTERCEPT_ZONE = create_zone. Support-power strikes (Tremor, Counterbattery Mission, Counterlaunch) spawn PK_ARC shells from 48 cells west of the target (warhead.packet=0, so Trident sees them; 1 charge each).
- SEMANTICS: a zone is NOT a kernel ZONE entity (those are untargetable in combat); it is a SimZone record in world.zones.zones. Zone members hold each template effect as a SimStatus timed effect with src_key FXK(SRC_ZONE, zone_idx) for the zone's remaining time and lose it the moment they leave (membership pass every 4 ticks, repair every 10); overlapping zones of one template share the entry (never stack); BUFF zones are latched snapshots (never re-evaluated, effects survive zone end). Cover/shelter: 2 occupants max (lowest ids, incumbents keep place), must be standing still, builder-only when the ability has pack_t>0, damage reduction = the ability's resist_bp (override_bp) rather than the template's. Dragonfall capsule becomes the engine as a NEW entity (new eid, position/facing/hp fraction kept, EV_ENGINE_ASSEMBLED p0=engine p1=capsule) instead of a def swap in place (the kernel has no def-swap). Zone bodies (puck hp 60, olm mobile workshop hp 600) are summon units flagged SM_BODY|SM_SHOOTABLE|SM_NO_VISION_GRANT; their def is summon.shared.sensor_puck / summon.ae.sensor_puck / summon.olm.repair_drone_station when authored, else summon.napc.pontoon with hp overridden to the zone's hp. Decoys: roster unit of the owner matching the template tag mask (lowest tier, then highest cost) or the template structure; F_DECOY, hp = template hp, weapons locked forever, die with the zone. Unit abilities: point (x,y) means 'none' when <=0 both (so SimCmd.use_ability defaults work); launch range 12 cells; cooldown starts at use; state in the ability slot (SL_STATE SA_*, SL_T_END, SL_AUX0 = zone id / drone eid); timers TK_BUILD/TK_COOLDOWN/TK_RESPAWN dispatch to SimZoneAbilities.on_timer. Lagos: the carrier gets a summon_orbit slot (AK_SUMMON_ORBIT, extra slot count) and an ATTACHED drone (F_TETHERED, scripted orbit at 1.5 cells); the drone heals (free, 1%/s, nearest damaged friendly infantry, pulse every 10 ticks) because economy's repair order only supports paid repair; while the drone is down the carrier's repair slot is SF_SUSPENDED and auto-cast is switched off; respawn after summon_respawn_t (1800).
- STATE/HASH: new hashed state: SimZoneSystem.hash_state (next_id, n_intercept, every SimZone, inter_slots, pend, attach_q), SimCompSummon (all fields), SimPlayerFx (allocated for every player in SimZoneSystem.init_world, so every world checksum changed). Derived, not hashed: sum_ids, wh_cache, selector mask cache. Zone/summon/power checks are part of SimAbilitySystem.invariants() (SimInvariants runs them).
- FIX sim_ability_system.gd: _on_timer dispatches TK_BUILD/COOLDOWN/RESPAWN to SimZoneAbilities; ability_param folds active timed PARAM_MODs (SimStatus.fold_params, with stack_group de-dup); on_disembark calls SimPowerFx.on_disembark first; request_pack calls SimZoneAbilities.request_pack; new order_gate rejects player orders (no OF_AUTO) for DF_UNCONTROLLABLE entities; invariants() also validates zones; debug_validate expiry check was off by one (< instead of <=). FIX abilities/sim_status.gd: has_from, fold_params, end_mask also reads breaks_on, ends_on_move and STATIONARY condition (timed effect ends on movement), DISABLE with on_expire locks weapons for fx.duration_t when its window expires. FIX abilities/sim_ability_cmds.gd: USE_ABILITY rejects SF_SUSPENDED slots. Test edits: tests/sim/test_sim_types.gd (SimCompSummon/SimPlayerFx are filled components), tests/abil/test_abil_real_data.gd (Lagos carrier has one extra slot).

**Tests:** tools/gd check --strict (whole game): 1195 files, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings, OK. tools/gd test abil -q: 208 of 208 passed (2620 checks); new: test_ab3_zones 26, test_ab3_summons 12, test_ab3_powers 23 (generated every-power and every-superweapon tests with area/duration/effect-held oracles), test_ab3_research 1 (generated, all 40 entries, ~60 effects: 7 stats, 2 resists, 18+3+1 param mods incl. 13 economy knobs, 7 grants, 1 flag, 10 conditional effects bound and 7 of them activated), test_ab3_determinism 4. test sim -q 140/140, combat -q 119/119, econ passed. Full suite (before final edits): 1713/1724; failures outside my area: ai/test_ai_strategy hunt-op (still fails with the zone stage replaced by a stub, so not mine), ui_boot, 8 view recipe tests. Determinism: xplat_determinism.py xplat_ab3.gd IDENTICAL on macos-arm64, linux-x86_64, linux-arm64 (80 hashes, re-run after the last edits); xplat_ab2.gd still IDENTICAL (65). Soak: soak_rosters.gd 96 matches errors=0 and the digests of macOS and linux arm64 are identical for all 96; soak_units.gd 32 rosters errors=0 stuck=0.

**Known gaps:**
- Missing data: summon.nec.survey_drone and summon.sap.recon_balloon do not exist, so those two powers use summon.napc.uav as a stand-in (works, wrong model/faction). No body defs for the sensor puck or the Olm repair-drone station: the NAPC pontoon def stands in (view will draw a pontoon).
- docs/RULES_DATA_NOTES.md says Floating Workshop has delay_s 5; the shipped data has no delay for it (only Field Repair Drop and Expeditionary Workshop delay 100 ticks); implemented as the data says.
- Aura windows: Reserve Bandwidth and Central Priority share one command-field window record, so they keep the later end tick for both when they overlap. SimPlayerFx does not expose player_ability_param (windows live in the aura table and economy knobs).
- Rapid Turnaround uses the STAT_REARM_RATE lease on the chosen airfield; economy's K_REARM_RATE_BP knob is untouched. Condition IN_ZONE is not pushed to members. Survey Network's highlight_salvageable_wrecks is only a def flag for the view (wrecks have no vision component).
- Conditional research effects shared_fire_solutions (relay field), forward_fire_control and observer_network (target-near) are checked as bound only in the generated test; their activation is covered by test_ab2_auras.
- Diagnostic counters stat_passes/stat_members exist in SimZoneSystem; no zone or summon debug dump in SimWorld.dump_state.

**Cross-module requests:**
- DATA (units sheets): author summon.nec.survey_drone, summon.sap.recon_balloon (sight/detect 6 cells, life 15 s / 20 s, shootable), summon.shared.sensor_puck (static, harmless, hp 60) and summon.olm.repair_drone_station (static, hp 600); SimSummons.body_def picks them by these ids automatically. Decide whether summon.pd.decoy_transport stays (zone.decoy_transport uses roster units).
- ECONOMY (EC3B): drive support powers through SimPowerFx.validate/validate_target/apply and superweapons through apply_superweapon exactly as documented in DEVIATIONS; call apply() at activation for powers where SimPowerFx.self_warned() is true; pass one pre-drawn aux int; use world.zones.spawn_warning for the marker. Economy's SimCompTemp is unused: the summon record is SimCompSummon (e.summon).
- UI/VIEW: draw world.zones.active_zones() (fields per SimZone; capsule = segment ax,ay-bx,by with radius = half width; charges for Trident; warn_vis for warnings; zones.visible_to(z, pid)); events 246-249, 256-257 are new; zone bodies and decoys are ordinary entities (F_DECOY: enemy decoys show as their look-alike until SimVision.decoy_identified).
- AI/UI: send CMD_USE_ABILITY with x,y = -1 (or 0,0) for 'at the unit'; cover/decoy/puck/smoke slots report readiness via SimAbilitySystem.slot_ready.
- COMBAT (optional): a real def swap (capsule to engine in place) would keep the eid; today the engine is a new entity.

## F:sap

**Summary:** SAP (VIEW-M8) is done: styles/sap.json (sap plus india/thailand/pakistan overlays), 19 authored unit recipes (13 baseline plus 6 unique), 2 authored faction structures, and the Protectorate dialect of the 12 shared structure archetypes, delivered as style slots in styles/sap.json. Vehicles carry the bible signatures: stepped sand/indigo layered plates, saffron-footed DEPLOY_Z braces that swing out to the ground in the action pose, and paddle interception sensors with lilac slits. Every unique unit is a visible variant of the unit it replaces. The tests, recipe validator for sap ids and lint pass. Shared structure roofs and towers still read almost fully indigo in game and need an archetype change (see CROSS_MODULE_REQUESTS). Screenshots are in /private/tmp/claude-501/<project>/fc5c5792-a96e-458a-8c9a-611a38233495/scratchpad/fsap_work/shots/.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Style ids: `sap`, `sap.india`, `sap.thailand`, `sap.pakistan` in styles/sap.json. The subfaction styles extend `sap` and change only `palette.acc` (bible sub accents #CC8600 / #FF9D60 / #D1B40B), `emblem` (ring / bar_notch / diamond) and slot content. Unique units always use the base `sap` style because `style_for_def` maps unit.sap.* to the faction style, so the subfaction kit-bash parts live inside the unique units' own recipes. Only shared structures and shared units pick up the subfaction overlay.
- Palette follows the art-direction bible exactly, mapped base=primary sand #CDB37A, sec=secondary indigo #4B50A8, acc=saffron #F4A521, dark=#2F3480, metal #3C3E48, glass #1B2350, rubber #141414, light #FFF2CC. plate=#12121A and concrete=#9A917C are my choices. This differs from the older napc/nec entries in styles.json, which use slightly different hexes.
- Kit values in `sap`: tank dictionary from art_direction 5.5.0 (len 3.5, hull_w 2.06, hull_top 1.2, glacis 0.35, turret_n 8, brake muzzle, skirt layered, roof sensors, deck braces). Units that pick their own paddle/brace layout override deck to `none`. Vehicles use recipe param `skirt: none` plus a recipe `skirt` slot (custom bevel-0 stepped skirt), which is about 190 triangles cheaper per vehicle than the macro's layered skirt.
- Style slots `extra`, `roof` and `front` are the Protectorate structure dialect. They are also the slot names of veh_tank, veh_apc and veh_amphib (roof). Every sap unit on those three archetypes therefore overrides `roof` in its own recipe. A future sap unit on them without a `roof` slot would inherit the structure paddle-mast slot and fail to compile. The structure slots re-derive pw/ph/cap with a `let`, because the validator's style-slot scope has fw/fh but not pw, ph, hh or bz.
- Structure recipes structure.sap.* keep archetype and kit params (str_defense_adv/bastion_tower, str_superweapon/three_masts) and override the roof/front/extra slots (extra carries sand cladding, team roof plate, lilac lamps, pennant). The generic dialect slots are therefore not applied to these two.
- Paddle sensors are RADAR parts with extra 0.25, so they turn slowly. Nothing in the shader can sweep +-25 degrees (art direction 5.5.8), and on structures they are static and face the camera. Deploy braces are DEPLOY_Z parts, param 0.5 -/+ k/2 per side, k=0.95. Deployed pose was verified visually (foot reaches the ground).
- Inf recipes add member-scoped extras through a for-loop in ops_after that mirrors the archetype's own member placement (radius 0.36-0.44 and jitter). Any change to the inf_* archetype layout would need those loops updated.
- Shield Rifle Squad is n=3 (kit shield at n=4 exceeds the 1.4k squad budget). Watchpost Recon Team uses gadget `echo` (both members carry scope + antenna, no radio pack) so both team patches stay visible; tripod optic sits in the `gear` slot.
- New test scene game/tests/visual/sap_sheet.tscn is a copy of recipe_gallery with --hover=0, --ty and --face options.

**Tests:** ["python3 tools/py/validate_recipes.py --limit 400 | grep sap -> no output, so no sap error or warning. The tree-wide run still reports other agents' errors (nec/olm/def/han style slots).", "tools/gd test test_view_sap_recipes -> 9 tests, 9 passed, 0 failed (493 checks, about 11 s). Covers: 19 units + 2 structures + 4 styles exist; every recipe builds a real model in sap and the 3 subfaction styles with LOD0/LOD1 triangle and height caps; the 12 shared structures in all 4 styles (2x2 3.0k cap, 8k for larger footprints, +2.5 percent grace for subfaction skins); muzzle sockets for every weapon in units_sap.json; palette against art-direction bible style.json (dE < 1) and sub accents; player-colour contrast; occlusion-aware team-mask share and dark mass; sibling and cross-faction geometry differs; deterministic hashes.", "Team-plate contrast: none of the 12 player colours has dE under 12 to the faction paint. The closest are amber vs saffron 12.2, azure vs indigo 17.6 and violet vs indigo 19.2. The plate's dark gasket plus light hairline separates them. Team-masked share is 3.6-11 percent on vehicles and ships (bible: 5-12), 5-21 percent on squads. The bible's 4-12 percent is checked 

**Known gaps:**
- Structure roofs and towers are indigo: the shared str_* archetypes colour roof plates and tower blocks with palette `sec`, which is indigo for SAP (cream for NAPC). In the in-game shot (match2.png) the base reads as roughly 80 percent royal blue instead of the bible's 34/30 sand/indigo mix. What I could do in style slots (sand terraces, buttresses, wall cladding on the two landmarks) is on ground/wall level only.
- Bible dialect 'sand over indigo' is inverted on shared structures because of the same archetype colour choice: sand below, indigo above.
- Subfaction differences on shared structures are subtle: band patterns (block/wave/dash, 0.09-0.16 m) on side terraces, plus small kit-bash parts on footprints larger than 2x2 (India layer plate + pennant, Thailand bollards + float bolsters, Pakistan periscope mast + rail cover). Emblem geometry (ViewEmblemGeometry) is not implemented, only the emblem tag in the style. 2x2 shared structures in subfaction skins are up to 3.6 percent over the 3.0k class tris (the vanilla sap look is under it).
- summon.sap.recon_balloon has no recipe. The summon def does not exist yet, only the power action, and adding a recipe file would require an index.json regeneration.
- No icon bake (M9) or wreck meta tuning: recipe meta.icon is set only on the tank recipes.
- Infantry back-patch team share is 5-21 percent because of the soldier macro; the bible's 5-12 percent applies to vehicles, and the test allows up to 22 percent for squads.
- The recipes were generated by scratchpad Python scripts (fsap_work/units_*.py, gen.py, style_sap.py) that are not in the repo. The JSON files are the source of truth.

**Cross-module requests:**
- RC (structure archetypes str_*.json): expose the roof/tower colour role, for example kit defaults `roof_col`/`tower_col` = "sec" in every str_* archetype, so light-hull factions (SAP, OLM) can set `roof_col: "base"`. Then SAP structures read sand-dominant from the RTS camera. After that, add `roof_col: base` to styles/sap.json kit.
- tools/py/validate_recipes.py (RA): the style-slot scope should include the common structure derive names (pw, ph, hh, bz, bd, bk, bw, k, hcap, ry, ...) or say clearly that style slots must re-derive them. NEC's style slots currently fail V-RCP-05 for that reason.
- ViewMeshBuilder / shader owner (VA1): a sweeping part kind (yaw oscillation of about +-25 degrees at 0.3 Hz, no full rotation) for interception paddles. RADAR only spins fully.
- AB3/econ owner: when summon.sap.recon_balloon is added, author its recipe (sum_balloon archetype) and regenerate index.json.
- Other faction owners: styles/def.json, han.json, napc.json and nec.json duplicate ids already in styles.json (V-RCP-12 warning and test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts fails); remove the ids from styles.json.

## F:pd

**Summary:** VIEW-M8 for Pacific Dominion is done. All 19 unit recipes are hand-authored, so no stub is left; the 2 faction structures and the 6 summons are authored too. styles/pd.json holds the faction style plus pd.australia, pd.indonesia and pd.japan.
Palette is the art bible 5.3.1 set (cerulean #1791BE, white #F1F4F6, coral #FF6F5B). The sub-styles change only the accent colour, a wave/diag/pinstripe flank band, a ring emblem with 1/2/3 pips, and one kit-bash part (recon pod / grab rails / sensor ball). The shared structures get a blue quay base, a coral rail with bollards and life ring, and a painted faction ring on the apron.
PD signatures modelled: coral waterline bands, white flotation sponsons, boat prows, drone pads, fold hinges, snorkel stacks with coral caps, white turret caps with coral rings.
Round 1 was the first full pass, rendered and inspected. Round 2 fixed what I saw: turret-roof clutter, Outrider outriggers that stood extended at rest, tri budgets and squad sizes, team-plate area, and a palette that was too dark. Round 3 fixed the mirrored Osprey rotor tips that floated free, added the Petrel spine stripe, and added the structure apron decal.
Tests: `tools/gd test view_pd_recipes` passes 10/10 (510 checks) and the recipe validator reports 0 errors. Screenshots are in <repo>/docs/shots/pd/; the key ones are listed under tests.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- STYLE IDS AND CONTENT: styles/pd.json defines pd, pd.australia, pd.indonesia and pd.japan (schema meridian.styles/1). The palette is exactly art_direction 5.3.1 (acc #ff6f5b, base #1791be, dark #0f3a63, glass #173a56, light #f4fbff, metal #3e4548, rubber #151515, sec #f1f4f6), plus concrete #6f8797 (blue quay) and plate #0c1822. This is NOT the render-spec 5.8.4 hex set (#1a5c99 etc.) that styles.json used for the older four factions; the art bible is authoritative and test_view_pd_recipes enforces dE < 1 against it. Sub-styles extend pd and override only palette.acc (#d94e3e, #ff6c7f, #ef7d3d) and slots; emblem is 'ring' everywhere.
- PD KIT VALUES (veh_tank params, reused by name across archetypes): len 3.6, hull_w 1.72, hull_top 1.06, glacis 1.1, track_x 0.78, track_w 0.32, wheel_n 4, wheel_r 0.27, turret_n 10, turret_rot 18, turret_hw 0.66, turret_hl 0.72, turret_h 0.34, turret_top 0.74, muzzle sleeve, deck drone_pad, skirt none (sponsons are modelled by the recipes, not by the skirt macro), roof cupola. The tank recipes override roof with 'snorkel' (an empty case) and supply their own roof slot.
- STYLE SLOT NAMES (new, PD-only): the validator checks style slots stand-alone, so the slots are per archetype family and carry their own anchors: band_<fam> (solid coral band in pd, wave / diag / pinstripe in the subs), pat_<fam> (empty in pd, pattern in the subs; used by hull_ship-based units that already have a coral stripe), emb_<fam> (ring emblem, plus 1/2/3 pips in the subs) and top_<fam> (empty in pd; recon pod / grab rails / sensor ball in the subs). fam is one of veh_tank, veh_amphib, veh_aa, veh_howitzer, veh_hovercarrier, air_jet, air_heli, ship_patrol, ship_escort, ship_carrier. Structure slots 'front' (coral quay rail with door gap) and 'extra' (life ring, bollards, painted faction ring; the subs replace 'extra' to add pips). pd defines NO 'roof' slot, so shared structures get no roof dressing.
- SQUAD SIZES (visual only): the inf_* archetypes already cost 1.3-1.4 k tris at 4 soldiers, so the tri cap left no room for gear. Ranger Marine n=3, Island Raider n=3, Harpoon Team n=2, Reef Technician n=2. The art size card says 4 for inf_line.
- HOWITZERS: Breaker and Outrider use veh_howitzer with skirt none, trk_w 0.12 and trk_x 0.6, so the archetype's tracks sit hidden behind four cheap 8-sided WHEEL parts per side. hull_wid is 2.0. Outrider's DEPLOY_Z stabiliser legs are authored in the stowed (hanging) pose: right side param 0.857, left 0.143, deploy = swing out.
- SHINANO: the siege module is a static half-raised barrel on the turret roof (15 degrees, hazard hinge), not a DEPLOY part, because shader part transforms do not nest and a DEPLOY part on a turret would stop yawing. meta.deploy = 'pose'.
- KANCIL: the bow ramp is a DEPLOY part authored closed (param 0.143), so deploy = 1 lowers it forward and flat.
- TILTROTOR: Osprey keeps the tilt archetype's static nacelles. Coral rotor tips are a second ROTOR part per nacelle; the right-hand one uses mirrored angles (s*120*i) because the archetype mirrors the nacelle.
- SUMMONS summon.pd.* (decoy transport, patrol aircraft, workshop aircraft, three drone wings) are no longer stubs: PD trim only, no new geometry families.
- TEST FILE test_view_pd_recipes.gd (adapted from the SAP one) uses two relaxed caps that come from the shared archetypes themselves: veh_aa LOD1 3200 (archetype baseline 2976), and 1x1/2x2 shared structures LOD0 3300 (generator / barracks archetypes already build 3188 / 3112 in every style). It adds test_palette_matches_the_bible_visual_direction (hue / saturation classes of the three named colours read from data/bible/meridian_factions.json).
- AUTHOR TOOLING: recipes and style are generated by tools/py/gen_pd_recipes/gen_all.py (idempotent; JSON is the source of truth and a regeneration overwrites the recipe files). tests/visual/pd_sheet.tscn is a copy of han_sheet with --ids / --styles / --nohover / --face options; the V-1 sheets were rendered with it.

**Tests:** ["`python3 tools/py/validate_recipes.py --strict`: 0 errors. Its only warnings are V-RCP-12 notices for def / han / napc / nec being defined in both styles.json and styles/, none for pd.", "`tools/gd test view_pd_recipes`: 10 tests passed, 0 failed (510 checks). Coverage: 19 unit recipes are authored (no stub) and build in pd, pd.australia, pd.indonesia and pd.japan; per-model LOD0 / LOD1 tri and height budgets; muzzle socket per weapon of units_pd.json; palette dE < 1 against the art bible and the bible's visual_direction; team-plate contrast for all 12 player colours (closest raw pair dE 19.9, colour 0 vs coral); team-masked top area 4-14 percent and dark mass >= 10 percent per model; sibling and cross-faction content hashes differ; builds deterministic; sub-styles keep the structure massing.", "`tools/gd test view_recipes`: 12 of 13 pass. The failure, test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts, is the def / han / napc / nec duplication and is not PD.", "`tools/gd test view -q`: 291 of 294 pass. The 3 failures are not PD: the style-conflict test above, SAP's own team-share test, and test_view_structure_archetypes (napc-style 3x3 generator / barracks at 3304 / 3252 tris).", "`tools/g

**Known gaps:**
- Animation-only intent that the shader cannot express: Storm AA's six-tube box sits at its 22 degree rest elevation and does not fold flat; Osprey nacelles are static (no tilt hinge); Shinano's siege module does not unfold.
- The blue-on-blue case is the weakest team-plate read: azure and cyan players against the PD hull separate only by the dark gasket. The tests pass (dE 34 and 22.5 to the paint), but on-screen it is muted (team_plate_colour_1.png).
- Ships were only checked on the flat lineup ground, not over the water shader.
- kit_top anchors (recon pod / grab rails / sensor ball on shared units) are eyeballed per family and can sit next to the archetype's own deck fittings on Storm AA and the escort. Infantry and structures get no kit-bash part; structure subs only differ by accent colour plus the apron ring pips, and the ring is often hidden under barracks sandbags.
- Wake / Kancil hover skirts and the fan guards are the archetype's black rubber. A white skirt (art card) would need a palette-independent colour in veh_amphib.
- The hive-tower 'Tempest Swarm Hub' is 12.2 m tall, inside the 14 m cap but it fills the frame at the default camera. The sea-spear folded mast is barely visible from the RTS camera.

**Cross-module requests:**
- validate_recipes.py (tools/py owner): style slots are validated stand-alone with every archetype default plus a few derived names, so a shared kit slot cannot read lets that the calling recipe defines. That forced 40 per-family slots. Please validate style slots in the scope of the recipes that call them, or declare slot scope contracts.
- veh_aa archetype (RB): op 26 calls {"slot":"skirt"} once at root, unmirrored, after the mirror_x that already calls it, so a style or recipe skirt slot is drawn twice on +x. PD avoids the slot (skirt none plus ops_after sponsons).
- veh_amphib, veh_apc and veh_tank share the slot name 'roof' with all 15 structure archetypes. A style 'roof' slot therefore cannot be structure-only. Please give the structure archetypes a distinct slot name (for example roof_struct).
- Shared structure archetypes: str_generator and str_barracks already build 3188 / 3112 LOD0 tris (cap 3000) in every style, and inf_* squads at n=4 already cost 1.3-1.4 k of the 1.4 k cap. Please trim the archetypes or raise the caps.
- Render/shader: a DEPLOY / DEPLOY_Z part cannot ride on a TURRET part (transforms do not nest). A nested-transform part kind, or a second mount whose barrel elevates 0..90 degrees and is driven by the siege weapon, would let Shinano's siege module unfold with the turret.
- Test owners: test_view_structure_archetypes builds the shared archetypes in the napc style and now fails at 3304 / 3252 tris for the 3x3 generator / barracks; test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts fails because def, han, napc and nec exist in both styles.json and styles/*.json (remove them from styles.json).

## F:han

**Summary:** Han Empire (VIEW-M8 F-han) is done. All 19 units (13 baseline + 6 unique) and both faction structures (Dragon Tooth Launcher, Dragonfall Field Foundry) are hand-authored recipes. game/data/recipes/styles/han.json holds the faction style plus han.china, han.vietnam and han.cambodia overlays. The shared structures read as Han through the palette plus a style slot `extra` that adds two porcelain standards per building (sensor crown on 3x3 and larger, crimson lantern on 1x1 and 2x2).
Signature parts: slow-spinning RADAR sensor crowns (Link Operator, walkers, tanks, Firefly, ships, Jade Carrier, structure standards), parked quad drones on rack cradles, eye lenses on the unmanned Firefly and Nest, and a diamond emblem on the tank skirts.
Unique units are variants of what they replace: Guard Tank (+10 percent hull, bare long gun with fume extractor, porcelain shoulder plates), Long Walker (wider stance, outrigger ring frame, rising crown-tine extension), Canopy Ranger (brown leaf mantle), Reed Skimmer, Lotus Tender (4-petal dock ring, manipulator arm), Mekong Engineer (folding boom antenna).
Validators and my own tests pass. Two `tools/gd test view` failures remain that are not mine, listed under known gaps.
Visual rounds: R1 baseline stubs, R2 all recipes drafted, R3 fixes after inspecting shots (details under deviations), R4 final V-1 sheets and comparisons.
Screenshots are in <repo>/docs/shots/han/.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Palette: styles/han.json uses the art-direction values from style.json palette.han, not the VA2 styles.json values. base #2d8862, dark #16483a, sec #ece7d9, metal #3a403e, glass #153b38, light #f2fff6 (cool lamp), plate #0f1614. acc is #ab1d2c, a slightly deeper crimson than the art-direction #B81F2E because it renders pink under ACES. Sub accents follow art_direction 5.6.1 exactly: china #de4649, vietnam #bc044e, cambodia #ab360a. Emblems are diamond, hex_notch, triple_bar, ring.
- Style ids: han, han.china, han.vietnam, han.cambodia, in styles/han.json. This file is merged over the old styles.json `han` entry, which is superseded (see cross-module requests).
- Two style slots are used. `extra` (base han style) is compiled inside every str_* archetype and uses only the injected footprint variables fw/fh (this is what the validator scope allows). It is not a recipe-level slot, so it does not depend on archetype internals. structure.han.* recipes set slots.extra to [] so they carry no duplicate standards. `deck` in the three subfaction styles exists only on veh_tank, veh_apc and veh_amphib and uses only hl and hw. Subfaction `kit.skirt` is layered for china and fabric for vietnam.
- The interpreter does not apply `kits`, so the kit-bash parts (ID plates, leaf clips, hose racks) are the style `deck` slots.
- Banner/Lance/Link Operator/Ranger/Mekong Engineer use their archetype's `gear` slot from the recipe (member-attached parts; the style gear slot is not used).
- unit.han.canopy_ranger has n=3 members. A 4-man rifle squad with a round helmet already costs 1376 triangles, so the leaf mantles would break the 1400 infantry cap. All other squads keep the archetype defaults.
- unit.han.firefly_aa_drone uses skirt=slab and turret_n=8, and its crown is tier 2. Otherwise LOD1 goes past the 2800 medium cap (the archetype itself was already at 2808).
- unit.han.jade_carrier and unit.han.lotus_drone_tender set deck=none. The Han kit's drone_rack deck would otherwise double the rack.
- Team-plate additions in recipes: track-cover stripes on tanks, Firefly and Nest; cab-roof plate on Jade Carrier and Lotus Tender; foredeck plate on Reed; wing-top plates on both walkers; ring plates on Long; aft side plates on Jade Escort; roof plates on Dragon Tooth. Measured occlusion-aware plan-view team share is 3-13 percent for vehicles (infantry 11-16 percent, an archetype property).
- Rounds: R3 removed the Guard `heavy` porcelain slab (hid the team plate). Fenders were moved from x=track_x to hw+0.1 (they were hidden under the hull deck). Lotus petals were enlarged. Ship drone racks were moved (they were hidden under life rafts). The Silkwing default plan replaced the small 'drone' plan. Structure standards were reworked so they no longer overhang the plinth or break the 3000-triangle cap of 2x2 buildings. A crown-topped standard replaced the lantern on 3x3 and larger buildings.
- All Han recipes carry meta.han_gen=true and no meta.stub, so gen_recipe_stubs.py keeps them (`--check --only '*han*'` reports 23 hand-authored kept, 0 stale). The 3 summon.han.* recipes are still stubs on sum_capsule/sum_engine/sum_drone_swarm and render fine in Han style.

**Tests:** ["python3 tools/py/validate_recipes.py --strict -> 0 errors, 4 warnings, all 'style X is also defined in styles.json' (def, han, napc, nec). Only the han one is mine.", "tools/gd check --strict -> 0 script errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings.", "tools/gd test test_view_han_recipes -q -> 10 tests, 10 passed, 248 checks (last run had a mid-flight sim parse error from another agent's files; it did not affect the result). It covers: authored (no stub) recipes; roster style resolution and no new palettes for subfactions; all 21 Han recipes build without a placeholder in the 4 Han styles within LOD0/LOD1/height caps (BUDGET table copied from test_view_archetypes); all shared unit/structure recipes build in the 4 Han styles (structures 3000/8000 triangle rule); muzzle{m}_0 socket per weapon of units_han.json; signature RADAR/DEPLOY parts; unique units differ from what they replace; palette check (jade/crimson/porcelain hue windows, bible visual_direction text, within delta E 8 of art_direction palette.han, sub accents within delta E 3 of 5.6.1 and >8 apart); team-plate contrast (all 12 lobby colours >25 delta E from the plate gasket, only low-contrast ids 2/9/11 near the paint); occlusi

**Known gaps:**
- Shared structures get palette, `extra` standards and material only. Roof-level Han dressing (sensor crown on the roof, tiered module stacks, drone racks on the roof) is not possible without a roof-height variable common to all str_* archetypes (see requests). Only the two Han structures have tiered eaves, crowns and racks.
- The style slot `roof` collides by name between vehicles and structures, so it is unused.
- Firefly keeps the archetype's hull RWS (reads slightly manned); the Reed Skimmer keeps an open cab with white rim and rear headlights, because veh_lightarty's skimmer chassis is built mirrored; the Nest cab is covered with a jade shroud plus eye lens as a workaround.
- Subfaction kit-bash appears only on veh_tank / veh_apc / veh_amphib bodies (Ox Tank and Jade Carrier in Han rosters). Shared infantry, ships, aircraft and other vehicle archetypes get only accent, emblem and skirt kit. On tanks the flank pieces sit behind the skirts.
- Emblem stamps exist only on the Ox and Guard tank skirts; no sub-badge disc and no emblems on other units.
- The Dragon Command Walker's 5-cell ground ring is FX, not model. Long walker deploy is a static-tine crown extension (SLIDE_Y) plus outrigger pads, not tines lengthening in place.
- Team share is 3-4 percent (design floor 4 percent) for Jade Carrier, Dragon/Long walker and Mekong Engineer, and 2.5 percent for the Dragon Tooth roof plate; the last one is at the structure minimum.
- Summons summon.han.* stay generic archetype stubs (no Han-specific ops).

**Cross-module requests:**
- Data/render owner (styles.json): delete the `han` entry from game/data/recipes/styles.json now that styles/han.json defines it fully. The same applies to def/napc/nec. This clears the validator warnings and the failing test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts.
- RC (structure archetypes): add a derive var common to every str_* archetype, for example `roof_y` (main roof height) plus `roof_cx/roof_cz`. Faction `extra`/`roof` slots could then place crowns, tiers and drone racks on roofs. Also give structure slots names that cannot collide with the vehicle `roof` slot, for example `s_roof`, `s_front`, `s_extra`.
- RB (veh_rocket): implement the declared but unused `unmanned` param (eye lens instead of the cab). Han's Nest and other drone launchers currently need a shroud workaround.
- RB (veh_lightarty): the skimmer chassis has its cab, windshield and headlights at +Z while the pod fires -Z, and the cab overlaps the launcher pivot at pz=hl-0.72. It looks mirrored and should be fixed; unmanned skimmers also need a no-cab option.
- RB (veh_aa): add an option to omit the hull RWS and hatch for unmanned AA drones (Firefly).
