- Interpreter/RA: apply the parsed `kits` so subfaction kit-bash parts can reach shared units of every archetype (units in a roster whose family the kit part lists), instead of only via the `deck` slot on tank/apc/amphib.
- Team-colour plates: the share is 15-16 percent on rifle and AT squads for every faction, above the 12 percent spec; consider smaller vest/back panels in ViewRecipeMacros._soldier.
- veh_aa LOD1 sits at 2808 triangles with default params against a cap of 2800; trim a module or drop the greeble at tier 1.

## F:ae

**Summary:** VIEW-M8 African Empire is done: styles/ae.json (faction style plus ae.nigeria, ae.kongo and ae.south_africa overlays) and hand-authored recipes for all 19 units, summon.ae.repair_drone, structure.ae.forge_cannon and structure.ae.horizon_mass_driver. The shared structure and service-unit archetypes wear AE through style slots. My checks pass: 9 AE tests (533 checks), the validator with 0 errors, and `tools/gd check --strict` clean for my files. One existing test still fails, test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts, because def/han/napc/nec are also defined in styles.json; that is not mine.

Look: ochre/charcoal/cyan, with mismatched bolt-on skirt panels, spare wheel, push bar, cyan lock collars and hub dots. Each unit expresses its role. The Kiln has a front plough, the Rhino capacitor cylinders, the Lagos a docked drone and comm mast, the Reclaimer a crane arm, and the Sovereign a stern gantry. The structure dialect is a jib crane, a pipe run and scrap on 3x3 and larger, and a caged cyan core.

Iteration rounds, all inspected:
- Round 1: tri-budget trims; Okapi cargo hold moved off the RWS; spare wheel resized; Kiln blade raised to hide the archetype's front lights; Lagos drone bay moved to the turret cheek; thicker Reclaimer crane; Hammerhead armour blocks; front-deck patches removed from the team-plate quiet zone.
- Round 2: Forge crane moved onto the turret roof; ball-sensor detector cues; Sunbird wing-tip fins; shared-structure budget split (2x2 crane only); hub dots and small details tiered to LOD0.

Screenshots are in docs/shots/ae/: F_ground, F_air, F_ships, F_struct1, F_struct2 (the V-1 rest-pose sheets), F_vs_def and F_vs_def_rts (side by side against DEF), F_teams (12 player colours on the Buffalo), F_subs, F_turn_buff, shared_service_units_4styles, plus the iter1_* and iter2_* round evidence.

Palette check against the visual direction: ochre, charcoal and cyan match the authored palette at ΔE00 0. One player colour clashes with AE paint. Player cyan is ΔE 3.4 from the AE cyan trim, so it needs the lobby hint (see the cross-module requests); the plate rings keep the plate itself readable. Brown is the documented ΔE 10.6 pair against the hull.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Style slots in styles/ae.json are `skirt` (generic patchwork panels, uses only hl/hw), `front` and `extra` (structure dialect). There is deliberately no `roof` style slot: vehicle and structure archetypes both use that name in different scopes, so a style `roof` would break one of them.
- The validator's style-slot scope has only fw/fh/door_*/pads_* plus archetype default names, so the structure slots recompute pw/hh/bw/bk/k/hcap from fw/fh as `let`s named aePW, aeHH, aeBK, aeK, aeHC, aeFZ.
- Structure dialect is size-split to fit the art-doc tri caps. 3x3 and larger get jib crane, pipe run, scrap stack and the caged cyan core. 2x2 gets a lite crane only (the shared 2x2 generator archetype alone is 2860 tris and 8502 verts). 1x1 gets nothing.
- Art-direction kit parts of the form `kit_<id>` are not implemented as `kit_*` slots. No archetype calls them, so the subfaction overlays use existing hooks: kit.roof (nigeria=mast, kongo=snorkel, south_africa=sensors) and the `extra` slot (comm mast + civic beacon, reed screen + snorkel stack, rangefinder box). Subfactions also change palette.acc (#03B3CB, #26D8DD, #4CD2FF).
- Style emblem values are from the validator's abstract set (ae=ring, ae.nigeria=triple_bar, ae.kongo=chevron, ae.south_africa=hex_notch). The AE sunburst emblem is not in that set, and there is no macro that stamps emblem geometry.
- unit_style resolution means `unit.ae.*` always wears style `ae` even for subfaction owners (style_for_def returns parts[1]). The overlays therefore only affect `*.shared.*` defs.
- Weaver AA and Lagos Drone Guard use the 2x2 missile-tube payload, not the art card's quad autocannon, because the balance data gives them aa_missile weapons; both get cyan lock-collar frames and side ammo boxes.
- Kiln Assault Crawler uses veh_siege weapon `siege` with a fat bore gun (gun_len 1.9, gun_r 0.21) and a custom front plough, not weapon `ram`. The ram variant places a rear slab with a bright cyan strip.
- Union Guard and Civic Rifle Team keep 4 soldiers but use the `angular` helmet: with the round helmet the archetype alone is 1376 of the 1400-tri squad cap.
- Cyan hub dots, wheel rings, push bars and some details are tier 2, so they are LOD0 only. This is needed to stay under the render.md LOD1 caps (light 2000, medium 2800).
- I overrode summon.ae.repair_drone's air hover with an ops_after `["meta","hover",3.5]` because recipe.meta is applied before the archetype's own hover op.
- Extra files I added: game/tests/visual/ae_turn.gd/.tscn (turntable and sheet tool with --items id@style, --grid, --teamcycle), game/tests/view/test_view_ae_recipes.gd, tools/py/ae_recipes/ (authoring scripts that regenerate the AE JSON idempotently), docs/shots/ae/.

**Tests:** Commands run:
- python3 tools/py/validate_recipes.py --strict: 0 errors; the only 4 warnings are other factions' duplicate style ids (def/han/napc/nec).
- tools/gd test view_ae: 9 tests passed, 533 checks. This is my new file game/tests/view/test_view_ae_recipes.gd (palette against style.json and the visual direction, team-plate contrast for all 12 player colours, per-model tri/vert/LOD1 budgets against art_direction 9.2 and render.md 5.8.2 for every AE model and for shared structures in ae and all three overlays, team surface, sockets, unique units differing from the units they replace, shared recipes building in all 4 AE styles, AE structure footprints).
- tools/gd test view_recipes: 12 of 13 pass. The one failure, test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts, is the duplicate style ids in styles.json and is not mine.
- tools/gd test view -q: run mid-work. It showed the Mamba budget failure I then fixed (3448 tris now), plus str_generator/str_barracks 3x3 over 3000 tris in the napc style, which I did not investigate; it has not been re-run in full since.
- tools/gd check --strict: no findings in ae_turn.gd or test_view_ae_recipes.gd; the run's overall FAIL comes from 8 warnings in oth

**Known gaps:**
- No faction or subfaction emblem geometry and no subfaction band patterns (checker/wave/block) on the models; only the palette accent and the style `emblem` field carry them.
- Structure roofs cannot get an AE dialect from the style, because the `roof` slot is shared with vehicles. AE identity on shared structures comes from palette, the crane, pipes, scrap and the caged core, so building silhouettes are still the shared archetypes.
- Aircraft fuselages are fixed by the archetype, so Sunbird and Hammerhead differ only through wing/tail params, patch panels, intake rings, wing-tip fins and armour blocks; the crank-wing read is mild.
- The Protea canister shroud, the Lagos drone and the Forge crane are static (no animated mode switch or drone launch). The drone rotors are not animated.
- Cyan is heavy on most AE models (hub dots, collars, work-light bars). Emissive area was judged by eye, not measured against the 3 % budget.
- The per-pixel area-mix and silhouette-IoU checks (art_direction P-6, F-5) were not run. Faction separation from DEF was judged by eye on F_vs_def and F_vs_def_rts.
- The generator 2x2 is 8590 verts (soft doc cap 8500; the shared archetype alone is 8502).
- AE hulls on the tan gallery ground are only moderately separated; the dark tracks and charcoal panels carry the read. I did not check other terrain looks.

**Cross-module requests:**
- RB (veh_siege archetype): the headlight and tail-light pairs are swapped. `light_pair` `light` is at +hl and the red pair at -hl, while the gun and front face -z, so every siege unit has red lamps on its front. The Kiln's plough hides them.
- RA (tools/py/validate_recipes.py): the style-slot scope should include the structure archetype derives (pw, ph, hh, bw, bk, bd, bz, k, hcap), or the structure archetypes should expose faction-dialect slots with names that vehicles do not use (for example str_roof). Right now every faction must recompute them from fw/fh, and a style `roof` slot is unusable.
- RA / RB: no archetype calls the `kit_<id>` slots the art spec 5.6.5 describes for subfaction kit-bash parts. Either add calls at anchors (skirt, roof, deck, gear per archetype) or amend the spec.
- Recipes owner: styles.json still defines def/han/napc/nec that styles/*.json also define, which fails test_real_style_files_have_no_conflicts. Remove the duplicates from styles.json now that the per-faction files exist. test_structures_fit_their_plinth_at_three_footprints also fails in the full `view` run (str_generator and str_barracks 3x3 at 3304 and 3252 tris against 3000, napc style), unrelated to AE; I did not investigate it.
- Render owner: ViewRecipeInterpreter applies recipe.meta before the archetype ops, so a recipe cannot override an archetype's `["meta","hover",...]` op. Apply recipe meta after the ops.
- Lobby/ViewStyle owner: AE's authored low-contrast list should include player colour 5 (cyan, ΔE00 3.4 to the AE accent trim) as well as brown (ΔE00 10.6 to the hull), for the plate_contrast hint.
- Art-direction owner: add a macro or ViewEmblemGeometry.stamp that stamps faction and subfaction emblems, so the `emblem` style field becomes geometry.
- Structure archetype owner: the 2x2 generator archetype is 8502 verts, over the 8500 art-doc cap before any faction adds detail.

## EC3B:powers+superweapons

**Summary:** EC3B (E7+E8) is done: the strategic framework and superweapons run on the real data and are green in tests. All 48 powers activate through CMD_USE_POWER and all 8 superweapons charge, warn and execute, both in small worlds and in real generated matches. The framework is SimStrategicSystem (slots, validation, scheduler, warnings, windows, commands), SimStrategicEffects (recipes, vision/terrain rules, activation, knob mirrors), SimSuperweapons (charge machine, launch, cancellation, EVT_SW_IMPACT, danger_fraction_bp) and SimSummonBrain (read queries over AB3's summon drivers). Execution reuses AB3's SimPowerFx and SimSummons rather than duplicating them. Cross-OS chain is identical on macOS, linux-amd64 and linux-arm64 for an 8-faction match that fires all 8 superweapons. Soaks re-run clean: soak_rosters 96 matches and soak_units 32 rosters, both 0 errors. Full `tools/gd test -q` has 2 view failures and 5 ui errors (AppLocalSession will not parse, boot budgets); none is in code I touched, and every econ/abil/combat/sim test passes.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- FIX sim_damage.gd: apply_emp set cc.emp_until but never SimCompEcon.shutdown_until, so an EMP-shut-down structure stayed online in economy, production and power. Now written for structures: max(shutdown_until, tick + dur).
- FIX sim_power_fx.gd Helios: the packet carries damage per second (620 dps), but every sweep pulse dealt the full 620. Pulses now deal dps x hit_every_t / 20 = 155, so a centreline unit takes about 9 pulses (about 1,400) rather than 5,600.
- FIX sim_status.gd check_end_on: `ab.watch &= ~WF_END_ON` left the id in SimAbilitySystem.watch_list, which failed debug_validate ('watch 0 vs watch_list'). Now uses SimCond.watch_clear.
- sim_power_fx.gd: SimPowerFx.apply(world, power_idx, pid, x, y, angle, aux, strike_shift = 0). Warned strike powers are applied at ACTIVATION with strike_shift = warning_t: marks and frozen positions are taken now, shells leave when the warning ends. The bombard SimWarning and its scheduled record are cosmetic phase changes.
- Superweapons are applied at EXEC, not at activation: the SK_WARNING_END record at exec_tick calls SimPowerFx.apply_superweapon(world, w_idx, pid, x, y, angle, hub_x, hub_y, aux, launcher_id). Nothing is spawned during the warning, so cancelling is trivial. aux is drawn once at launch (world.rng.next_u32() & 0x7FFFFFFF) and stored in the record. Support powers draw one aux per activation, or use the target entity id for Rapid Turnaround.
- SimStrategicSystem is NOT a pipeline stage: RefCounted; SimPowerSystem calls update() after its per-player loop. Not hooked through SimSystem, so launcher loss and EMP are detected by polling each tick before the scheduler runs; on_structure_lost(world, ent, cause) and on_shutdown_started(world, ent) remain as public entry points. emit_packet(world, SimImpactPacket) is an adaptor that builds a warhead and calls SimPowerFx.strike_packet; the real choke point is combat's detonation, which already applies Trident (spec `apply_area_damage` and `fire_log_query` do not exist as such). SimImpactPacket.team_mask, layer_mask and radius_min are ignored by the adaptor.
- Signatures: warnings_affecting(pid, out: Array) and attacks_pending_against(pid, out: Array) take an UNTYPED Array (the UI port passes a plain Array). They list only live warnings (phase WARNING or EXEC). cooldown_left_ticks(world, pid, slot); can_activate(world, pid, slot, tx, ty, angle, target_id) returns SimEconConst.RSN_*; use_power(world, pid, p_idx, x, y, angle, target_id) -> RSN_*; slot_info(pid, slot) -> SimPowerSlot; window_active(world, pid, p_idx) is keyed by POWER INDEX (a registry of running effects), not a window id; knob(world, pid, k) -> economy.knob; sell_locked(pid, eid); get_warning(id); schedule(...); pending_count(). SimSuperweapons.danger_fraction_bp(world, warning, x, y, t) takes the world first (all static; also available as SimSuperweapons.can_launch, launch, step, geometry, exec_span, affected_mask, in_zone, line_len, launcher_of).
- SimWarning geometry: LINE warnings (Atlas, Helios, Horizon) store (x, y) = line START and (x2, y2) = line END, radius = half width, width = 2 x radius (> 0 means capsule). Circle warnings (width 0): (x, y) = centre, radius; Tempest (x2, y2) = launcher (approach line), otherwise (x, y). Perun radius is 7 cells (the ring). payload_ids[0] holds the mirror zone id (SimZoneSystem.spawn_warning, -1 none). kind: WK_SUPER, WK_POWER (Tremor, Counterbattery, Counterlaunch), WK_SCAN (Wideband Scan). Phases: WARNING -> EXEC -> DONE or CANCELLED; done and cancelled records linger 40 ticks. The Trident warning mask is every non-vacant player for its whole life. The record stays in EXEC through exec_span (Helios 240, Horizon 180, Trident 500, Tempest 1200, Dragonfall 1300).
- Events: EVT_SW_EXEC_START(a=owner pid, b=w_idx or 100+p_idx, c=warning id); EVT_SW_IMPACT(a=w_idx, b=x, c=y, d=radius, e=damage type) is emitted only for superweapon packets, the Helios start and the Trident dome, NOT for support-power shells (combat's EV_IMPACT covers them); EVT_SW_CANCELLED(a=pid, b=w_idx, c=cause 1/2/3, d=warning id); EVT_SW_DONE(a=warning id); EVT_POWER_EFFECT_END(a=pid, b=p_idx) fires for every power with a duration, one tick after the effect ends; EVT_POWER_READY fires on the tick equal to ready_tick, but can_activate already passes at that tick in stage 1; EVT_POWER_UNLOCKED is not sent for a power with requires_mask == 0 (test fixtures).
- Data authority over spec text: Atlas rods land together at X (global.json delay_ticks 0, spec said X, X+4, X+8); Helios is 48 pulses of 155 (dps 620, traverse 12 s, every 5 ticks) not 60 x 140; Perun ring is a full disc of radius 7 at edge 30% (no annulus in the data); Tempest spawn ring is 1.5 cells (AB3); the swarm driver period is 10 ticks. Everything follows DefSuperweapon as compiled.
- Recon rule: a power with a MARK action is treated as reconnaissance (VR_NONE) even though power_actions.json says vision current for power.sap.counterlaunch_plot.
- Terrain rule: DECOY, REPAIR and RECON_SUMMON powers need a cell that is not cliff/void (passable ground or water); every other target only needs to be inside the map. There is no per-power terrain rule in the data.
- Windows: each WINDOW power also writes the readable economy knob temps (RELAY_DAMAGE_BP, CMD_DAMAGE_BP and CMD_RADIUS_CELLS (ADD, cells), JOINT_LANDING, RECOVERY_PRIORITY; production-rate and salvage knobs are written by AB3). The aura windows and timed effects of AB3 remain the effective mechanism.
- Scheduler kinds actually used: SK_WARNING_END, SK_PACKET (impact events only), SK_WINDOW_END, and a private SimStrategicSystem.SK_DONE = 20. The other SK_* constants (APPLY_FX, SPAWN_SUMMON, BEAM_PULSE, ...) are unused: two-phase powers, delivery aircraft, Helios pulses, Dragonfall assembly and the summon drivers are executed by AB3's primitives (SimPowerFx, SimSummons, the zone system).
- SimSummonBrain is a read-only façade (swarm_drones, capsules, engines, phase_of, expires_at, work_area). The autonomy itself (Tempest SWARM: 400 ticks from first arrival, hard cap 1200; Dragonfall CAPSULE: 100 ticks unfold; ENGINE: 1200 ticks, nearest enemy structure) is AB3's SimSummons.step. Drones fired from inside a Trident dome bypass it (the 'fired from inside' rule).

**Tests:** WHAT I RAN AND WHAT IT SHOWED
- `tools/gd check --strict game/src/sim game/tests/econ game/tests/support game/tests/scenarios`: 0 errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings.
- `tools/gd test strategic_powers`: 15 tests pass (10.1-J, S8, all 48 powers through the command path with a per-kind effect check, all ten effect kinds, absolute cooldowns, credits, vision rules, warnings).
- `tools/gd test test_superweapons`: 27 tests pass (10.1-K, S6):
  - Charge: exact recharge of 9600, 8400 and 7200 ticks, starts empty, no overcharge, shortage pauses charging.
  - Warning and cancellation: activation restarts the charge; a launcher lost or EMP-shut during the warning cancels without refund; a shortage does not cancel; a late loss does not cancel; sale refused while warning.
  - Timelines: Atlas 3 rods, Aurora, Helios pulses, Perun core plus ring, Horizon impacts at X, X+90, X+180 with debris blocking placement and MCV deploy, Tempest, Dragonfall, danger fraction.
  - Trident: interception (24 charges, Atlas leaves 0, Perun leaves 8, shell costs 1, under 8 charges no reduction, 50% cap shared with own 40% resistance, EMP bypass).
  - S6: Aurora cancels the enemy Atlas.
- `tools/gd test strategic

**Known gaps:**
- No visual tests for warnings (10.4) and no view code draws SimWarning or the mirror warning zones; the view has to render them (warning_shape from SimWarning, affected_mask filter).
- EVT_SW_IMPACT is not emitted for support-power shells or per-hit Tempest and Dragonfall shots.
- Trident 'ordinary missile costs 1 charge' is covered by a shell packet test (strike_packet shell=true), not by a real enemy missile unit.
- Survey Drone (NEC) and Recon Balloon (SAP) still use the UAV as stand-in because no unit sheet defines summon.nec.survey_drone / summon.sap.recon_balloon (data request from the rules-data notes, unchanged). power.sap.counterlaunch_plot is 'current' vision in the data and is overridden to recon in code.
- The 48-power tests check that each kind's effect exists in sim state (zone, buff event, window, shells, mark, summon), not the exact numbers of every row; numeric row checks are AB3's test_ab3_powers.
- The scenario tests take 5 s per run (8 players, 1900 ticks, movement on); strategic_determinism plus match tests add about 35 s to the suite.
- test_sim_determinism goldens stay untouched because the pristine strategic hash and the no-event rule for requires_mask==0 keep them identical.

**Cross-module requests:**
- VIEW: draw warnings from SimStrategicSystem.warnings (or warnings_affecting(viewer, out)): LINE = capsule from (x, y) to (x2, y2) with half-width radius, CIRCLE = disc, Tempest also the approach line to (x2, y2); phases WARNING and EXEC; Trident dome visible to all; listen to EVT_WARNING (404), EVT_SW_EXEC_START (407), EVT_SW_IMPACT (408), EVT_SW_CANCELLED (406), EVT_POWER_EFFECT_END (403).
- UI: UiSimPortWorld.strategic_warnings already works with the untyped Array; refresh its 'once that query exists' comment. AI: ai_world_view.strategic_warnings (TODO(sim)) can now call world.strategic.attacks_pending_against(me, out), SimSuperweapons.danger_fraction_bp(world, warning, x, y, t) and can_activate(...) for reason codes.
- DATA: (1) if the 4-tick Atlas stagger of economy 5.21 is wanted, set delay_ticks 4/8 on the atlas packets in global.json (the code reads whatever the data says); (2) power.sap.counterlaunch_plot target vision should be `any` per economy 5.16 (code overrides it for any MARK power); (3) define summon.nec.survey_drone and summon.sap.recon_balloon.
- KERNEL/QA: tests/sim/test_sim_types.gd and other 'empty stub hashes nothing' loops are unaffected, but any golden that runs a power or superweapon now hashes the strategic state (warnings, scheduler, windows) in stage 4 and the slot fields in SimPlayerEcon.
- COMBAT: SimDamage.apply_emp now writes SimCompEcon.shutdown_until for structures (economy, production and superweapons read it); review whether emp_until and shutdown_until should be unified. The Helios per-second conversion lives in SimPowerFx.apply_superweapon; combat's warhead per_second flag itself is still ignored by spawn_sweep.

# === Wave 6 app+fx+audio (wf_826b46f4-4fe) ===

## FX1:manager+recipes

**Summary:** FX1 delivered: FxManager, FxBatch, FxAssets, FxRecipeBook and FxCatalog are in game/src/view/fx. The six fx_* shaders and fx_common.gdshaderinc are in game/assets/shaders, and game/data/recipes/fx.json is seeded with the 35 spike-lineage effects. Batches are MultiMesh ring buffers driven by fx_time (34 batches including the new wake). Admission has per-class token buckets, distance (with the focus-distance fix), projected-size and frustum culling, and hard caps. Trackers, stampers, loops and cancel work. Scorch paints ViewScorchLayer, with no Decals or GPUParticles. The recipe engine compiles fx.json (extends, vars, for/if/let/native, rand/randr/rsq) and errors carry the layer path. A composite spawn costs 35-54 us in the real renderer and the test median is under the 100 us limit. Spike contact sheets 0-3 reproduce through the recipe engine and I inspected all four over two rounds. Mobile and Compatibility both compile with no shader errors. Chaos battle on Forward+ at 1280x720 (display-locked at 8.33 ms): about 500 spawns/s, frame p50/p95/p99 all 8.33 ms (same as baseline), draws 287 to 308 (p95), FxManager.advance 175 us per frame, 54 us per spawn. 60 simultaneous composites: p50 8.33 ms, p95 11.1 ms (baseline 9.26), 784 live instances, 35 us per spawn.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- FxDef is the inner class FxRecipeBook.FxDef, as in spec 2.1. Its fields are id, cls, kind, radius, nominal_scale, min_dist, template, layers and meta. FxManager.setup(cam, q, book, ground, scorch_layer) takes null for ground and scorch_layer (flat ground, no persistent marks). It cannot be called twice with a different book without rebuilding.
- FxManager extras beyond spec 3.6: schedule_call(delay, cb), start_loop(..., delay=0.0), wake(pos, heading, length, width, life=4.0), set_distort(bool) and distort_enabled() (ViewQuality's fx_distort clamp calls set_distort after set_quality), lod(), sync_camera(), recipe_book(), batch(name), batch_names(), live_instances(), live_lights(), pending_scheduled(), emitter_count(), bucket_tokens(cls). Test hooks: viewport_size_override, trace_emits and emit_log. dome() takes mode as a fourth argument, passed to the shader in INSTANCE_CUSTOM.w (> 0.5 means intercept/cyan). ViewWorld must set fx.camera_focus_dist every frame. FxManager._process calls advance(delta) by default; call set_process(false) to step it explicitly.
- Admission does not use Camera3D.unproject_position or is_position_behind. It projects with a cached camera transform, fov and viewport, so it works without the camera being in the tree. Class max_dist 0 means unlimited (SUPER). fx.json `classes` overrides the built-in tables.
- cancel(handle) also drops the group's scheduled work and stops its emitters, and only kills batch slots that have not been reused (FxBatch.kill_if). SUPER-class sub-effects spawned inside a SUPER composite join its group.
- The stamper (start_stamper) now passes b = stamp position + travel direction, so a wake layer or a directional effect knows the heading. POINT effects ignore b.
- start_tracker admits once (one token of the effect's class, culled on either endpoint). Segment spawns skip the bucket but still cull. Trackers are capped at 24; a refused tracker returns -1.
- scorch(pos, radius, life, hot) paints ViewScorchLayer (CRATER when radius >= 3, else SCORCH) and flushes at most every 0.25 s. hot > 0 also spawns a 2 s gglow sprite (param 0.55*hot). Scorch is skipped over water and during prewarm(). life is advisory because the layer is permanent.
- fx.json schema, superset of spec 7.5. Top-level: schema, classes, mappings, effects. Effect keys: class, kind, radius, scale, min_dist, vars, layers, extends, template, meta, desc. Layer common keys: op, when, delay, at (a|b), along, off[3]. Ops: sprites, puff, ring, wake, dome, column, ribbon, tracer, trail, cone, beam, rail, shimmer, bolt, light, scorch, shake, fx, loop, for, if, let, native. Ribbon-type ops take to (a|b) and to_off[3]; the scalar arc_h replaces a vector arc; cone takes dir "fwd"|"back"; light takes color [r,g,b]. `fx` takes id, scale, b_at, b_off and inline (true = run the child's layers as part of the composite: no admission, may target templates; used for the vehicle, cannon-impact and collapse explosions). `loop` takes id, interval, duration, scale, b_at, b_off. Unknown keys are compile errors. Frame variables: s, dist, ax..az, bx..bz, dx..dz, flight (= dist / speed when a `speed` var exists; an effect var named `flight` overrides it), then vars in order, loop variables and lets. min_dist on an effect skips LINE effects shorter than that. Natives: register book.natives[name] = Callable(m, a, b, s, args) before load_file.
- ViewExpr (M1's file) has no rand/randr/rsq. FxRecipeBook rewrites those calls into hidden frame variables that are refreshed with randf() before every evaluation, so ViewExpr is untouched.
- fx.json ids follow the spec lineage: expl_small/medium/large, muzzle_small_arms, muzzle_mg, muzzle_cannon, muzzle_at_missile, muzzle_artillery, muzzle_beam_thermal, muzzle_rail, hit_rail, hit_bullet (impact_ground extends it), hit_infantry, aircraft_contrail, wake_foam (new, uses the wake batch), sw_atlas_rod. Kept from the spike: cannon_impact, sw_orbital_strike, and the emp, wreck, collapse, weld, beam_tick and impact_water effects. Templates: tpl_explosion, tpl_expl_vehicle, tpl_expl_collapse, tpl_expl_cannon. The file is only a seed; F3 will replace and extend it.
- FxCatalog(book): muzzle_id(warch, pres_muzzle=""), impact_id(warch, result, splash_units, target_air=false), explosion_id(fx_kind, radius_units, damage_type), explosion_scale(id, splash_units), trail_id, death_id(kind, size_class, flags), arc_apex(warch), default_fx_for_missing(id) and the settable pres_names. Ids missing from the book fall back with one Log.warn per id. death_id expects DefEnums.SizeClass integers: vehicles <= LIGHT light, MEDIUM medium, otherwise heavy; ships < SHIP_MEDIUM small, otherwise large.
- Shaders live in game/assets/shaders and include res://assets/shaders/fx_common.gdshaderinc, which includes view_common.gdshaderinc (fx_time) and declares fx_lod. fx_ring gained mode 5 WAKE. fx_dome reads its colour mode from INSTANCE_CUSTOM.w.

**Tests:** tools/gd check --strict on game/src/view/fx, game/tests/view and game/tests/visual/fx_sheet.gd: 0 script errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings. tools/gd test test_fx_ test_view_globals: 37 passed, 0 failed (586 checks). test_fx_manager.gd (21 tests) covers the F1 acceptance rows: the TINY bucket gives 60 then 50 more after advance(0.1); the focus-distance rule holds (96 m gives TINY 166 m and MEDIUM 296 m); behind-camera and frustum culls; force bypass; ring overwrite equals 5; batch caps; cancel; schedule and re-cull; tracker interval, cap of 24 and bucket bypass; quality LOW and MEDIUM; pause and time_scale; every fx.json effect spawns something; shake falloff; stamper cap of 8 per frame. test_fx_recipes.gd (12 tests) covers fx.json compiling, layer paths in errors, reference and cycle checks, inheritance, expression scope, rand/randr/rsq, delay and when, natives, the expl_small/medium/large batch sets against the spike, the cost per composite spawn, and FxCatalog. Visual runs: tools/gd run res://tests/visual/fx_sheet.tscn --gui --size 1280x720 -- --mode=sheet --sheet=0..3 --out=<png> (sheets 0-3 inspected); mode=chaos and mode=count give the perf numbers in the summary; tools/gd

**Known gaps:**
- Compatibility renders, but the spike's cluster-sprite dimming there is not fixed (VIEW-Q3). ViewQuality's fx_compat_boost key is not wired into FxAssets yet.
- The FX event router and mappings from sim events to spawn() (F4), the content of the remaining ~80 effects (F3), and ViewWorld's ownership of the FxManager (setup, prewarm plus clear_all 8 frames later, camera_focus_dist, camera_shake to ViewCamera.add_shake, paused/time_scale bound to game speed) are not done. ViewWorld has no fx member yet.
- In headless tests fx_time and fx_lod are only verified in the visual runs, because the dummy renderer cannot read global shader parameters back.
- Heat shimmer and the EMP refraction ring stay subtle or glassy, as noted in the spike. The 60-effect run shows a blocky look where many scorch stamps overlap; that comes from ViewScorchLayer stamp sizes and is not FX code.
- Chaos and count perf were measured only on Forward+ (Metal, display-paced at 8.33 ms, so only overruns are resolved). No Mobile or Compat perf numbers were taken. GPU ms is not measurable on Metal.
- Wake and rings snap to terrain height only within 3 m above the ground and not over water, which is a heuristic. The wake foam shader was only compiled, not inspected visually.

**Cross-module requests:**
- ViewWorld (W1): own an FxManager child at the world origin. Call setup(camera, quality, FxRecipeBook loaded from res://data/recipes/fx.json, ViewTerrain, ViewScorchLayer). Call fx.prewarm() behind the loading screen and fx.clear_all() 8 frames later. Set fx.camera_focus_dist from ViewCamera each frame. Connect camera_shake to ViewCamera.add_shake. Bind paused and time_scale to game speed and pause. After ViewQuality.apply, call fx.set_quality(q.fx_quality()) then fx.set_distort(q.get_bool(&"fx_distort")).
- project.godot / APP1 (optional): declare fx_time and fx_lod in [shader_globals]. ViewGlobals.ensure() registers them at runtime otherwise.
- F3 (fx.json content): extend or replace game/data/recipes/fx.json, keeping the `mappings` keys that FxCatalog reads (muzzle_by_family, impact_rules, death_by_kind, arc_apex). The catalog needs these ids to exist to avoid fallback warnings: hit_bullet_hv, hit_bullet_structure, beam_thermal_hit, hit_flak_small, expl_kinetic, expl_strategic_large, expl_air_burst, expl_underwater(_big), aps_flash, death_vehicle_light/medium/heavy, death_sink_small/large, death_infantry, death_air_explode, death_drone, death_silent, trail_missile, trail_missile_white, trail_rocket, trail_shell, trail_torpedo, and the remaining muzzle_* ids of spec 5.9.2.
- F4 (fx router): GameData.pres_names goes into FxCatalog.pres_names. Use FxCatalog.explosion_scale(id, splash_units) as the spawn scale for expl_*. Use start_tracker for continuous beams and start_stamper for dust, contrail and wake.

## AU1:toolkit+sfx

**Summary:** AU1 done. tools/py/audio is a deterministic seeded-numpy synthesis pipeline: build_all.py sfx renders 435 catalog entries (492 OGG files, 19.35 MB, cold build about 25 s with 6 jobs, no-op rebuild 0.2 s). The library covers 27 weapon archetypes plus 160 faction-flavour files, projectiles, impacts, explosions, collapses, deaths, engine/step/air loops, structures, economy, 8 power classes, 26 superweapon assets, alarms, 32 UI sounds and 6 ambience beds. build_all.py check passes with 0 failures on all QA classes, budget and the flavour-distinctiveness gate. check --verify re-renders everything and matches every hash. Two clean builds (jobs 6 versus 3) give identical OGG, manifest and index bytes. rifle_shot reproduces the spike (source LUFS -21.04/-21.98/-22.46 against -21.0/-22.0/-22.3). tools/gd test audio_assets passes 3 tests with 3431 checks, so Godot imports and loads every OGG. I inspected about 15 spectrogram sheets (weapons, explosions, structures, superweapons, powers, ui, ambience, loops, flavours) and tuned recipes from them. Nothing was listened to.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- AssetSpec has the spec fields plus two optional extras: qa (QA class override) and flavour (faction code). The reference recipe keeps its spike seed via params['seed_name'] (format field {v}). The RNG is otherwise rng_for(f'{id}#{variant}', salt).
- File naming: channels='mono' gives <asset>.mono.ogg, 'stereo' gives <asset>.ogg, 'both' gives both files. The manifest and index 'file'/'f' of a 'both' asset is the stereo file. asset_index.json rows are {f,b,c,l,n} plus an optional 'm':1 meaning a <asset>.mono.ogg twin exists (3D events load it). Groups are only emitted for consecutive _1.._N runs of at least 2.
- Manifest entries carry extra keys: lufs_src, seam_src, len_delta, snr_db, mono_file/mono_bytes/mono_sha256/mono_pcm_sha256, and an optional 'prov' dict that voice tasks can use to feed PROVENANCE fields. manifest['qa']['distinct'] stores the flavour-distinctiveness baseline.
- Path mapping beyond spec 7.8: snd.intercept.aps|zone -> sfx/intercept/aps|zone; snd.air.* -> sfx/air/*; snd.eco.* -> sfx/eco/{cash (3 variants), cash_big}; snd.collapse.sN -> sfx/collapse/sN; snd.emp.hit shares sfx/impact/emp. The rule 'snd.a.b.c -> sfx/a/b_c' is in tools/py/audio/README.md.
- Most SFX are mono-only. 'both' (stereo master plus mono twin) is used for structures online_*, sell, captured, unit_out_*, hq_deploy, power_down/up, powers activate/end, flat superweapon launch/end/aurora impact, explosion huge, collapse s3/s4 and death ship.
- Loops: sfx/proj/{missile,torpedo} are seamless path-voice loops; sw charge/loop, power_*_loop, struct hums/repair/salvage and beam_thermal_loop are 4 s mono loops (peak -6). Hums, repair and salvage sit at -24 to -28 LUFS.
- Superweapon assets (26): atlas launch|impact(3 variants); aurora charge|launch|loop|impact|end; helios charge|launch|loop|end; perun launch|impact; tempest launch|loop|end; dragonfall launch|loop|impact(3 variants)|end; horizon charge|launch|impact(3 variants); trident launch|loop|end. Charge sounds are loops so the runtime can pitch them.
- The spec brief said collapse 3-9 s but the 10.2 table says 1.5-8 s. I followed the table: s1..s4 are 3, 4.7, 6.4 and 8 s.
- The QA attack metric is the 10 to 90 percent rise to the median level of the first 40 ms, using a 4 ms envelope of the signal above 150 Hz, with the window starting at the onset. It is robust against noise peaks and sub-bass ripple. The spec numbers (6/10/12 ms) are checked with this definition.
- The 10.2 numeric limits are literal, except that two are relaxed for faction flavours (small_arms_fx centroid >= 480 Hz, attack <= 10 ms; cannon_fx attack <= 32 ms, 200 Hz-2 kHz share >= 3 percent). Other values marked [tune] in catalog/qa_thresholds.py: oneshot/proj/impact/death length windows, codec SNR floor 8 dB for SFX, ui/alarm loudness windows.
- AssetSpec.lufs on a one-shot is only a cap on top of peak normalisation, used to keep sustained sounds (missiles, powers, structure cues, whistles, air) within about -14 to -20 LUFS. Loudness is enforced (plus or minus 1 LU) only for loops and ambience.
- beam_thermal start has no pre-charge whine, because fire feedback must be immediate. Missile launches are 1.8/2.5/3.4 s (light/mid/heavy) with a receding envelope.
- The loudness meter uses the exact libebur128 coefficients and matches ffmpeg ebur128 to under 0.05 LU. It replaces the RBJ approximation of the spike.
- Master chain additions: DC removal also on loops (whole-period mean), and edge fades only when the encoded file breaks x0 <= 0.016 or x_end <= 0.0022. True-peak overshoot after encoding triggers deterministic re-encode passes (up to 4).
- DSP_VERSION = 1 and MASTER_CHAIN_VERSION = 1; generator string 'meridian-audio-dsp 1'. A flavour recipe's hash also covers the base recipe it wraps.
- build_all.py music, voice, responses and events are hook stubs that print 'not available' (AUD-T4/T5/T6). check classifies only sfx/ui/alarm/amb assets.

**Tests:** ["python3 tools/py/audio/bootstrap.py --voice: reuses .cache/venv (numpy 2.5.3, soundfile 0.14.0 with libsndfile 1.2.2 and libvorbis, Pillow 12.3.0, onnxruntime present).", ".cache/venv/bin/python tools/py/audio/qa/test_dsp.py: OK. Biquad vs scipy.lfilter max error 1.5e-9; LUFS ours -19.67 vs ffmpeg -19.7; true peak, circular seams, RNG independence and OGG byte reproducibility pass.", ".cache/venv/bin/python tools/py/audio/qa/test_pipeline.py: OK. Two builds with different --jobs are byte-identical, the no-op rebuild takes 0.18 s, and a forced rebuild keeps PROVENANCE created_utc.", "build_all.py sfx --jobs 6: 435 assets in 25.9 s cold (--jobs 3: 34 s). Two clean builds into scratch dirs: diff -rq shows only PROVENANCE.json differing (created_utc stamps); every OGG, manifest and index is identical.", "build_all.py check --verify --jobs 6: check ok, 0 failures. Class summary: alarm 5, ambience 6, cannon 51, cannon_fx 112, death 10, explosion 22, impact 20, loop 40, oneshot 76, proj 4, small_arms 9, small_arms_fx 48, ui 32, all with 0 failing. Verify re-rendered 435 assets with 0 hash mismatches. The flavour baseline is recorded in the manifest.", "Budget: 492 files, 19.35 MB of the

**Known gaps:**
- No human listening: quality is judged by LUFS, true peak, envelope, spectral, loop-seam metrics and spectrograms only. The audio spec's ear-review gate (10.6) and faction ABX are still required.
- Music (AUD-T4), announcer/voice and unit-response bleeps (T5) and the events/validators scaffolder (T6) are not part of this task. build_all.py music|voice|responses|events are stubs.
- Faction weapon flavours cover the 10 families x 8 factions x 2 variants, but they are parametric re-voicings of the base sounds (pitch, tilt, tail, drive, signature layer). The distinctiveness gate is objective only.
- Loop metadata for Godot: .ogg.import sidecars are generated by Godot with loop off. The runtime must duplicate the stream and set loop from the asset_index 'l' flag (as in spike finding 7).
- Cross-platform byte-equality is not checked. Only macOS arm64 was built and verified; on other toolchains check --verify falls back to metric comparison.
- The 16-bit source WAV masters are not kept on disk; only their SHA-256 (pcm_sha256) is in the manifest.
- The flavour baseline records the first accepted build. Later tuning that increases distinctiveness raises the baseline automatically, so the baseline moves with any tuning.

**Cross-module requests:**
- game/export_presets.cfg (export owner): exclude audio_manifest.json, PROVENANCE.json, NOTICE.txt and every *.import from the export (repository hygiene only). Ship asset_index.json plus the OGGs.
- Events scaffolder / data authoring (AUD-T6): use the id rule in tools/py/audio/README.md and read the mono/stereo variants from asset_index.json ('m':1 means a .mono.ogg twin exists). Loop flags come from 'l'. Sim-side sound tags are unchanged: weapon archetype names are the asset names.

## APP3:options+fieldmanual

**Summary:** APP3 delivered: Options (6 schema-driven pages, live apply, per-page Revert/Reset, 15 s "Keep these settings?" that reverts itself, key-binding table with rebinding and conflict dialog), Field Manual (overview, units, structures, research, powers, tech tree, compare, search; resolved stats with delta/floor/conditional markers), procedural cursors (17 base + 2 reticles + 8 scroll arrows), first-run dialog, credits data (engine MIT, fonts OFL). 51 UI tests pass; `check --strict` on src/ui and tests/ui was clean. Screenshots at 1920x1080 and 1280x720 inspected and iterated. The one-line "Guided" tag fix in UiFmModel (a weapon shows Guided only for missile, rocket or torpedo projectiles) was made after the last test run and has not been re-run, because the project lock was held by another agent's test run. The Field Manual has no 3D turntable viewer, and cursors, first-run dialog, Field Manual entry points and the Esc-menu Options button still need other owners to wire them (see CROSS_MODULE_REQUESTS).

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- DefBrowser does not exist in the data module. UiFmModel builds the cards itself from GameData, DefRoster (layers 1-2 clone) and DefPlayerView (layer 3). build(data, roster, view_override = null): pass a live DefPlayerView to get live values (model.live). Public API: overview(), units() (producible plus spawnables), structures() (HQ first), research(), powers() (3 powers plus the superweapon card), tech_tree(), compare(other) -> {a, b, only_a, only_b, replaced, modifier_diffs, units}, compare_unit(base_unit_idx, other) -> [{label, a_text, b_text, a, b, delta_bp, tone}], search(text, limit), card_for(kind, idx), ref(kind, idx). Stat rows are {key, label, value, text, base, base_text, delta_bp, delta_text, tone, floor, conditional, tip, stat}.
- Field Manual deltas show the combined static modifier (DefRoster.static_sums), so a +10% trait reads +10% even when the integer stat changed by +9.92%. Live views and floor hits fall back to the measured change.
- UiFmText.def_name(def) names defs with an empty ui_name (all summons) from the last id segment, for example 'UAV'.
- Options pages: Graphics, Audio, Controls, Interface, Network and Storage. The spec's Accessibility, Language and camera-speed rows are sections of Interface, matching the task text. Language is a disabled drop-down. UiOptionsLayout.HIDDEN lists the state-only schema ids (meta/*, window_pos, monitor, last_*, recent_hosts, help_shown) that have no row.
- UiScreenOptions.open(page: StringName = &"graphics") -> bool and UiScreenFieldManual.open(params: Dictionary = {}) -> bool push the overlay through AppState.push_mode. The Esc menu (APP1's UiDlgGameMenu) must close its own dialog first, because dialogs sit above pushed screens.
- UiScreenOptions.settings (Node) and modal_opener (Callable) are injectable for tests. The 15 s dialog is UiDlgKeepSettings (result 1 keep, 0 revert; tick(delta) drives time). Keep-triggering ids are UiOptionsLayout.KEEP_IDS (window_mode, resolution, vsync).
- UiCursors.register_all(scale_pct: int = -1, style: int = -1) reads access/cursor_scale and ui/cursor_style from AppSettings when the arguments are -1. AppBoot already calls it with no arguments. The other UiCursors entry points are set_state(state), set_scroll(Vector2i), slot_of, image_for(state, px, high_contrast), hotspot_of and refresh(); UiCursors.enabled_override is a test hook that forces registration in headless runs. Recipes are SVG rasterised with Image.load_svg_from_string. Scroll hotspots are computed from rotate_deg, not stored in the JSON.
- UiDlgFirstRun.open(settings_node = null) shows the dialog on the AppScenes modal stack. It writes net/player_name, video/quality, video/ui_scale and access/colour_mode on Start, clears meta/first_run on Start or Use defaults, and cannot be dismissed with Escape.
- data/text/credits.json keeps the heading/lines schema that UiScreenCredits reads (not the spec 7.3 title/entries schema) and now has Game Engine (MIT), Typefaces (OFL), Audio and Open Source Notices sections.
- New shared classes: UiActionNames (action labels and categories), UiTeamSwatch, UiDlgKeyConflict (0 cancel, 1 swap, 2 replace), UiOptionsKeybinds.

**Tests:** `tools/gd check --strict game/src/ui game/tests/ui game/tests/visual/app3_dialogs.gd`: 0 script errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings (run before the Guided-tag one-liner). `tools/gd test test_ui_fm_model test_ui_field_manual test_ui_options_schema test_ui_cursors test_ui_screens_menu test_ui_keymap -q`: 51 passed, 0 failed, 5234 checks. New files: test_ui_fm_model 7 (golden cards vs resolved defs and DefPlayerView for napc.vanilla, napc.canada and han.china; pinned Rifle Squad values; structure/research/power/superweapon cards; compare; search; tech tree; all 32 rosters build readable cards), test_ui_field_manual 7, test_ui_options_schema 12 (every schema row on exactly one page, guards, revert, 15 s keep dialog, preset overrides, key table), test_ui_cursors 8 (includes first-run and credits). Screenshots via `tools/gd shot res://src/app/boot.tscn out.png --size 1920x1080 --frames 60 --allow-errors -- --screen=options --page=<graphics|audio|controls|interface|network|storage> --fresh-settings --no-audio` and `-- --screen=field_manual --page=<overview|units|structures|research|powers|tree|compare> --roster_id=roster.napc.canada`, plus `tools/gd shot res://tests/visual/app3_dialogs.

**Known gaps:**
- No 3D model viewer (UiModelViewer) in the Field Manual unit card: it shows a large glyph tile instead.
- Field Manual: the tech tree has no pan-drag or zoom (it scrolls); there is no in-match highlight of owned structures; the 'Include completed research' toggle is only available through params.view (a live DefPlayerView), with no toggle widget.
- The Field Manual is not drill-down in COMPACT size class; it keeps the three-column layout, which fits at 1024x576 and above.
- The Field Manual has no 'counter suggestions', because the data has no per-unit counters field. Matchups (effective against, weak against, resists) are derived from the damage matrix instead.
- The Options slider-tick sound needs UiOptionsPageAudio.audio_port to be set; nothing is wired because there is no Snd autoload yet.
- The Keep dialog covers window mode, resolution and vsync only. The Renderer row's 'Apply and restart' calls AppRelaunch.relaunch and was not exercised.
- Cursor sizes are unverified on macOS Retina (spec risk R4). The UiCursors 'Input' calls could only be counted, not observed, in headless tests. Cursor images are baked lazily per state on first use, not in one boot batch.
- Storage page sizes only walk user://logs, crashes, replays, screenshots and cache; those folder names are assumed.
- The last edit (Guided tag condition in UiFmModel) was not re-run under tools/gd because another agent held the project lock; it only changes which enum values gate a tag.

**Cross-module requests:**
- APP1 / UiDlgGameMenu: add 'Options' and 'Field Manual' buttons to the Esc menu. Close the menu dialog first, then call UiScreenOptions.open() / UiScreenFieldManual.open({roster_id: <local roster>, view: <live DefPlayerView, optional>}). Also bind F1 (action open_field_manual) in UiScreenGame to UiScreenFieldManual.open().
- UiScreenMainMenu (UI5 file): (1) add a FIELD MANUAL button navigating to &"field_manual"; AppFlow already allows MAIN_MENU -> FIELD_MANUAL. (2) When params.first_run is true, call UiDlgFirstRun.open(). The OPTIONS button enables itself now that UiScreenOptions exists.
- UiLobbyBriefing (UI5 file): a click on a unit, structure or power chip should call UiScreenFieldManual.open({roster_id: <slot roster id>, focus_id: <def id, e.g. 'unit.napc.rifle_squad'>}); page switches automatically from the def kind.
- UiScreenGame / UiInputController: call UiCursors.set_state(state) with the intent cursor integers (already equal to UiCursors.State) and UiCursors.set_scroll(edge_dir as Vector2i) while the pointer is in the edge-scroll band. Call UiCursors.refresh() after skin changes.
- AppBoot (UI2): _step_services calls UiKeymap.load_from(store) statically, but it is an instance method. Use UiKeymap.instance().load_from(store), otherwise the persisted key bindings are never loaded at start; the Controls page saves them to [keys].
- AppApply.input_sink is still unset. Whoever wires the input module should register it, since the key table and the input/* rows call AppApply.on_changed(&"controls/*") and the input/* apply hooks.
- AppSettings (UI2): expose a public schedule_save() so the key table can use the 0.5 s debounce instead of flush().

## APP1:net+ai+ingame-menus+end

**Summary:** APP1 done. Single-player and LAN now share one code path: AppMatch.start_local builds a real NetSession (Role.LOCAL) through the new AppNet autoload (registered before AppState in project.godot), AppNetSetup (options + strict net.md 7.1 config completion) and AppNetWorldJob (AppMatchJob wrapped as NetWorldJob). AppLocalSession, AppNetPortLocal are removed. AI: AiFactory.make now installs AiBrain (install_brain flag, AiBrain.install idempotent); AppAiHook (instance per match, owned by AppMatchContext) feeds net's ai_factory/ai_release/ai_think_period/ai_default_handicap, so lobby levels Easy..Brutal, roster personalities (style 0 = doctrine) and dropped-human takeover all use the real AI; --bots (SimBot) kept, plus --bots=human (bot plays the human slot, real AI opponents) and --bots=all. In-match: UiDlgGameMenu (Esc, inline surrender/leave confirmation, LOCAL pause), UiMatchOverlays (pause banner, stall overlay, host stall prompt, desync dialog, F3 UiNetOverlay, connection-lost), surrender/defeat banners, and the end screen (UiScreenEnd with Summary/Military/Economy/Graphs tabs, UiMatchStats sampled series, UiScore, UiEndGraph). Acceptance: (a) LOCAL session == direct AppMatchJob sim and == SimMatchKit (checksum at 200/400/600 and final) and command-log replay matches; (b) headless human-bot vs Medium vs Hard, 7200 ticks (6 min) ends with the end-screen model populated (APPTEST_SUMMARY/PLAYER lines), exit 0, 0 engine errors, identical chain on two runs; (c) 1920x1080 screenshots of game menu, pause banner, stall overlay+prompt+F3, desync dialog and all four end-screen tabs inspected (iterated on the end screen layout twice); (d) tools/gd test app ui net -q: 691 passed, 0 failed.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- AppMatch.start_local(config, opts) -> AppMatchContext stays the single launch call; opts: with_view, unpaced, ticks (stop polling at that tick: ctx.limit_reached, session stays PLAYING), pause_at, speed_pct (50..200 list or 0), discard_events, bind, title, player_name. ctx.session is a NetSession. New ctx members: ai (AppAiHook), job (AppMatchJob for the loading screen), stats (UiMatchStats), human_bot, commands_sent, limit_reached, last_end, frame() (polls session + bot + stats + limits), tick(), summary(end={}), crash_info() (whitelisted keys). ctx.config is the completed net.md 7.1 config (superset of the UI one). AppLocalSession.job()/tick()/commands_sent() no longer exist: use ctx.job, ctx.tick(), ctx.commands_sent.
- AppNet (autoload, no class_name): ctx, attach(ctx), detach(ctx), session(), end_session(), make_options(extra). It polls the attached context in _process at priority -100. Without the autoload (tools) AppMatch adds an AppSessionDriver(ctx) node instead. AppBootHook.run(state, store, args) ensures AppNet exists and sets the --bots mode (AppBootHook.bot_mode(args) -> '', 'ai', 'human', 'all').
- AppNetSetup.complete_config: UI config -> strict net config. Only the first human is the local peer (peer 1); further humans become AI (AppAiHook.takeover_level); teams outside 1..4 become 8+pid; rules keys outside net's rules_schema (victory) are dropped, missing ones defaulted; map layout is rounded to net's LAYOUTS 2/4/6/8 (3 players -> 4-slot layout, size raised to MapGenParams.min_size). AppNetSetup.net_layout(players) is also used by AppMatch.simple_config and UiLobbyState.layout_players() (edited in UI5's file so lobby preview == built map).
- AppState.bind_session(session, end_via_screen=false) + unbind_session(): match_started -> IN_MATCH with {ctx}, launch_aborted -> SKIRMISH_LOBBY with {role:'local', error}, kicked -> MAIN_MENU with {message}, match_ended -> END_SCREEN unless end_via_screen (AppMatch binds with true: the game screen shows the result banner then navigates itself).
- AppAiHook is now an instance (AppAiHook.new(telemetry)) holding an AiFactory: make(pid, level, style, seed) -> Callable(world, out), release(pid), shutdown(), made_pids(), make_human_bot(pid, submit); statics: factory (test override of the AI slots, new (world, out) signature), set_bot_mode(mode), human_is_bot(), think_period_turns(level) = ceil(think_period_ticks/2) (Easy 15, Medium 10, Hard 5, Brutal 3 turns), takeover_level. bots_enabled/bots_all kept, bots_human added. AppMatch.make_ai_thinker now takes the hook as first argument.
- AiFactory: install_brain (default true) makes make() call AiBrain.install; AiBrain.install is idempotent; AiFactory.release(pid) now sets ctx.brain = null to break a ctx <-> brain-module reference cycle (without it a match leaked ~5000 objects and tools/gd run failed with 'resources still in use at exit'). AppAiHook.shutdown() releases all thinkers at ctx.dispose().
- AppMatch.result_summary(ctx, end={}) = ui.md 4.9.2 model from UiMatchStats.build: outcome (0 victory, 1 defeat, 2 draw, 3 observed), reason, winner_team, duration_ticks, players[{stats, score, score_parts, apm, status, is_local, is_ai, ai_level, eliminated, elim_reason, alive_ticks, ...}], series{pid:{harvested, spent, army, kills}}, map_name/seed/family/size, plus aliases result ('victory'|'defeat'|'draw'|'observed'|'left') and tick/duration_s. A local player who is eliminated or surrendered is a DEFEAT even if the sim ended with no winner (sim rule end_when_no_humans). The series key is 'kills' (units_killed + structures_destroyed) instead of the spec's value_destroyed.
- Sim counters that do not exist yet (value_destroyed, value_lost, powers_used, sw_launched) are -1 in stats; UiScore treats them as 0 and estimates the military term from kills (KILL_UNIT_VALUE 600, KILL_STRUCT_VALUE 1500 credits) with score_parts.estimated = true.
- UiScreenGame edits: session is NetSession; game menu is UiDlgGameMenu (Resume/Options/Field Manual/Surrender/Leave, inline confirm; LOCAL holds a pause while open and while Options/Field Manual are pushed, respecting input/pause_on_menu); overlays via UiMatchOverlays; F3 toggle_net_overlay; local resign/defeat banners; end banner from UiScreenGame.end_banner(outcome, reason); new --preview tags menu, stall, desync, net, tab_<name> (end screen tab). UiScreenLoading reads ctx.job; its Cancel disposes the context (cancels the running job).
- AppBoot._step_services now calls UiKeymap.instance().load_from(store) directly (the soft static call raised an engine error on every boot).
- AppTestBoot: new flags --ai-levels=1,2 (per AI slot), --bots=human, prints APPTEST_SUMMARY and APPTEST_PLAYER lines (end-screen model) before APPTEST_END, reason is victory|defeat|draw|ticks|timeout; APPTEST chain= is now NetSession's input chain.
- NetSession pause is asynchronous (a ctrl record at the next turn boundary): request_pause returns at once, is_paused() flips a turn later. Game speed uses net.speed_pct of the config.
- UiNetPortSession (by-name adapter) is still not used; AppNetPortSession is the typed adapter over NetSession.

**Tests:** "tools/gd check --strict on game/src/app game/src/net game/src/ui game/tests/ui game/tests/net: 0 script errors, 0 lint, 0 warnings in my files (3 unrelated warnings in src/ai/ops/ai_op_capture.gd and ai_op_salvage.gd from another agent). tools/gd test app ui net -q: 253 files, 691 tests, 691 passed, 0 failed (27166 checks). New: tests/ui/test_app_net.gd (18 tests: config completion, LOCAL == direct sim == SimMatchKit checksums, command-log replay, pause/resume/surrender, takeover, tick limit / pause-at, AiFactory brain install, think periods, real Hard AI through the bundles + determinism, bot modes, released AI is freed, AppNet autoload, end model, score formula) and tests/ui/test_ui_match_dialogs.gd (8 tests: game menu, pause banner, stall overlay/prompt, desync dialog, net overlay, UiMatchOverlays on a real NetSession, end screen tabs). Also on Linux (tools/gd linux test): the parity/replay/pause/takeover tests and score/banner tests pass (8+3). Acceptance (b): tools/gd run res://src/app/boot.tscn -- --autostart=match --players=3 --humans=1 --ai-levels=1,2 --bots=human --ticks=7200 --speed=0 --fresh-settings --no-audio --map-size=112 --map-seed=11: exit 0, 0 ERROR lines, 8 s, t

**Known gaps:**
- No LAN roles in the app yet: AppNet only drives LOCAL sessions (host_lan/join_lan/start_local_lobby, LAN browser/lobby screens are not built); the overlays and AppNet.attach are role-agnostic and tested against a LOCAL session with emitted stall/desync/disconnect signals only (no real stalls in LOCAL).
- Replays (NET-8) do not exist: End screen has no Watch/Save replay; game menu 'Leave' does not autosave a replay; desync dialog offers Show folder / Copy report / Leave but no 'Save replay' (the package already contains the .mfreplay).
- Observer/replay HUD (UiObserverBar, scoreboard F2, UiReplayBar) not built: after surrender or defeat the player keeps the normal HUD with a banner; an all-AI (--humans=0) session runs but is shown with the player HUD.
- Pause banner does not show 'N pauses left' (NetSession does not expose pauses_used); chat overlay, notifier/ribbon feed and audio wiring (AppAudio, audio_sink) are not part of this task and not wired.
- Score military term is an estimate and 'damage' columns read 0 until the sim keeps value/damage counters; end_when_no_humans (sim rule, not in net's rules_schema) ends a match as soon as the last human surrenders, even in a 3+ player game against AIs.
- Play again returns to the skirmish lobby with the last saved setup (user://last_skirmish.json), not an in-memory copy.
- AI takeover level/style is net's fixed TAKEOVER_AI_LEVEL; the lobby always sends AI style 0 (roster doctrine).
