## MIS3:mission-content

**Summary:** MIS3 shipped 9 missions. Tutorial tut_field_training (NAPC, open 128 seed 10, 21 gated steps + 1 secondary) and 8 Operations op_napc (Open Road, evac convoys + checkpoint twist + vehicle recovery), op_nec (Lattice, 3 Relays, blackout, siege camp), op_olm (Deep Wells, 3 pumps, forward depot), op_def (Iron Schedule, 3 junctions, grain depot dilemma, drone swarm), op_pd (Marais Landing, coast, seize then hold a port 7:00), op_han (Common Plan, 3 link nodes, 'Central Priority' order vs spare the depot), op_ae (Second Harvest, prelude battle, Steward's levy), op_sap (Slow Tide, Trident + 3 lines). Each op has 2-3 primary + 1-2 secondary objectives, scripted waves, twist, defeat conditions, bible fault-line briefing and debrief texts. Authored with a Python DSL (tools/py/mis3, gen_missions.py); JSON is the shipped content, manifest updated. MissionRun harness + MissionBot (SimBot steered by per-mission goals) + TutorialPlayer prove completability. Bot reaches the win on 47 of 48 runs (8 ops x 6 seeds), 655-840 s; idle player loses in all 8; tutorial passed by scripted real commands in 621 s with no timeout; real app path --autostart=mission=<id> runs all 9 (tutorial to win through the app).

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Downstream relies on: ids tut_field_training + op_napc/op_nec/op_olm/op_def/op_pd/op_han/op_ae/op_sap; group 'tutorial' order 0, group 'operation' order 1..8; titles 'Field Training' / 'Operation <name>'; briefings have 3 sections (Situation, opponent, Orders) with lore links; hidden-kind objectives are internal flags (live_x, done_x, n1.., lb, lc, arr1..) that the UI must not list; messages with speaker 'Debrief' (debrief_win / debrief_lose, op_han has two win variants) are meant for the end screen.
- Authored at Medium: enemy AI levels are 0 (napc, def, pd, han, ae, sap) or 1 (nec, olm) so MIS2's Easy shift has no effect where authored 0; waves are fixed counts (not difficulty-scaled).
- Tutorial map is 128 (4-slot layout needed for a separate rival player slot), not 96; fog off; start credits 4500; every step has one shared timeout timer (step_to) whose fix helps the player (spawns the building/units, orders, funds); a funding safety net trigger gives 1500 credits up to 4 times. UI-only steps (camera, selection, stop/scatter, control groups) are time-gated since no condition can see UI actions.
- Superweapon drill uses a real rival NEC Aurora EMP array (slot 2, spawned at step 8, AI Hard switched on at step 21); the AI decides when to fire (usually within about 2-4 min of step 21), so the step has a 240 s timeout that destroys the array.
- Wave/gate mechanics use only existing conds/actions; 'seize a port' = neutral-owned port structures transferred by script when the zone is clear (capture works only on neutral map objects); wrecks cannot be scripted (scripted kills make none), so op_ae stages a real prelude battle and 'salvage' is not measurable by any condition.
- The bot harness needs per-mission steering (mission_goals.gd) because SimBot never attacks scripted objectives, skips amphibious units (opts.amphibious added in MissionBot) and unarmed escorts (include_defs); MissionBot also launches the Trident (op_sap). Balance data was edited concurrently by BAL3 during testing (units_*.json 23:14) and changed outcomes once; re-run mis3_bench after balance changes.

**Tests:** validate_missions.py --strict: 0 errors/0 warnings, 10 files. tools/gd check --strict: clean (0 lint, 0 warnings). tools/gd test -q mis3 mission data_missions net_mission: 70/71 passed (content 6 tests incl. tutorial played with commands, map quality of all 9; completion: bot wins 8 ops x 2 seeds in 8-22 game min, idle never wins); the 1 failure is ui/test_ui_mission_hud::test_a_recorded_mission_replays_with_its_objectives (MIS2), which also fails alone now, not caused by MIS3 (passed ~23:10, tree changed by other agents since). Bench (mis3_bench.py, bot): seeds 777,1234,4321,999,31337,2468: 47/48 wins, only op_han seed 31337 lost; wins: napc 804 s, nec 840, olm 780, def 690-708, pd 655, han 704-713, ae 840, sap 692-696. Idle (14-25 min cap): all lose (napc 168 s, nec 595, olm 497, def 666, pd 1080, han 1260 clock, ae 574, sap 1320 clock). Hard AI alone (driver ai) wins op_ae and op_nec only. App path: --autostart=mission=<id> --bots=human boots all 9; tutorial ends result=win tick 22561. Maps (all 128, 4-slot layouts; map test: no safe-template, validator clean, >=2 exits, fair): napc open s14, nec urban s11, olm open arid s21, def open arctic s31, pd coast s3, han open s61, ae op

**Known gaps:**
- No human playtest; 'competent human at Medium' is only proxied by the bot (47/48) and the idle check. op_han seed 31337 and op_olm have thin margins (operators die at node 3 / pumps).
- Hard AI alone cannot finish the objective-driven ops (checkpoint, depot, junctions, port, nodes, lines); only op_ae and op_nec.
- Tutorial UI-only lessons are not verified by any gate; messages name the default keys.
- op_pd/op_sap rely on amphibious units / Trident use that the bot only partly exercises; Easy/Hard difficulty not benchmarked (only Medium).
- Secondary objectives intact (op_napc), guns (op_def), layers (op_sap), engineers (op_ae) are not all reachable by the bot; map seeds were checked with the generator validators and ASCII dumps, no screenshots of the missions were taken.
- Real-app headless run shows the plain SimBot (--bots=human) loses op_nec, as expected: it attacks instead of defending.

**Cross-module requests:**
- MIS1: add UI-event conditions (camera moved, selection made, control group assigned/recalled, rally set, stop/scatter/hold issued) so tutorial steps can be gated instead of time-boxed.
- MIS1/MIS2: a 'difficulty' condition or per-action count scale (spawn_units) so wave size scales with the selector; today difficulty shifts AI levels only (AppMission.ai_level_for).
- MIS1: a 'stat' condition (credits harvested/salvaged, units lost/killed) so salvage-driven objectives (op_ae) can be measured; and an AI-module use of players[].ai.aggression (currently stored but unused).
- AI module: Hard AI plays scripted objectives poorly (never attacks neutral-held or placed objective targets); optional objective-target hint would let the full AI complete missions.
- MIS2: show hidden-kind objectives never; show 'Debrief' speaker messages on the end screen; ui/test_ui_mission_hud::test_a_recorded_mission_replays_with_its_objectives currently fails alone (seek lands on 101 instead of 70).

## BAL3:rebalance

**Summary:** BAL3 done in 3 tuning rounds plus an India probe, measured on the paired Hard-vs-Hard RR (112x112, 16 min, fog; 1140 matches = the 768 standard schedule + 372 extension matches on suspects, so suspects n=96 games each, others n=54-69). BASELINE reproduced AIT's outliers: canada 69, eurocorps 26, kongo 31, ae.vanilla 35, australia 35, alpine 32 (6 rosters outside 35-65, spread sd 10.8). FINAL (fresh copy of the live tree with all changes, 1140 paired matches, 0 errors): 0 rosters outside 35-65, sd 6.4 (score-share sd 7.2 -> 4.5). KEY FINDING: unit numbers inside the +-8 % fair-cost band move the Hard RR only a few points (equal-spend fight grid and RR correlate weakly: olm rosters lose the grid but win the RR, kongo the opposite); the RR is dominated by AI economy/army-survival (corr with collectors 0.64, units alive at end 0.80). The big moves came from AI dials: Eurocorps 26 -> 52 (defense_pct 25->15, siege 60->50, retreat 50/75: its field army was starved), kongo 31 -> 47 and japan 38 -> 52 (eco_x10 +5/+6), sap.vanilla 39 -> 59, australia only 35 -> 39 (eco 8 + expand E + PD +6 %). Canada 69 -> 52 and napc.vanilla 58 -> 48 from NAPC -4 PI (undoing BAL2's +4) and Narwhal/Beaver -3. Unit changes: 24 units, all clamped by validate_balance (1 scaled down: wedge_recon_fighter). BEFORE>AFTER win % (n after, 95 % CI): canada 69>52 (96,+-8); algeria 65>47 (63,+-9); india 64>65 (96,+-8); nigeria 63>61 (96,+-7); mexico 62>52; el_andalus 61>56; russia 59>55; napc.vanilla 58>48 (96,+-8); thailand 58>54; indonesia 58>53; def.vanilla 57>49 (96,+-7); vietnam 54>50; olm.vanilla 53>52; nec.vanilla 52>44; china 52>51; pakistan 52>46; pd.vanilla 52>58; han.vanilla 51>41 (69,+-9); kazakhstan 51>54; saudi 51>52; cambodia 47>55; north_korea 47>44; south_africa 46>48; usa 45>45; nordics 41>39; sap.vanilla 39>59 (60,+-8); japan 38>52 (63,+-9); australia 35>39 (96,+-8); ae.vanilla 35>41 (96,+-8); alpine 32>39 (66,+-8); kongo 31>47 (96,+-7); eurocorps 26>52 (96,+-7). Win% = (W + D/2)/N; win = elimination or a 25 % adjudicated score gap.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- CHANGED NUMBERS (PI = health x damage, split sqrt/sqrt; build = build_time_s; costs, ranges, speeds, structures, defences, superweapons, bible modifiers untouched). NAPC: rifle_squad hp 419->402, guardian_tank hp 925->906 / dmg 144->141, javelin_team -4 PI (undoes BAL2's +4 offset where canada 69 % and exchange 1.64); narwhal -3 (hp 925->911), beaver -3. AE (no faction offset before, units sat at fair-cost 0): union_guard hp 400->428, buffalo_tank 900->~934, pike_team, forge_howitzer, weaver_aa +7 PI cumulative (ae.vanilla 35, kongo 31, exchange 0.81-0.91). PD: ranger_marine, tide_tank, harpoon_team, storm_aa +6, outrider_howitzer +5, wedge_recon_fighter +1 (clamped from +5 at the fair-cost floor) (australia 35, japan 38). NEC: jager_squad, spike_team, leopard_tank, archer_spg +3; marte_heavy_mbt and charlemagne_siege_tank build_time_s -8 % (41.0->37.7 s, 66.5->61.2 s) because Eurocorps' +10 % vehicle build-time modifier is not compensable by PI (both units sit at fair-cost -7 %). SAP: arjun_assault_tank hp 1528->1505, gaj_siege_platform hp 3492->3439 -3 PI (India 64 %; probe shows no measurable effect, stays at 65 %).
- AI DIALS (ai_personality.json, AI-only issue = starved economy/field army, not numbers): nec.eurocorps retreat_hp_pct 35->50, return_hp_pct 85->75, defense_pct 25->15, siege 60->50; pd.australia eco_x10 0->8, expand D->E; pd.japan eco_x10 0->6, expand D->E; ae.kongo eco_x10 0->5; sap.vanilla eco_x10 0->5, aggression 35->45, defense_pct 30->20, expand D->E. Evidence: low-win rosters had 8-9 collectors vs 10-12 and lost 4-5 (field 2-3). A retreat-only variant on australia/ae.vanilla/kongo showed no effect, so those dials were not shipped there. These dials only affect AI-controlled rosters; human-played Eurocorps/Australia are unchanged except for the numbers above.
- Downstream pinned numbers changed: napc.guardian_tank health 925->906, napc.rifle_squad health 419->402, narwhal 925->911 (x1.21 canada layer 1119->1102), guardian with NAPC +10 % health 1018->997. The LAN data hash changes (balance + AI files), peers must be rebuilt together; replay fixtures with recorded real AI/units (app_ai_2p_3min.mfreplay, xplat_2p_ai.mfreplay) must be regenerated by whoever owns replays (see make_replay_fixture.gd header).
- Method note: win rate is the BAL2 adjudication (score gap >= 25 %); I also report score share (own / (own + opp) score), which is much less noisy (sd 7.2 -> 4.5). Rounds are re-applied from the pre-BAL3 sheets by tools/py/bal3_changes.py (entries accumulate per unit and are applied once; a later bal2/bal3 re-run would undo hand edits). The 'fin' verification ran on a fresh copy of the live tree (includes other agents' concurrent data edits) and matched the experiment tree g4 almost exactly.

**Tests:** validate_balance --strict: 0 errors, 0 warnings, 4 waived, exit 0. tools/gd check --strict: OK (1553 files, 0 violations, 0 warnings). python3 tools/py/gen_unit_reference.py: 9 files regenerated. 'tools/gd test data abil econ combat sim ai -q --timeout 2700 --stall 0': 1241 tests, 1238 passed; the 3 failures were pinned guardian/narwhal healths (test_data_playerview x2, test_data_resolver), updated (906, 997, 1102) along with test_ui_fm_model rifle 419->402. 'tools/gd test unit ui golden integration net -q --stall 0': 1038 tests, 1035 passed; the 3 failures (test_app_replay x2, test_ui_mission_hud::test_a_recorded_mission_replays_with_its_objectives, replay diverges at tick 20) are PRE-EXISTING and not from BAL3: they fail identically on a private copy of the current tree with my unit sheets and ai_personality.json reverted. python3 tools/py/balance_calc.py validate reports 6 failures ('json structure') caused by another agent's current global.json edits (0 failures with the pre-BAL3 global.json); BAL3 did not touch global.json. Experiments: all on private copies of game/ (no shared file swapped), 14-16 parallel headless matches via tools/py/bal2_run.py --project, equal-spend grid 

**Known gaps:**
- Residual low end (all inside 35-65 but with wide CIs): australia 39 (+-8), nordics 39 (+-9), ae.vanilla 41 (+-8), alpine 39 (+-8), han.vanilla 41 (+-9). Residual high: india 65 (+-8, exchange 2.1, 26 eliminations won vs 3 lost; -3 PI had no effect, it is an AI/doctrine strength with eco_x10 already at -10), nigeria 61, sap.vanilla 59 (overshoot from four combined dials), pd.vanilla 58. n=54-96 per roster gives +-7-11 points, so rosters inside 38-62 are not distinguishable.
- Eurocorps is fixed only through AI dials: its heavy-composition equal-spend grid score is still -0.54 (worst of 32; Marte/Charlemagne sit at the -7 % fair-cost floor and the +10 % cost / +10 % build-time bible modifiers cannot be compensated by numbers). A human playing Eurocorps (or the AI after a doctrine change) may still find the roster weak; same for Australia (land vehicles -10 % health).
- The RR measures economy and army survival much more than unit strength; the grid and RR disagree for olm.* (grid worst, RR strong) and kongo. Deployable artillery does not deploy in the grid (BAL2 caveat), and siege ops in the AI still destroy almost nothing, so artillery rosters are under-measured.
- Not retuned: structures, defences, superweapons, first-strike timing; 16-minute cap ends before superweapons fire. Mexico/USA still doctrine-limited (usa 45, mexico 52).

**Cross-module requests:**

- AI owner: siege units still contribute almost nothing and Eurocorps/Australia lose the field army to siege+defence weights; consider a floor on field-army share before siege/defence spending, and sap.india (65 %, exchange 2.1) needs a doctrine look since numbers do not move it.
- Human playtest focus: (1) Eurocorps heavy armour (Marte 1540 cr effective, slow production) and Australia artillery vs a rushing human; (2) Canada Narwhal/Beaver on water maps after -3 PI; (3) AE baseline roster (kongo, vanilla) feel with +7 % unit power; (4) PD baseline units +6 % (japan, australia); (5) Sap India Arjun/Gaj and Nigeria infantry-cost edge; (6) early-game safety (AI still has about 3 units at 4:30, a human rush punishes it).

# === Wave 11b HOT1 + QA2 (wf_4acfefc9-755 resumed) ===

## HOT1:hotkeys+small-gaps

**Summary:** HOT1 done. Every keymap action now has a handler in UiScreenGame._on_action (powers F5-F7, F8 superweapon, Space alert jump, M/Y modes, J ping, I/O/K/L abilities, Ctrl+Delete scuttle with confirm, T/Ctrl+T, Ctrl+G, Ctrl+Tab, Tab/Shift+Tab, Alt+Q.. cards and x5, F4, Print, Alt+Enter, camera pad, in-match chat). Chat was kept because the net layer has an in-match channel (NetSession.send_chat/chat_received); pings use the existing MAP_PING message. The lobby Veterancy switch is gone (stored rule untouched). A key-probe test presses every default binding in a live LOCAL match, the golden replay's observer HUD and the tutorial mission. Bugs found on the way and fixed: target-less powers could never be cast from the UI, Follow showed hotkey F instead of Ctrl+F, build cards, power dock and the sidebar tooltips hard-coded key labels, the minimap logged an engine error for a degenerate camera quad, line powers never committed. Docs regenerated and rewritten, private LAN address masked by recapturing lan_lobby.png (and lobby.png, which showed Veterancy), docs/shots/ is in .gitignore. Windows was never executed (no Windows host); that is stated in the CHANGELOG, KNOWN_ISSUES and README.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- Print and Alt+Enter are now in contexts game+observer, not global: they work inside a match only (docs say so). toggle_scoreboard (F2) is observer-only in the keymap (a player match would leak the opponents' economy).
- UiCommandBus.use_power no longer vision-checks the (0,0) point of target-less powers; before, those 6 powers were refused with NO_VISION from the UI.
- Hotkey labels on build cards, the power dock and the superweapon ribbon now come from UiKeymap.label (Option+Q on macOS, rebinds show); ability-bar chords are shown compact (^F for Ctrl+F). test_ui_build_model accepts Alt+ or Option+.
- UiHudPresenter ability-button building moved to the new UiAbilityButtons (the presenter hit the 1500-line lint limit); the presenter exposes press_power_slot, press_superweapon, press_ability_hotkey, press_card_slot, jump_to_alert, show_ping, notice, ground_sim_at, last_alert.
- Line powers now commit on a drag (press start, release direction) or on a second click; before the release was never delivered. A drag during targeting or placement no longer changes the selection.
- pause_on_focus_loss pauses a LOCAL match on window focus loss, but not when started with --autostart (test boots, stress scripts).
- New test-boot flags: --keys=<script> (AppTestKeys) and --lan-address=<text> (lobby screenshots only). New preview tag --preview=chat.
- Pillow is not installed anywhere on this machine, so the private address was removed by recapturing lan_lobby.png (and lobby.png) with the new flag instead of painting over.
- In the key-probe the real match is driven with one tick per frame (opts.max_ticks_per_poll = 1) and the net port is wrapped by a spy that still forwards to the real session; chat is probed by briefly switching the session role (restored before any frame runs).

**Tests:** tools/gd check --strict: clean (1558 files). tools/gd test hot1 -q: 17/17 on macOS arm64 and 17/17 in the Linux amd64 container (tools/gd linux test hot1 -q). Mutation check: removing the cam_jump_alert and hide_ui handlers makes the probe fail. Full suite 'tools/gd test -q --timeout 2700 --stall 0': 2445/2445 passed (100235 checks, 683 s), run before the last small edits. After them: 'tools/gd test hot1 ui_app app_ -q' 102/102, check --strict clean, python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/py/tests 195 OK (2 skipped), version.py check OK. Live GUI runs with --keys, screenshots inspected: F5 targeting ring at the pointer, F5 cast (dock shows 1:29 cooldown, credits debited), F8 superweapon targeting preview with red hatching over own units, Ctrl+A and Tab (sidebar switched to DEFENSE), F4 (HUD-less view) plus Print (PNG written), chat box preview. Images: <repo>/docs/shots/hot1/f5_targeting.png, f5_cast_cooldown.png, f8_superweapon_targeting.png.

**Known gaps:**
- Windows was never executed (no Windows host): nothing about HOT1 or the build was run on Windows.
- No two real machines exchanged in-match chat or pings; the UI half is tested over the real session API and signals, the net half by the existing net tests and the two-process harness.
- Print/Alt+Enter work in a match only; cursor confinement, sidebar side, selection brackets, zoomed-out markers and sidebar hover hotkeys Options rows still do nothing.
- cmd_ability_2..4 (O K L) have no unit to act on in the shipped data; the probe asserts a no-op for them.
- The lobby still lists 'Spectators in the lobby' (spectators exist only in the lobby, removed at launch); no in-match chat in single-player or replays (the key says so).
- Pings relay to teammates only; chat input and F4 hide-interface were verified in a live GUI frame, but a human has not typed in the chat box with a real keyboard.

- UI/Options owner: Options rows that still do nothing: ui/sidebar_side, ui/brackets, ui/strategic_markers, input/confine_cursor, input/sidebar_hover_hotkeys. Wire or remove them (listed in KNOWN_ISSUES section 2). HOT1 connected ui/camera_pad, ui/minimap_sweep, ui/chat_fade_s, ui/chat_opacity and input/sticky_modes, move_speed_match, move_reverse, group_double_tap_center, esc_clears_selection, pause_on_focus_loss.

## QA2:exported-build-qa

**Summary:** QA2 final QA done on exported builds of the final source. One real bug found and mitigated: a Godot 4.7.2 GDScript operator-cache race that crashed about 1 % of exported real-renderer match starts with SIGSEGV in a WorkerThread; HARD1's "caller thread can't call propagate_notification" line was only the crash handler's side effect. After the fix there were 0 crashes in 1,000 boots; before it, 4 in about 390.
All verification items pass. Full suites are 2448/2448 on macOS arm64, Linux arm64 and Linux amd64. The exported macOS .app, the .app inside the DMG, and the Linux amd64 and arm64 builds each pass the 14 export_qa steps (smoke, matches, tutorial plus 8 operations, record/replay) with identical fingerprints. The .deb installs on Debian 12 and 13. All 20 xplat scenarios are identical on 3 platforms.
Measured on the exported macOS app: 1.65 s to menu, 2.6 s to a 96x96 match, 2.8 s to a 192x192 match. RSS is 607 MB at the menu, 710 MB in a match and 730 MB after 30 game-min. Frame time p95 is 10.7 ms (5 min at 1x) and 10.96 ms (30 min at 2x) at 1080p, 4 players, 192x192, with 0 hitches.
The Windows .exe was NOT executed; only static checks (PE, pck hash, CI job) were done. Audio was not heard and no real two-machine LAN game was played.
Manifest and results: builds/RELEASE_MANIFEST.json (artifact sizes and sha256, QA results merged) and docs/RELEASE_CHECKLIST.md section 9.

**Deviations (downstream code relies on these):**
- ENGINE BUG: Godot 4.7.2 stores an untyped operator's evaluator in the GDScript function bytecode on first execution and publishes it unsynchronised. Two threads starting the same fresh function can then call a null or torn pointer. Isolated reproducer: python3 tools/py/gdscript_race_probe.py (cold run crashes 6/6, --warm run 0/6).
- Downstream rule: never start a burst of WorkerThreadPool tasks on a function that has never run; run it once on the calling thread first.
- ViewModelBuilder.request() now builds the first request of each of up to WARM_MAX=24 distinct archetypes synchronously on the main thread (finalised and model_ready emitted at once), and only from the main thread. Cost is about +0.15 to 0.3 s on the loading screen. New static ViewModelBuilder.reset_warm() is for tests. stat_sync_builds is not incremented by warm builds.
- ViewTerrainBake._tasks and ViewTerrainLayers._run_rows run item 0 on the caller first, then add_group_task over items 1..n-1. Output is unchanged.
- tools/py/app_two_process.py: with --binary the child runs on a pty, because the release engine does not flush stdout per line on a pipe and the host's APPLAN_LISTEN was never seen.
- The exported app returns exit code 0 and the editor path returns 3 for engine ERROR lines at exit. quit_stress and boot_stress with --binary scan for ERROR lines themselves.
- CHANGELOG, KNOWN_ISSUES and qa_tooling section 8 mention the bug. CHANGELOG [0.1.0] Known limitations gained one bullet.
- builds/qa/perf_4p_192_1x_stress.json and perf_8p_192_30min.json were deleted because they came from older builds. builds/qa/logs2/ holds this pass's raw logs.

**Tests:** ["tools/gd check --strict: 0 errors, 0 lint violations, 0 warnings.", "tools/gd test -q --timeout 2700 --stall 0 on macOS: 2448/2448 in 719 s.", "tools/gd linux --arch arm64 test: 2448/2448 in 695 s. tools/gd linux --arch amd64 test: 2448/2448 in 1268 s.", "New tests/view/test_view_model_warm.gd: 3 tests pass. tools/gd test view/: 424 passed.", "python3 -m unittest discover -s tools/py/tests: 195 OK with a venv that has pillow and pyyaml (system python: OK with 2 skipped).", "validate_balance --strict, validate_recipes --strict, check_rules_data, validate_missions --strict and validate_audio --strict are all clean.", "xplat_determinism on all 20 tests/scenarios/xplat_*.gd (native, amd64, arm64): all 20 identical.", "app_two_process --linux (amd64 and arm64), the editor macOS run and the exported macOS app (--binary) all pass. app_replay_proof on macOS and --linux passes.", "export_qa --missions all on macOS .app, the DMG, Linux amd64 and Linux arm64 (same pck): 14/14 steps each, --compare IDENTICAL; macOS also editor-equal.", "package_linux --test-deb: .deb installs, runs and removes on debian:12-slim and debian:13-slim. version.py check --packages passes. codesign --verify on the 

**Known gaps:**
- The Windows .exe was never executed; the exe has no embedded icon or Meridian version resource.
- Audio was never heard and no real two-machine LAN game was played.
- The engine race is mitigated, not removed. A rare never-run code path started by two workers in the same instant could still crash. Observed: 1 crash in 600 boots with WARM_MAX=16, 0 in 1000 with 24. Retest on every Godot upgrade and report upstream.
- Three simultaneous anomalies appeared once in the 600-boot validation batch: one SIGSEGV with the same worker signature, plus one kill and two 150 s hangs. The hangs are probably the crash handler stalling after the same crash, but that is not verified. The same configs ran 12 times without a repeat and 1000 further boots were clean.
- Quitting within about 200 ms of a headless match start can print 'resources still in use at exit' lines. Exit code is 0 and there is no hang or crash. Seen in 1 of 60 randomised runs and about 25 % of manual early-quit runs.
- macOS is ad-hoc signed, not notarized. CI was never run on GitHub.
- Frame-time numbers are only valid on an idle machine with the game window uncovered.
- Linux arm64 uses the stock arm64 release template with the same pck, because no arm64 preset is shipped. The .deb is amd64 only.

**Cross-module requests:**
- Orchestrator: record QA2 in docs/DEVIATIONS.md and STATE.md, in particular the Godot 4.7.2 operator-cache race and the rule 'warm a function on one thread before a burst of workers'.
- Orchestrator: STATE open items about the SIGABRT/SIGSEGV at match start are resolved by this finding.
- View owner: if new WorkerThreadPool or Thread code is added, run its function once on the caller first. Consider typed operands in the interpreter hot paths so the VM uses validated operators, which have no cache.
- Release owner: before publishing, run python3 tools/py/release_manifest.py --check. If the exports are rebuilt, rerun package_*.py and export_qa.py and regenerate the manifest.
- Windows tester: run MeridianFracture.exe from the zip once on a real Windows 10/11 machine, as listed in docs/RELEASE_CHECKLIST.md section 8.
